- **Evidence:** grep -n 'waitForTimeout\|setTimeout' tests/e2e/*.ts: hit-targets:32 (1.5 s × 21), motion:34 (1.5 s × 21), phone:142 and :148 (1.0 + 0.5 s × 21, loop at :136), rail-no-scroll:61 (1.5 s, once), requests:43 (1.5 s, once); sum 97.5 s. rail-no-scroll.spec.ts:43-65: 'The rail is its final width on FIRST PAINT', compares width at domcontentloaded against width after the 1.5 s wait. design-invariants.spec.ts:77-89 holds the poll pattern.
- **Sceptic:** The sleep sites and the 97.5 s sum are right. But rail-no-scroll.spec.ts:61 is a measurement window, like requests.spec.ts:52: the test asserts the rail's width does not change after first paint, and the defect was a late snap once /api/prefs answered. A poll that stops when the width is stable would pass on the first reading, before any snap. That wait must stay fixed.
- **Proposal:** Apply design-invariants' pattern. hit-targets polls collectCensus until boxes are present and two readings agree. phone polls scrollWidth until it is stable after the resize. rail-no-scroll polls the aside's width. motion keeps a bounded wait, but as a poll that ends once no finite animation is running (the allowed spinner excepted). Folding hit-targets into design-invariants (tests-14) removes 31.5 s outright.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- tests/unit/read-only-is-testable.test.ts:36 and :81-101 pin the content of tests/helpers/api-test-helpers.ts: it must contain requireActual("@/lib/api/api-auth") and must not hard-wire isReadOnly. The tests-01 factory and the tests-13 deletion must repoint this oracle in the same commit, or it turns red.
- 20 of the 21 `jest.mock("@/lib/api/api-auth", () => ({}))` mocks sit in suites whose imported routes never import @/lib/api/api-auth: b4-emits-agent, the b6 config, credentials and keyless suites, the config-put suites, credentials-api, logs-api-route, memory-unsupported-write, models-api, the profiles suites, sessions-detail-in-flight-note, the stories suites and the template suites. Those mocks are probably deletable outright, about -20 lines, checked one suite at a time for transitive imports. Converting them to a real spread buys no read-only protection.
- The tests-04, tests-05 and tests-06 savings overlap. The leading comment block tests-04 counts (9,849 lines in 669 suites by a scratch count) includes the 338 one-line @jest-environment docblocks and the 169 file-level no-require-imports disables. A plan that adds the three estimates double-counts about 500 lines.
- c7-the-lib-root.test.ts's census test asserts the exact lib-root file list (report.libRoot.files equals the STAYS keys), which a count-only baseline cannot hold. If tests-02 removes the census spawns, that name-level assertion needs a home that does not spawn the census.
- tests/jest.setup.ts warns 'avoid calling restoreAllMocks() in new tests', because it can strip the global @/lib/db mock, yet `grep -rl restoreAllMocks tests/unit | wc -l` gives 15 suites. Not verified whether any of them depends on run order. Worth one sweep.
- Behind tests-06: jest.config.js:76 wraps the whole config in next/jest createJestConfig, whose transform and moduleNameMapper do not carry into a `projects` array. A node/jsdom split therefore needs per-project wrapping, or a default-node switch that keeps the jsdom docblocks. Neither route is costed in the finding.

### Not examined

- No jest, Playwright or coverage run, per the review rules. Suite durations come from the local jest perf-cache (2026-09-10T18:58Z) and coverage from the gitignored coverage/coverage-summary.json (2026-09-07). Both need a fresh run before any number is acted on.
- The e2e total (about 276) is derived from static test() counts and loop sizes; npx playwright test --list was not run.
- tests/integration/test_full_install_update_process.py (1,613 lines) and tests/scripts/run-shell-custom-tests.sh (554 lines): only their CI wiring was checked, not their content or duplication.
- tests/e2e/lib/census-analysis.ts and census-collect.ts (873 lines) and their unit oracles (u0-census-arithmetic.test.ts, 461 lines): only imports were checked.
- tests/helpers story.tsx, fixtures.ts, fetch-map.ts, render-with-query.tsx and page-subtitle.ts: adoption was counted; the code was not read line by line.
- Of the 126 source-text suites, the 93 mixed ones were counted, not classified one by one; 8 of the 33 pure ones were classified from their headers.
- Mock-conflict analysis for subject merges covered route-handler groups only. lib and component groups such as @/lib/db/sql-migrations (25 suites) and @/lib/composer/schema (22) were not analysed.
- Whether the 47 whole-module api-auth mocks currently hide a real read-only defect was not tested; that needs the suites run with PS_READ_ONLY=1.
- The code quality of mock-hermes, mock-hindsight, mock-llm and test-harness/ was not reviewed; only whether and where they run.
- The two e2e flake hypotheses (a reused keep-alive socket; a re-press interrupting a force-dynamic navigation) come from reading the code and were not reproduced under load.

## Dimension: tooling

scripts/ holds 92 tracked files and 13,696 lines. Counting scripts, the mock servers, test-harness, docker, ops and .github, tooling comes to 15,498 tracked lines, and the shrink-only line census measures none of them (line-census.mjs:50-51 walks only src and tests). The gate is 12 commands chained into `npm run lint` plus 8 more run by hand from org/HANDOVER.md:81-88. No command in the repo runs the whole gate by exit code, and the mutation sweep (sweep.py) and the record renderer (taskops) live outside the repo. knip exits 0 and design-lint reports 0 violations, but knip's project leaves out tests/ and test-harness/. There, a 367-line benchmark-gateway harness has been calling /api/benchmarks since commit 4935ac31 (2026-07-25) deleted it, and 9 tooling files have zero references in any tracked file. The biggest removals: a 907-line growth log in design-lint.baseline.json for a gate that reads zero; the bench harness (-368); 339 one-line jest node docblocks that a per-extension environment default would make unnecessary; setup.mjs (169), whose only caller, install.ps1, is now a stub; and 116 eslint-disable lines a tests-only override would free. Config drift: pnpm keys in .npmrc make every npm command print 3 'Unknown project config' warnings, while CI and Docker use npm. Node 20, pinned 10 times in CI and the Dockerfile, reached end-of-life on 2026-04-30. src is type-checked three times per gate, but the 12 TypeScript files in scripts/ are checked by nothing (a scratch check finds only 4 TS5097 errors). About 20 path references inside tooling files point at files that C7 moved or that were deleted, and check-doc-links cannot catch them because it only walks docs/.

### Measurements

- **Tracked files and lines under scripts/**: 92 files, 13,696 lines. Method: `git ls-files scripts | xargs wc -l`
- **Tracked tooling lines outside the line census**: 15,498. Method: `git ls-files scripts mock-hermes mock-hindsight mock-llm test-harness docker ops .github | xargs wc -l; line-census.mjs:50-51 walks only src and tests`
- **package.json scripts**: 48. Method: `node -e 'Object.keys(require("./package.json").scripts).length'`
- **Commands chained in npm run lint**: 12 (10 node/tsx checkers + eslint + typecheck:tests). Method: `read package.json scripts.lint`
- **In-repo command that runs the full gate**: 0. Method: `package.json has none; git ls-files | grep -i -E 'sweep|mutant|taskops|gate\.(sh|mjs|py)' returns only src/tests sweep modules; HANDOVER.md:81-98 lists the 9 manual gate steps and names sweep.py and taskops from PatterTech_EOS`
- **knip exit code on the tree**: 0 (no findings), plus 3 npm 'Unknown project config' warnings. Method: `npx knip --include files,dependencies,unlisted,unresolved,exports,types,duplicates,binaries --no-config-hints`
- **design-lint violations**: 0 in every rule. Method: `node scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs --report`
- **Line census counts**: srcLines 100,881; testLines 121,114; srcRepeatedWindowLines 1,000; testRepeatedWindowLines 4,343; oneImporterComponents 103. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report`
- **Tracked tooling files with zero references in any other tracked file**: 9 (5 ch-*.sh hardware shims, scripts/maintenance/hindsight-rederive.sh, test-harness/baseline-fix-verify.mjs, poll-pair.mjs, poll-run.mjs). Method: `scratch node script: for each tracked file, search every tracked text file for its basename`
- **Benchmark-gateway harness lines targeting deleted routes**: 367 lines in 8 files. Method: `wc -l docker-compose.bench-gateway.yml tests/scripts/bench-gateway-itest.sh test-harness/bench-gateway* test-harness/baseline-fix-verify.mjs test-harness/poll-*.mjs; ls src/app/api has no benchmarks`
- **TypeScript files in scripts/ type-checked by any tsconfig**: 0 of 12; a scratch tsconfig over them reports 4 errors, all TS5097. Method: `tsconfig.json:33 excludes scripts; tsconfig.tests.json:25 includes only tests and src; npx tsc -p <scratch tsconfig extending tsconfig.json over scripts/**/*.ts,*.mts>`
- **Type-check passes over src per gate**: 3 (typecheck:tests, tsc -p tsconfig.json, next build). Method: `package.json lint; ci.yml:97-98; HANDOVER.md:84; next.config.ts has no typescript.ignoreBuildErrors`
- **ci.yml size and repetition**: 442 lines; 17 checkout uses incl. 8 identical 11-line retry blocks; 6 setup-node; 4 npm ci retry blocks; 4 explicit prebuild steps. Method: `grep -c on .github/workflows/ci.yml`
- **Node 20 pins**: 7 workflow node-version entries + 3 Dockerfile FROM lines. Method: `grep -c 'node-version: "20"' .github/workflows/*.yml; grep -c 'FROM node:20' Dockerfile`
- **eslint-disable no-require-imports lines in tests**: 116 in 89 files. Method: `git grep -c 'eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports' -- tests`
- **Jest environment docblocks**: 339 one-line + 37 block-form node docblocks (375 files, all .ts); 158 files declare jsdom; 119 of 501 .ts suites run under jsdom with no DOM usage. Method: `git grep over tests/unit for '@jest-environment node|jsdom'; per-file grep for @testing-library|renderHook|document.|window.`
- **Identical loadEnvLocal copies in scripts/tooling**: 5 (plus 2 more dotenv parsers with different semantics). Method: `git grep -n 'indexOf("=")' -- scripts; side-by-side read of the five functions`
- **Stale repo paths inside tooling files**: about 20 verified absent targets. Method: `scratch regex scan of tooling files for repo paths, each confirmed with ls`
- **Broken relative links in tracked .md outside docs/ and org/**: 3. Method: `scratch scan: TRADEMARK.md:27, branding/guidelines/COLORS.md:16 (x2)`
- **design-lint.baseline.json content**: 907 lines; one key (__growth__); 2 log entries holding 870 + 19 per-file 'grew' lines; no live counts. Method: `node -e 'Object.keys(require("./scripts/tooling/design-lint.baseline.json"))'`
- **Comment lines in scripts js/ts**: 2,091 of 9,135 (23%). Method: `grep -c -E '^\s*(//|\*|/\*)' over git ls-files scripts/*.{mjs,ts,mts,cjs}`
- **Tracked root entries**: 43; generated/local-only root dirs present: .next 66M, coverage 23M, data 13M (untracked part), tmp 6.6M, site 5.0M, tsconfig.tsbuildinfo 360K, all gitignored. Method: `git ls-files | awk -F/ '{print $1}' | sort -u | wc -l; du -sh`

### Findings that survived verification (30 of 33)

#### tooling-04 · Node 20, pinned throughout CI, Docker and engines, is past end-of-life

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** security, medium, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/docs-pages.yml`, `Dockerfile`, `package.json`, `.github/dependabot.yml`, `docs/start-here/install.md`, `scripts/bootstrap/install.sh`
- **Evidence:** `grep -n node-version .github/workflows/*.yml` gives 6 hits in ci.yml (:71,140,188,208,268,407) and 1 in docs-pages.yml:14. `grep -n FROM` gives node:20-bookworm-slim at Dockerfile:2,10,23, docker/TestHarness.dockerfile:3, mock-hermes/Dockerfile:5 and mock-llm/Dockerfile:3, so 13 pins in total. package.json has engines {"node":">=20"} and @types/node ^20.19.43. dependabot.yml:14-16 says 'Stay aligned with CI Node 20'. install.md:21 and install.sh:32,85,141,144-145 (`NODE_MAJOR -lt 20`) carry the minimum. `node --version` on the operator's host prints v24.15.0. bench-gateway.Dockerfile:11,16 builds on the Hermes image's Node 22 and goes with tooling-02.
- **Sceptic:** The finding is real, but the pin count is short by three: docker/TestHarness.dockerfile, mock-hermes/Dockerfile and mock-llm/Dockerfile also build on node:20. The operator's own machine already runs Node 24.15.0, so local development and CI/Docker run different runtimes today. Node 20's end of life on 2026-04-30 matches the published Node release schedule.
- **Proposal:** In one batch, move CI, Docker, engines, @types/node and the install docs to a supported LTS (22 or 24), and drop the dependabot ignore. Rebuild better-sqlite3 natively and run the full gate plus the docker-image and install-harness jobs.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the documented minimum runtime (engines, install.md, install.sh checks) and CI (policy 'sensitive' .github/workflows, Dockerfile).

#### tooling-29 · gitleaks skips the entire tests/ tree

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** security, medium, 3, adjusted
- **Files:** `.gitleaks.toml`, `.github/workflows/gitleaks.yml`
- **Evidence:** .gitleaks.toml:3-7 has `[allowlist]` with description 'Synthetic API key strings in unit/integration tests' and paths = ['''tests/''']. `git ls-files tests | wc -l` gives 740. gitleaks.yml runs on push and pull_request to main and dev with fetch-depth: 0 and gitleaks/gitleaks-action@v3. The number of synthetic-key findings the allowlist currently suppresses is not measured.
- **Sceptic:** The allowlist gap is real, but the replacement cost is unmeasured. Nobody has listed the synthetic key shapes the tests use, and too narrow a regex allowlist would turn the gitleaks workflow red on existing fixture commits. The first step is a local, read-only gitleaks run over tests/ with the allowlist removed, to count findings before choosing between regexes and stopwords.
- **Proposal:** Replace the path allowlist with `regexes` or `stopwords` matching the synthetic key shapes the tests use, and scan tests/ like everything else.

#### tooling-05 · .dockerignore lets the local database, auth token and generated output into the build context

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** security, low, 9, confirmed
- **Files:** `.dockerignore`, `Dockerfile`, `test-harness/bench-gateway.Dockerfile`
- **Evidence:** .dockerignore:1-19 has no data, tmp, site, public/help or org entry. Dockerfile:16 runs `COPY . .` in the builder, and Dockerfile:54 copies only data/seed into the runner. `du -sh` gives data 13M, tmp 6.6M, site 5.0M, public/help 4.1M and org 2.3M, about 31MB in all. `ls data` shows auth-token, control-hub.db with -shm/-wal, 8 control-hub.db.pre-baseline-* files and 3 pre-s1-hotfix files. prebuild-db.mjs:26-28 mkdirs data/ when it is missing. build-site.mjs:50-52 says org/ is deliberately not published.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. .dockerignore leaves out data/, tmp/, site/, public/help/ and org/, and the builder stage runs `COPY . .`. The proposed excludes are safe: the build reads docs/ (the `*.md` exclude is anchored at the root, so nested docs stay), and build-site does not read org/. prebuild-db.mjs creates data/ itself.
- **Proposal:** Add `/data/*`, `!/data/seed/`, tmp, site, public/help, .claude, .swc, org, test-results and playwright-report to .dockerignore. Confirm docs/ stays in the context, because prebuild's build-site --help-only reads it.

#### tooling-02 · Benchmark-gateway harness has targeted deleted routes since the benchmark subsystem was removed

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -368, confirmed
- **Files:** `docker-compose.bench-gateway.yml`, `tests/scripts/bench-gateway-itest.sh`, `test-harness/bench-gateway.Dockerfile`, `test-harness/bench-gateway-entrypoint.sh`, `test-harness/bench-gateway-e2e.mjs`, `test-harness/baseline-fix-verify.mjs`, `test-harness/poll-pair.mjs`, `test-harness/poll-run.mjs`, `package.json`
- **Evidence:** `git show --stat 4935ac31` shows 'feat!: delete the benchmark subsystem, amend ADR-0004' (2026-07-25). `ls src/app/api | grep -i bench` finds nothing. `grep -n benchmarks test-harness/*.mjs` hits bench-gateway-e2e.mjs:58,67,81,95, baseline-fix-verify.mjs:18,22,29,35, poll-pair.mjs:8 and poll-run.mjs:8. bench-gateway.Dockerfile:7 names src/lib/runtime/gateway-manager.ts, which does not exist. A basename reference scan finds nothing outside themselves for baseline-fix-verify.mjs, poll-pair.mjs and poll-run.mjs. `git grep -l bench-gateway-itest` returns only package.json:57. `wc -l` over the 8 files totals 367. docker-compose.real-hermes.yml:21 uses test-harness/hermes-seed.Dockerfile, so that file stays. The vestigial src/lib/db/apply-bench-gateways-migration.ts belongs to the src dimension.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived all of it. Commit 4935ac31 deleted the benchmark subsystem, there is no src/app/api/benchmarks, and the harness still calls /api/benchmarks. Three of the files have no reference from any other tracked file. package.json is the only thing that references the itest script and its npm script.
- **Proposal:** Delete all 8 files and the `test:e2e-bench-gateway` script in one commit. test-harness/hermes-home/ and hermes-seed.Dockerfile stay, because docker-compose.real-hermes.yml:21 uses them.
- **Operator decision:** Removes the CLI command `npm run test:e2e-bench-gateway` and docker-compose.bench-gateway.yml, which matches the policy 'sensitive' pattern docker-compose*.yml.

#### tooling-07 · Jest defaults every suite to jsdom, so 375 suites carry a node docblock and 119 more run under jsdom they do not use

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** performance, medium, -300, adjusted
- **Files:** `jest.config.js`
- **Evidence:** jest.config.js:15 sets `testEnvironment: "jest-environment-jsdom"`, and line 2 uses next/jest. `git ls-files '*.test.ts' '*.test.tsx'` gives 684 (501 .ts, 183 .tsx). `git grep -h '@jest-environment node' -- tests | sort | uniq -c` gives 339 one-line `/** @jest-environment node */` and 37 ` * @jest-environment node`. 158 files declare jsdom, 6 of them .ts. 126 .ts suites have no node docblock. Filtering those with `grep -L -E '@testing-library|renderHook|document\.|window\.|localStorage|jsdom'` leaves 114, so 12 use the DOM. 31 .tsx suites have no jsdom docblock. Net: -339 docblocks, +12 jsdom docblocks on DOM .ts suites, +~15 config lines (projects), about -312; or with a node default, -339 +43, about -296. Tests .ts lines count toward testsLines (121,114 against 116,000).
- **Sceptic:** The finding is real, but two counts are off. With a wider DOM grep, 12 of the 126 un-docblocked .ts suites touch the DOM, not 'at most 7', so 114 suites would change environment rather than 119. 31 .tsx suites also rely on the jsdom default. Running jest `projects` per extension under next/jest needs a createJestConfig call per project. A simpler route is to default to node and add jsdom docblocks. Either way about 114 suites change globals (window absent, fetch present under node), so a per-suite identity proof is mandatory.
- **Proposal:** Pick the environment by extension with jest `projects`: .ts on node, .tsx on jsdom. Then delete the 339 one-line node docblocks and give the few DOM-using .ts suites (at most 7 by the grep) an explicit jsdom docblock. Prove identity per suite (same test names and pass counts, via jest's JSON reporter) before deleting. Time the suite before and after.

#### tooling-08 · 116 eslint-disable lines in tests exist only because no-require-imports has no tests override

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, -265, adjusted
- **Files:** `eslint.config.mjs`, `org/HANDOVER.md`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -c 'eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports' -- tests` sums to 116 in 89 files. `git grep -h -E '^\s*/\* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports' -- tests | wc -l` gives 169, in 169 files. `git grep -h 'no-require-imports' -- tests | wc -l` gives 285. Only 3 eslint-disable lines in tests name another rule. eslint.config.mjs:25-40 has one global rules block and no files override. HANDOVER.md's Open items has 'An eslint policy call for the operator ... A tests-scoped override of that rule would free about a hundred such lines'. Net: -285 +6 config, and +~15 if the reasons are kept, so about -265.
- **Sceptic:** The finding is real but undercounted by more than half. Besides the 116 next-line pragmas there are 169 file-level `/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports */` comments in tests. A tests-scoped override would make all 285 redundant. About 15 of the file-level ones carry a written reason (a computed path, better-sqlite3 not newable, quest modules not yet written), which should survive as plain comments. The operator call named in HANDOVER.md still applies.
- **Proposal:** Add a `{ files: ["tests/**"], rules: { "@typescript-eslint/no-require-imports": "off" } }` block and delete the 116 pragmas by codemod. Prove no other rule was being suppressed on those lines.
- **Operator decision:** Lint policy change, which HANDOVER.md:136 reserves for the operator.

#### tooling-12 · One-off maintenance scripts that have served their purpose or carry machine-specific paths

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -235, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/maintenance/hindsight-rederive.sh`, `scripts/tooling/eos-compile.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-to-runtime.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/render-seed-platform-toolsets.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/vendor-fonts.mjs`
- **Evidence:** A basename reference scan shows hindsight-rederive.sh referenced by no other tracked file. `git grep -l ps-watchdog` returns only that file. eos-compile.mjs:24 is `const EOS = process.env.EOS_ROOT || "C:/Users/Daniel/Documents/Coding/Github/PatterTech_EOS"`. eos-compile.mjs:141-160 says 'The repair is NOT to make the regex match ... would write docs/policy.json and docs/TASKS.md at paths this repo does not use', and :162-175 refuses to run and exits. ADR-0010 names it at lines 44, 48 and 104. tests/unit/b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts:138-147 reads its source. migrate-to-runtime.mjs is invoked by setup.mjs, scripts/lib/ps-migrate.sh and ps-deploy.mjs. render-seed-platform-toolsets.mjs is referenced only by data/seed/README.md. vendor-fonts.mjs is referenced by src/app/layout.tsx.
- **Sceptic:** hindsight-rederive.sh is a confirmed orphan (235 lines). The eos-compile.mjs part misreads the file. Line 154 is not a target: lines 141-160 are a deliberate comment explaining why the matrix parse is left unrepaired (a repair would regenerate 32 governance files and write docs/policy.json), and the script refuses to run on a zero-row matrix. So 'fix its docs/ targets' would be harmful. Only the hard-coded EOS_ROOT default is a fair tidy. Removing the script leaves ADR-0010 (protected) pointing at it and needs two b15 tests edited.
- **Proposal:** Delete hindsight-rederive.sh. For eos-compile.mjs, either remove it (the operator decides) or at minimum require EOS_ROOT instead of defaulting to a personal Windows path, and fix its docs/ targets. Keep migrate-to-runtime.mjs until the operator retires pre-runtime installs. Keep render-seed-platform-toolsets.mjs and vendor-fonts.mjs as documented regenerators.
- **Operator decision:** eos-compile.mjs is named by ADR-0010 in the protected org/decisions/ set, so removing it leaves a dangling reference in a document that cannot change without an ADR. Removing migrate-to-runtime.mjs strands installs that predate the runtime core.

#### tooling-10 · Five hardware scripts ship as both .sh and .mjs, plus forwarding ch-* shims for the .sh copies

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -218, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/hardware/ps-db-backup.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ps-disk-report.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ps-health-check.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ps-log-rotate.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ps-system-report.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-db-backup.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-disk-report.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-health-check.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-log-rotate.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-system-report.sh`, `src/lib/host/hardware-cron.ts`, `docs/running/cross-platform.md`
- **Evidence:** `git ls-files scripts/hardware | xargs wc -l` gives .sh 51+35+37+41+34 = 198, .mjs 80+59+30+62+36 = 267, and five 4-line ch-{db-backup,disk-report,health-check,log-rotate,system-report}.sh shims. `git grep -l 'ch-db-backup.sh'` (and the other four) returns nothing. `git grep -l 'ps-disk-report.sh'` returns only the file and its ch shim. hardware-cron.ts:16-22 offers the .mjs presets when isWindows and otherwise only ps-backup.sh. cross-platform.md:114-116 says 'Schedule the cross-platform Node versions'. host-scheduling.md:14 describes 'an .sh/.mjs pair each'. tests/scripts/boot-smoke.mjs uses ps-system-report.mjs.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived the line counts and references. No test reads the .sh files: b6-database-backup.test.ts:95 mentions one in a comment, and b13 uses 'ps-log-rotate.sh' only as a fixture name, so the test edits are optional. On Linux, the tier-1 platform, hardware-cron.ts offers neither twin as a preset, only ps-backup.sh, and cross-platform.md recommends the Node versions. The operatorDecision on existing crontab lines stands.
- **Proposal:** Keep the .mjs versions. Delete the five .sh copies and their five ch-* shims, and update the two docs and two tests.
- **Operator decision:** Installed users may have crontab lines naming ps-*.sh or ch-*.sh. Setup copies only files that are absent, so existing installs keep their copies, but fresh installs lose those names, and documented files go.

#### tooling-03 · setup.mjs, the Node twin of setup.sh, lost its only caller when install.ps1 became a stub

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -169, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/bootstrap/setup.mjs`, `scripts/bootstrap/env-local.mjs`, `install.ps1`, `tests/unit/b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts`, `tests/scripts/run-shell-custom-tests.sh`, `docs/running/deploy.md`, `docs/running/host-scheduling.md`
- **Evidence:** setup.mjs:5 says 'The Windows path (run by install.ps1)'. install.ps1:1-22 only calls Write-Host. `git grep -n 'setup\.mjs'` outside org/ finds only docs/running/deploy.md:37, docs/running/host-scheduling.md:14, env-local.mjs:2,4, tests/scripts/README.md:27, run-shell-custom-tests.sh:423,425 and tests/unit/b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts:124. org/plans/2026-09-final-release.md:225 lists 'delete setup.mjs or make it the one implementation [remove/M]'. `wc -l` gives setup.mjs 169, setup.sh 327 and env-local.mjs 76. env-local.mjs is also imported by tests/unit/b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts:100-106 and named in the scripts/lib/ps-env.sh:24 comment. setup.mjs:27 defines `npmBin = () => (isWindows ? "npm.cmd" : "npm")`.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. install.ps1 is a 22-line stub that runs nothing, and no script, CI step or src file invokes setup.mjs. The operator's own release plan already carries this removal (org/plans/2026-09-final-release.md:225). The finder under-listed what depends on env-local.mjs: a unit test imports it, so deleting it takes a test block as well as the shell block.
- **Proposal:** Delete setup.mjs. Then either delete env-local.mjs with its shell-test block, or keep it only if another caller appears. Update the two docs and b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts:122-124 to read setup.sh alone.
- **Operator decision:** Removes a documented script under scripts/bootstrap/, a policy 'sensitive' path.

#### tooling-11 · The pre-rename compatibility layer (ch-*, CH_*, CONTROL_HUB_*, control-hub) persists in scripts and root config

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -150, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/application/ch-deploy.sh`, `scripts/hardware/ch-backup.sh`, `scripts/lib/ps-rename-migrate.sh`, `scripts/maintenance/ps-relocate.sh`, `scripts/tooling/ps-deploy.mjs`, `next.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`, `.env.example`, `docker-compose.yml`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -o -h -E '\bCH_[A-Z_]+' -- scripts | wc -l` gives 22 hits in 19 files. The same count gives 24 for CONTROL_HUB and 88 'control-hub' hits in 22 files. `git grep -n 'rename-migrate' -- scripts src tests package.json .github Dockerfile` returns only ps-deploy.mjs:205 '// ── rename migration (port of ps-rename-migrate.sh)'. The .sh is named only in docs/running/data-storage.md:31 and docs/running/deploy.md:39. src/lib/host/paths.ts:76,119 call `readEnv("PS_DATA_DIR", "CH_DATA_DIR", "CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR")`. next.config.ts:9 still reads CH_ALLOWED_DEV_ORIGINS. playwright.config.ts:86-93 sets CH_DATA_DIR beside PS_DATA_DIR and gives a reason. ch-deploy.sh:3 says 'Removed after v1.0', and package.json is at version 0.1.0. ch-backup.sh appears as a crontab fixture in tests/unit/join-crontab-lines.test.ts:57,71. The ch_data volume is at docker-compose.yml:29,46 and docker-compose.real-hermes.yml:66,71. `wc -l` gives 43 + 71 + 5 + 4 = 123 file lines, plus roughly 30 alias lines.
- **Sceptic:** The counts are right, but two claims are wrong. First, ps-rename-migrate.sh is not sourced by ps-deploy.mjs: ps-deploy.mjs:205 is a Node port of it, and nothing invokes the .sh. So deleting it does not cost pre-rename installs their automatic rename; it only removes a manual script named in two docs. Second, the aliases are a live src contract, since src/lib/host/paths.ts reads CH_DATA_DIR and CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR. Removing them from .env.example or playwright.config.ts while src still honours them creates drift, so the change must land together with the src dimension. ch-deploy.sh promises removal 'after v1.0' and the package is at 0.1.0, so deleting it now breaks its own stated contract.
- **Proposal:** Make one operator decision: drop pre-rename compatibility. That means deleting ch-deploy.sh and ch-backup.sh, ps-rename-migrate.sh and its call, and ps-relocate.sh; removing the CH_ and CONTROL_HUB_ fallbacks from scripts, next.config.ts, playwright.config.ts and .env.example; and fixing the '~/control-hub/data' defaults in comments. The src-side aliases (e.g. deploy-status.ts:25 LEGACY_DEPLOY_STATUS_BASENAME) belong to the src dimension. Do this after tooling-10, which removes the other five shims.
- **Operator decision:** Removes the env var aliases CH_ALLOWED_DEV_ORIGINS, CH_DATA_DIR and CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR and the CLI entry points ch-deploy.sh and ch-backup.sh. Pre-rename installs lose automatic DB rename on update. Renaming the ch_data compose volumes would orphan existing user volumes. scripts/application/ and scripts/bootstrap/ are policy 'sensitive' paths.

#### tooling-13 · prebuild still runs the legacy partial migration applier, swallowing errors, before every build

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, high, -150, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/tooling/hermes-registry-import.mjs`, `package.json`, `src/lib/db/apply-sql.ts`
- **Evidence:** package.json has prebuild = `node scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs && node scripts/docs/build-site.mjs --help-only`. ci.yml:77-78, :146-147, :223-224 and :283-284 run it explicitly, and each job's later `npm run build` runs it again through the npm lifecycle. prebuild-db.mjs:18-23 sets DB_DIR = join(ROOT, "data"), :59 `const BASELINE_VERSION = 3;`, :89 calls applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade, :135-139 spawns tsx seed-catalog.ts with PS_DATA_DIR: DB_DIR, and :140 has `shell: process.platform === "win32"`. db-schema-ensure.mjs:139-164 does warn-and-continue. src/lib/db/apply-profiles-tools-upgrade.ts:14 (43 lines) exports the same applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade. migrate-db.ts:5-10 names 'the migration-applier footgun'. apply-sql.ts:39-43 and the composer migration appliers at apply-composer-node-cancelled-migration.ts:59 and apply-composer-rejected-migration.ts:72 compensate for the database prebuild-db.mjs ships. hermes-registry-import.mjs:11 imports columnExists. org/policy.json risk.path_patterns.sensitive lists prebuild-db.mjs, db-schema-ensure.mjs and migrate-db.ts.
- **Sceptic:** Every quoted line checks out, and there is more duplication than reported: db-schema-ensure.mjs's applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade and ensureProfilesToolsParity have a TypeScript twin in src. But the net saving is unmeasured, and nobody has checked whether `next build` needs a database at all. Swapping in migrate-db.ts would add a tsx spawn, importing src/lib/db, to every build. prebuild-db.mjs:140 carries a win32 shell flag the operator's Windows build depends on. The risk stays high and confidence is low.
- **Proposal:** First measure whether `next build` needs any DB (prerendered routes opening getDb()). If it does, replace prebuild-db.mjs's applier with the migrate-db.ts path, so there is one applier. Delete applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade and ensureProfilesToolsParity if nothing else needs them; keep columnExists/tableExists, which hermes-registry-import.mjs imports. Keep the build-site --help-only half of prebuild, and keep hermes-registry-import.mjs, which src/lib/db/upgrade.ts:255 spawns at runtime.
- **Operator decision:** Changes build behaviour. prebuild-db.mjs, db-schema-ensure.mjs and migrate-db.ts are listed as policy 'sensitive' paths in org/policy.json (R2 tier floor).

#### tooling-09 · Five identical loadEnvLocal functions across scripts/tooling TypeScript entry points

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -72, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/ensure-hermes-model-sync.ts`, `scripts/tooling/import-hermes-state.ts`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/tooling/seed-catalog.ts`, `scripts/tooling/retention-prune.ts`, `scripts/tooling/_env-local.mjs`, `scripts/bootstrap/env-local.mjs`
- **Evidence:** `diff <(sed -n 15,34p scripts/tooling/X.ts) <(sed -n 26,45p scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts)` shows no differences for ensure-hermes-model-sync, import-hermes-state and seed-catalog. retention-prune.ts:46-64 is the same logic with readFileSync inlined. Each is called once, at ensure-hermes-model-sync.ts:37, import-hermes-state.ts:44, migrate-db.ts:63, retention-prune.ts:74 and seed-catalog.ts:37. _env-local.mjs:27 exports a different loadEnvLocal(dir) used by ps-deploy.mjs:22,474. `git grep -l loadEnvLocal -- tests` returns nothing. Net = -(20*4+19) + 22 + 5, about -72.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it by diff. Four copies are byte-identical and retention-prune's is a 19-line variant. Five consumers pay for a helper. Recomputed net: 99 lines removed minus about 22 for the helper (it needs a root parameter plus fs/path imports) and 5 import lines.
- **Proposal:** Create scripts/tooling/_load-env-local.ts with that exact function and import it in the five scripts. Leave _env-local.mjs and env-local.mjs alone, because their semantics differ and merging them would change behaviour.

#### tooling-17 · pnpm configuration beside an npm lockfile makes every npm command warn

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, -25, confirmed
- **Files:** `.npmrc`, `package.json`, `.gitignore`
- **Evidence:** `npm config get registry` prints `npm warn Unknown project config` for "packageManager", "approve-builds" and "shamefully-hoist". .npmrc:1-2 and 12 set those keys, and lines 3-11 carry the jest 30.3.0 hoisting comment. package.json has `pnpm.onlyBuiltDependencies` [better-sqlite3, esbuild, sharp, unrs-resolver]. `ls node_modules/.pnpm` fails, and node_modules/.package-lock.json exists. package-lock.json is 471,717 bytes. .gitignore:48-52 (pnpm-lock.yaml, pnpm-workspace.yaml, a.out, .npmrc, !/.npmrc) repeats at :69-73. Dockerfile:8 and ci.yml:76 run npm ci.
- **Sceptic:** I reproduced the three warnings with `npm config get registry`. The repo uses npm throughout: there is a package-lock, node_modules has .package-lock.json and no .pnpm directory, and CI and Docker run `npm ci`. The .gitignore duplication is exact.
- **Proposal:** Standardise on npm, which CI and Docker already use: delete .npmrc, the package.json pnpm block and the pnpm and duplicate lines in .gitignore.
- **Operator decision:** Changes package-manager configuration for anyone installing with pnpm.

#### tooling-24 · install.ps1 and Windows-only branches remain after the Linux-first decision

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -20, adjusted
- **Files:** `install.ps1`, `knip.json`, `scripts/tooling/_platform.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/ps-deploy.mjs`, `scripts/hardware/ps-system-report.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs`, `src/lib/host/hardware-cron.ts`, `docs/running/cross-platform.md`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -c -E 'isWindows|win32' -- scripts` gives setup.mjs 3, ps-system-report.mjs 1 (:22), _platform.mjs 3, prebuild-db.mjs 1 (:140 `shell: process.platform === "win32"`) and ps-deploy.mjs 4 (:21,181,192,361). The same grep over src gives 12 hits in 5 files. cross-platform.md:31 says 'No native-Windows support', and :96-98 keeps platform.ts fallbacks so `npm run dev` runs on native Windows. install.ps1:4,22 link docs/CROSS_PLATFORM.md, which is missing. This session's platform is win32.
- **Sceptic:** The counts are right, but the scripts-side removal is partly harmful. prebuild-db.mjs:140 `shell: process.platform === "win32"` runs on every `npm run build`, and the operator develops on a native Windows host, which cross-platform.md:96-98 keeps working for the dev loop. Removing that branch breaks the operator's own build. setup.mjs's branches go with tooling-03. That leaves ps-deploy.mjs (4 hits), _platform.mjs (3) and ps-system-report.mjs (1), about 20 lines. The install.ps1 link fix is the certain part.
- **Proposal:** At minimum, fix install.ps1's link. The operator decides whether install.ps1 stays as a signpost for Windows users. Scripts-side Windows branches (the deploy runner, setup.mjs if it survives tooling-03, ps-system-report.mjs tasklist, prebuild shell flag) can go if native-Windows production is truly dropped; keep src/lib/host/platform.ts fallbacks for the dev loop.
- **Operator decision:** install.ps1 is a policy 'sensitive' path and a documented entry point. Removing Windows branches from the deploy runner changes behaviour for anyone running it on native Windows.

#### tooling-20 · CI workflow repeats setup blocks and runs prebuild twice back-to-back

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -17, adjusted
- **Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- **Evidence:** `wc -l ci.yml` gives 442. `grep -c 'uses: actions/checkout@v5'` gives 17. `grep -c 'npm ci || (npm cache clean --force && npm ci)'` gives 4. `grep -c 'uses: actions/setup-node@v5'` gives 6 (boot-smoke's has no cache). `grep -c 'Cache Playwright browsers'` gives 2 (:214-222 and :274-282). Explicit `npm run prebuild` steps sit at :78, :147, :224 and :284, followed by `npm run build` at :102, :162, :226 and :286. The retries are a ruled quarantine (ci.yml:3-19, WG-DEL-004, 'Review by 2027-01'). Net = -28 + 15 - 4, about -17.
- **Sceptic:** The counts are right, but the net is smaller and one claim is too narrow. prebuild runs twice in all four build jobs, because `npm run build` triggers the prebuild lifecycle; only in the e2e jobs is it back to back. The Playwright cache-and-install block has exactly two consumers, so it does not pay for a composite input. Recomputed net: the four 8-line setup-node plus npm-ci blocks become four `uses` lines (-28), a composite action costs about +15, and dropping two e2e prebuild steps saves -4.
- **Proposal:** Move setup-node, the npm ci retry and an optional Playwright install into a local composite action, as .github/actions/forbid-hermes-paths-in-api already is. Checkout and its retry cannot move, because a local action needs the checkout first, and the retries are a ruled quarantine (ci.yml:3-19). Drop the 2 explicit prebuild steps in the e2e jobs.
- **Operator decision:** CI configuration is a policy 'sensitive' path (R2).

#### tooling-15 · src is type-checked three times per gate, and tsconfig.json reads stale .next type stubs

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** performance, medium, -8, adjusted
- **Files:** `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.tests.json`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/pull_request_template.md`, `docs/CONTRIBUTING.md`, `org/HANDOVER.md`
- **Evidence:** typecheck:tests is `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.tests.json` and is the last step of lint. ci.yml:97-98 and :151-152 run `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json`, followed by `npm run build` at :102 and :162 in the same jobs. next.config.ts has no typescript.ignoreBuildErrors. tsconfig.json:25-33 includes `**/*.ts`, `.next/types/**/*.ts` and `.next/dev/types/**/*.ts`, and excludes 'trace_import.ts', which `ls` cannot find. tsconfig.tests.json:2 names docs/REPO_GUIDE.md (missing) and :5 says '~2,300 tests'. `git log -S 'rm -rf .next/dev' -- org/HANDOVER.md` returns 9336c4ee (2026-09-10). `git grep -F '.next/dev' -- org docs .github scripts` returns only HANDOVER.md:83.
- **Sceptic:** The triple check is real, but the causal claim is unsupported. Nothing records why HANDOVER.md removes .next/dev: the step arrived in 9336c4ee (T-0142) with no stated reason. In CI, both jobs that run `tsc -p tsconfig.json` also run `npm run build` in the same job, which type-checks again, so dropping the tsc step loses only fail-fast ordering, not coverage. tsconfig.json's `**/*.ts` include also covers next.config.ts and playwright.config.ts, which would need adding to tsconfig.tests.json.
- **Proposal:** Add next.config.ts and playwright.config.ts to tsconfig.tests.json include, drop the separate `tsc -p tsconfig.json` step from both CI jobs, the PR template, CONTRIBUTING and the gate, and leave .next route types to next build. Remove the dead exclude and fix the stale header comment.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the documented contributor checklist and CI steps (policy 'sensitive' .github/workflows).

#### tooling-31 · build-test-macos repeats the full Ubuntu gate for a platform ruled dev-tier

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, -7, adjusted
- **Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `docs/running/cross-platform.md`
- **Evidence:** ci.yml:124-162 (build-test-macos) runs prebuild :146-147, ESLint :148-149, the forbid action :150, tsc :151-152, test:coverage :153-154 and build :155-162. cross-platform.md:29 describes macOS as 'Best-effort developer environment (Unix); built + unit-tested in CI.' boot-smoke (ci.yml:167-190) runs on macos-latest. Removable without changing that tier: 2 + 1 + 2 + 2 (prebuild) = 7 lines.
- **Sceptic:** The duplication is real, but the net is overstated and one option contradicts a documented contract. cross-platform.md's tier table says macOS is 'built + unit-tested in CI', so dropping test:coverage changes a documented support tier. Dropping only the platform-independent steps (ESLint, the forbid action, tsc) and the redundant explicit prebuild saves about 7 lines, not 20.
- **Proposal:** The operator decides: keep it as is, or reduce the macOS job to install plus build plus boot-smoke, dropping lint and coverage, which are platform-independent.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the CI matrix for a supported platform.

#### tooling-18 · About 20 references in tooling files point at paths C7 moved or that were deleted

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** doc-drift, low, -4, adjusted
- **Files:** `install.ps1`, `tsconfig.tests.json`, `scripts/lib/ps-migrate.sh`, `scripts/lib/ps-env.sh`, `scripts/bootstrap/setup-hindsight.sh`, `scripts/hardware/reconnect-hindsight.sh`, `scripts/tooling/ps-deploy.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/check-icon-button-names.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/eos-compile.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/maintenance/ps-migrate.sh`
- **Evidence:** All confirmed with ls/grep: install.ps1:4,22 (docs/CROSS_PLATFORM.md); tsconfig.tests.json:2 (docs/REPO_GUIDE.md) and :5 ('~2,300 tests'); scripts/lib/ps-migrate.sh:6 (present tense 'Sourced by ... scripts/lib/ps-deploy-impl.sh'). src/lib/paths.ts is named at ps-migrate.sh:14, ps-env.sh:88, setup-hindsight.sh:298 and reconnect-hindsight.sh:91; it now lives at src/lib/host/paths.ts. reconnect-hindsight.sh:127 prints `npm run restart`, but package.json has no restart script. check-icon-button-names.mjs:260 names src/app/orchestration/chat/page.tsx, which is missing (src/app/work/chat/page.tsx exists). action.yml:11 says '@/lib/hermes-agent-runtime', but src/lib/hermes-agent-runtime.ts is missing and src/modules/hermes/lib/agent-runtime.ts exists. CODEOWNERS:6 names /tools/, which is missing and redundant with line 1 `* @Daniel-Parke`. eslint.config.mjs:31-32 names CardDetailModal, which appears in no other tracked file. migrate-db.ts:15 says 'falls back to ~/control-hub/data'. maintenance/ps-migrate.sh:14 gives '~/control-hub/data' as the default, but :40 falls back to it only when ~/patterstage/data is absent. Dockerfile:52-53 says 'can't seed the benchmark catalog'. bench-gateway.Dockerfile:7 goes with tooling-02.
- **Sceptic:** About 16 of the roughly 20 references are real. Three are not drift. design-lint.mjs:328 is a past-tense history note ('used to be'). eos-compile.mjs:154 is a deliberate warning (see tooling-12). ps-deploy.mjs:8 ('Ports scripts/lib/ps-deploy-impl.sh') records provenance, and deploy.md:39 notes the file is gone. The finder missed Dockerfile:52-53, which still says the seed exists to 'seed the benchmark catalog'. The design-lint growth log names 608 dead src/components paths, but it is an append-only ledger, so it is out.
- **Proposal:** Fix each reference in one doc-drift batch: about 20 one-line edits. Delete CODEOWNERS lines 3-6, which are also redundant with line 1 `* @Daniel-Parke`. Add shell and .mjs comment spellings to the path-move checklist the next move uses.

#### tooling-23 · npm script names overlap and some have no users

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** naming, medium, -3, adjusted
- **Files:** `package.json`, `docs/contributing/testing.md`, `docs/contributing/output-canary.md`, `tests/unit/b15-docs-pipeline-is-wired.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** A reference count outside package.json, package-lock.json and org/tasks (`git grep -l -F`) gives: dev:network 0, test:watch 0, test:restart-recovery 0 (tests/integration/runtime/restart-recovery.mjs exists), canary:snapshot and canary:assert 1 each (docs/contributing/output-canary.md), lint:design 1, discover-hermes 1 (docs/running/deploy.md). ci.yml:110 runs `npm run canary -- --out`. Pins: b15-docs-pipeline-is-wired.test.ts:62-92, u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts:107-112, c0-the-line-census.test.ts:66, b2-copy.test.ts:68 and icon-buttons-are-named.test.ts:129.
- **Sceptic:** The finding is real but incomplete. test:restart-recovery also has no reference outside package.json and was not listed. The renames save about 3 lines while editing 5 pinned suites and at least 4 docs, so the value is naming only and it should be a low priority. dev:network and test:watch are conventional developer conveniences, and zero references does not make them dead.
- **Proposal:** Adopt one naming scheme: test:e2e for Playwright only; smoke:runtime, smoke:composer and itest:hermes for the others; census:design beside census:lines. Remove test:e2e-bench-gateway (tooling-02) and fold canary:snapshot into canary. Update the pinned tests and docs in the same commit.
- **Operator decision:** Renames CLI commands named in docs (testing.md:113-114, runtime-architecture.md:182, output-canary.md).

#### tooling-16 · knip is green partly because its config hides test-harness orphans, a dead devDependency and unlisted dependencies

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, -2, confirmed
- **Files:** `knip.json`, `package.json`, `tests/unit/dashboard-helpers-unit.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b6-cleared-defaults-stay-cleared.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** knip.json:2 is `"$schema": "https://unpkg.com/knip@5/schema.json"`, while package.json devDependencies has knip ^6.16.1. knip.json:11-17 project covers only src, scripts and mock-*. `git grep -n ts-jest` (excluding the lockfile) returns knip.json:19 and package.json:91. jest.config.js:2 uses next/jest. tests/unit/dashboard-helpers-unit.test.ts:5 imports from "@jest/globals", and tests/unit/b6-cleared-defaults-stay-cleared.test.ts:617 does require("jsdom"). Neither is in dependencies or devDependencies; jsdom arrives through jest-environment-jsdom ^30.3.0. The lint:knip script is `knip --include files,dependencies,unlisted,unresolved,...`. knip.json:31-36 ignores tasklist, taskkill, netstat and where.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. knip's project leaves out tests/ and test-harness/, where three orphans live. The $schema pins knip@5 while knip 6 is installed. ts-jest's only references are knip.json and package.json. The ignore list hides two unlisted dependencies, even though lint:knip explicitly includes the 'unlisted' check.
- **Proposal:** Add tests/**, test-harness/**, jest.config.js and playwright.config.ts to knip's entry and project; remove the ts-jest devDependency and its ignore; either declare @jest/globals and jsdom as devDependencies or replace the two uses; bump $schema to knip@6. Expect the widened scope to surface a batch of new findings, and take them as their own batch.
- **Operator decision:** Removes the ts-jest devDependency (no users found).

#### tooling-28 · eslint config justifies globally disabled rules with a component that no longer exists, and lints generated output

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, -1, confirmed
- **Files:** `eslint.config.mjs`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -l CardDetailModal` returns only eslint.config.mjs (comment at :31-32). `node -e` over package.json dependencies and devDependencies filtered for compiler or babel returns []. `grep reactCompiler next.config.ts` finds nothing. node_modules/eslint-plugin-react-hooks/package.json has version 7.0.1. eslint.config.mjs:9-24 globalIgnores has no site/** or public/help/**. `npx eslint site/search-index.js` produces no output (no ignore warning). `du -sh` gives site 5.0M and public/help 4.1M.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. CardDetailModal appears nowhere else in the repo. No React Compiler package or next.config setting exists. The installed hooks plugin is 7.0.1. Linting a file under site/ prints no 'File ignored' warning, so `eslint .` walks the generated site locally.
- **Proposal:** Add site/** and public/help/** to globalIgnores. Measure the violation count of both disabled rules on src (for example `npx eslint src --rule '{"react-hooks/set-state-in-effect":"error"}'`), then either re-enable them with per-line pragmas where justified or rewrite the justification to what is true.
- **Operator decision:** Re-enabling or keeping the two rules off globally is a lint-policy call.

#### tooling-21 · Coverage floors are enforced only in CI, not in the gate as practised

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, 0, confirmed
- **Files:** `jest.config.js`, `scripts/tooling/coverage-floor-check.mjs`, `org/HANDOVER.md`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- **Evidence:** jest.config.js:11 requires coverage-floors.cjs, and :50 sets `coverageThreshold`. package.json test is `jest` and test:coverage is `jest --coverage`. HANDOVER.md:84 runs `npx jest`. ci.yml:100 and :154 run `npm run test:coverage`. coverage-floor-check.mjs:1-30 says it 'holds a DECLARED number (the coverage floors in coverage-floors.cjs) against a committed baseline'. CONTRIBUTING.md:26-27 lists both `npm test` and `npm run test:coverage`.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. coverageThreshold only applies when coverage is collected. The handover gate runs plain `npx jest` while CI runs test:coverage, and coverage-floor-check compares declared numbers without measuring coverage.
- **Proposal:** Make the gate's single jest step `jest --coverage` (and fold this into tooling-01's step list); drop the duplicate `npm test` from CONTRIBUTING.md.

#### tooling-30 · Nothing enforces protection of main: the pre-push hook is opt-in and branch protection requires zero checks

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/git-hooks/pre-push`, `docs/CONTRIBUTING.md`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `docs/contributing/testing.md`
- **Evidence:** `git config --get core.hooksPath` exits 1 (unset). CONTRIBUTING.md:84 says 'Optional pre-push hook ... Install with git config core.hooksPath scripts/git-hooks'. scripts/git-hooks/pre-push:1-23 blocks non-merge pushes to main. testing.md:180 says 'branch protection on main currently requires zero checks, so acceptance-gate reports and blocks nothing'. ci.yml:422-425 carries the same comment. Dockerfile:7-8 runs `COPY package.json package-lock.json* ./` then `RUN npm ci`, with no .git in the stage.
- **Sceptic:** The evidence is right, but the optional `prepare` script is harmful as written. npm runs `prepare` on every `npm ci`, including the Dockerfile deps stage, which copies only package.json and the lockfile with no .git, and the install-harness containers. An unguarded `git config` fails there and aborts the install. It would also silently change every contributor's persistent git configuration. What remains is branch protection, which is an operator setting outside the repo, so the net is 0 lines.
- **Proposal:** The operator makes acceptance-gate and build-test-ubuntu required checks on main. Optionally add a package.json `prepare` script that sets core.hooksPath (+1 line).
- **Operator decision:** Repository settings and persistent git configuration are the operator's to change.

#### tooling-32 · Unit suites pin tooling text, so every tooling refactor edits tests

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** test-debt, low, 0, confirmed
- **Files:** `tests/unit/b15-docs-pipeline-is-wired.test.ts`, `tests/unit/u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts`, `tests/unit/icon-buttons-are-named.test.ts`, `tests/unit/c0-the-line-census.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b2-copy.test.ts`, `tests/unit/read-only-is-testable.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -l 'package.json' -- tests/unit | xargs grep -l -E 'readFileSync|require\(.*package\.json|import .*package\.json' | wc -l` gives 8. `git grep -l 'scripts/' -- tests/unit | wc -l` gives 59. `git grep -l tsconfig -- tests/unit | wc -l` gives 10. b15-docs-pipeline-is-wired.test.ts:62-92 pins docs:* scripts, tsx, lint containing docs/check.mts, prebuild and markdown-it. u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts:107-112 splits scripts.lint on '&&' against testing.md. b1-deploy-api-flag.test.ts:121-125 reads the setup.sh and setup.mjs source.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived the counts. Eight unit suites read package.json, 59 mention scripts/ and 10 mention tsconfig. The lint-chain count is pinned against testing.md but not against CONTRIBUTING.md, which is why 'nine gates' drifted.
- **Proposal:** Budget these edits into tooling-01, 03, 15 and 23. When the gate runner lands, replace the scattered scripts.lint pins with one assertion that the runner's step list contains each gate script, rather than adding more string pins.

#### tooling-33 · Two-consumer duplicates in scripts do not pay for a shared interface: leave them

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, 0, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/_platform.mjs`, `src/lib/host/platform.ts`, `scripts/bootstrap/hindsight-server.py`, `scripts/bootstrap/hindsight-docker/server.py`
- **Evidence:** `wc -l` gives scripts/tooling/_platform.mjs 122, scripts/bootstrap/hindsight-server.py 76 and scripts/bootstrap/hindsight-docker/server.py 48. cross-platform.md:41-45 says 'The runner (ps-deploy.mjs) executes in bare node, outside the Next build, so it cannot import the first seam and carries its own copy of the same primitives (isWindows, detachedSpawn, isPidAlive, killByPort, killPid, portInUse). Change one and change the other.' ps-deploy.mjs:21 imports those names from ./_platform.mjs.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. Both pairs have exactly two consumers, and the platform twin is documented as deliberate. One precision: the documented reason is that the deploy runner executes in bare node outside the Next build, not specifically Node 20. Even on Node 24, platform.ts uses the @/ alias and so is not trivially importable from bare node.
- **Proposal:** No change. Record them as known so the next programme does not spend a batch on them. Revisit _platform.mjs only if the runner moves to a Node version that runs TypeScript natively.

#### tooling-25 · Of three compose files, one is exercised in CI, one only in docs, one is dead

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, low, 2, confirmed
- **Files:** `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.real-hermes.yml`, `docker-compose.bench-gateway.yml`, `mock-hermes/Dockerfile`, `mock-llm/Dockerfile`, `mock-hindsight/server.mjs`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -n 'docker compose' -- .github tests/scripts` finds only real-hermes-itest.sh:9 (docker-compose.real-hermes.yml) and bench-gateway-itest.sh:8. ci.yml:409 runs `npm run test:e2e-hermes`. ci.yml:377 runs `docker build -f Dockerfile -t patterstage:ci .` and nothing uses compose. docker-compose.yml:36-38 builds mock-hermes/Dockerfile. `git ls-files mock-hindsight` returns only server.mjs.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it. Only docker-compose.real-hermes.yml is exercised, through test:e2e-hermes in CI. No test script or CI job uses docker-compose.yml, and the docker-image job builds only the Dockerfile.
- **Proposal:** Keep docker-compose.yml as the documented try-it path, but add a cheap CI validation (`docker compose -f docker-compose.yml config -q` in the docker-image job) so it cannot rot unseen. Delete the bench file (tooling-02). Keep all three mocks.
- **Operator decision:** CI change (policy 'sensitive' .github/workflows).

#### tooling-14 · TypeScript in scripts/ is type-checked by no tsconfig

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** type-safety, low, 3, confirmed
- **Files:** `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.tests.json`, `scripts/docs/check.mts`, `scripts/docs/extract.ts`
- **Evidence:** tsconfig.json:33 excludes 'scripts', and tsconfig.tests.json:25 includes only tests/** and src/**. `git ls-files 'scripts/**/*.ts' 'scripts/**/*.mts'` lists 12 files (1,601 lines), of which 4 are .d.mts. A scratch tsconfig run via `npx tsc -p` reports check.mts(29,31), (30,45) and (31,37) and extract.ts(231,5), all TS5097. Tests import design-lint.mjs 17 times, docs/lib.mjs 16, derive-surface-ladder.mjs 2 and output-canary.mjs 1 (`git grep` in tests/unit), so their .d.mts files are already checked. Adding the two include globs and allowImportingTsExtensions (valid with noEmit:true) is about +3 lines.
- **Sceptic:** I reproduced it with a scratch tsconfig in the scratchpad that extends the repo tsconfig over scripts/**/*.ts and *.mts. It reports exactly 4 TS5097 errors and nothing else. Four of the 12 files are .d.mts declarations that tests already pull in through their .mjs imports, so 8 files, all real TypeScript, gain checking.
- **Proposal:** Add 'scripts/**/*.ts' and 'scripts/**/*.mts' to tsconfig.tests.json include, with `allowImportingTsExtensions: true` (valid because noEmit is set). Consider checkJs for the .mjs gates as a later, separately measured step.

#### tooling-19 · check-doc-links walks docs/ only, so root and folder markdown drifts unchecked

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 6, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs`, `TRADEMARK.md`, `branding/guidelines/COLORS.md`, `REBRANDING.md`
- **Evidence:** check-doc-links.mjs:20 sets `const DOCS = join(ROOT, "docs")`, and :39 runs `for (const file of walk(DOCS))`. The scratch scan (git ls-files *.md minus docs/, org/ and data/seed/, 16 files) reports: TRADEMARK.md:27 -> src/lib/theme.ts; branding/guidelines/COLORS.md:16 -> ../../docs/design-tokens.md; COLORS.md:16 -> ../../src/lib/theme.ts. None of the three targets exists, and docs/contributing/design-tokens.md does. `git ls-files branding/assets` returns only README.md.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived it with an independent scratch link scan over all 16 tracked .md files outside docs/, org/ and data/seed/. It finds exactly the same 3 broken links. The checker walks docs/ only.
- **Proposal:** Have the checker walk `git ls-files '*.md'` minus org/ and data/seed/ (agent content), then fix the 3 links (theme tokens now live in src/app/globals.css and docs/contributing/design-tokens.md). Keep REBRANDING.md and TRADEMARK.md at the root.

#### tooling-22 · The shrink-only census does not see tooling, and scripts carry 23% comment lines

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, medium, 10, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/output-canary.mjs`, `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`, `tests/unit/c8-the-programme-is-closed.test.ts`, `tests/unit/c0-the-line-census.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** line-census.mjs:50-51 walks only join(ROOT,"src") and join(ROOT,"tests"), and :46 already accepts .mjs. A per-file loop of `grep -c -E '^\s*(//|/\*|\*)'` over 44 non-.d.mts js/ts/mjs files in scripts gives 1,988 comment lines of 8,743. It gives design-lint.mjs 268/884, output-canary.mjs 216/748, docs/lib.mjs 194/786 and check-form-control-names.mjs 88/318. c8-the-programme-is-closed.test.ts:81-83 has `expect(Object.keys(now).sort()).toEqual(keys.slice().sort())` against AT_C0 (:22). c0-the-line-census.test.ts:27 defines MEASURES. Tracked tooling outside the census totals 15,498 lines (`git ls-files scripts mock-hermes mock-hindsight mock-llm test-harness docker ops .github | xargs cat | wc -l`).
- **Sceptic:** The gap is real, and the per-file comment counts match exactly, but the aggregate differs by regex. Adding a measure to the census means editing the C8 closure oracle, whose test asserts the census key set equals the programme's AT_C0 keys. That mutates a closed programme's record-bearing test. A separate tooling census, or its own oracle, keeps the C0-C8 closure intact. Many scripts comments are warrant or defect histories (eos-compile.mjs:141-160, coverage-floor-check.mjs:13-20), so a C5-style cut will keep more than the 23% suggests.
- **Proposal:** Add a scriptsLines measure, and optionally scriptsRepeatedWindowLines, to the census, so a tooling batch is refereed like src was. Update the c0 MEASURES list and c8's key-equality test in the same commit. Run a C5-style pass over scripts/tooling, keeping comments that name a defect.

#### tooling-01 · No in-repo command runs the whole gate by exit code; the practiced gate lives in a handover note and scratch scripts

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, medium, 200, confirmed
- **Files:** `package.json`, `org/HANDOVER.md`, `docs/CONTRIBUTING.md`, `.github/pull_request_template.md`, `docs/contributing/testing.md`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `playwright.config.ts`, `tests/e2e/design-census.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** `node -e "console.log(Object.keys(require('./package.json').scripts).length)"` prints 48. HANDOVER.md:81-88 lists the gate steps and :90-98 names `sweep.py mutants.json` and `taskops.render_views(...)` from PatterTech_EOS. `git ls-files | grep -i -E 'sweep|mutant|taskops|gate\.(sh|mjs|py)'` matches only src/lib/sessions/session-orphan-sweep.ts and 3 unit tests, so there is no gate runner in the repo. CONTRIBUTING.md:22-28 gives 7 commands, with jest twice (npm test and test:coverage), and :31 says 'nine gates'. pull_request_template.md:18-23 gives 6. testing.md:164 says 'chains twelve gates', which u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts:107-112 enforces, and nothing checks CONTRIBUTING.md. ci.yml runs docs:build at :87, the forbid-hermes-paths action at :96, test:coverage at :100 and :154, boot-smoke at :190, and bash tests/scripts/run-shell-custom-tests.sh (554 lines) at :53. design-census.spec.ts:74 has `test.skip(!RUN, ...)`. playwright.config.ts:81 has `reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI`. check-derived-views.mjs:118-122 prints the out-of-repo taskops command.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived every citation. package.json has 48 scripts and none of them runs the full gate. The gate practised in HANDOVER.md is 9 manual steps plus an out-of-repo sweep and renderer, and the written lists disagree with each other. The +200 net lines is a new runner, so it is an estimate and nothing is removed. One caveat: the proposed test comparing the JSON list to ci.yml adds another suite that pins file text, which is the pattern tooling-32 criticises. Prefer a runner the CI jobs call over a text comparison.
- **Proposal:** Add scripts/tooling/gate.mjs plus `npm run gate`, reading an ordered step list from one JSON file. The runner should: refuse or free the known ports; run each step and record its exit code and duration; stamp the tree (git status --porcelain and git write-tree) before and after, failing if it changed; and print a one-screen table. Add a unit test that checks the JSON list against the named steps in ci.yml, so the two cannot drift, and have CONTRIBUTING.md and the PR template point at `npm run gate` instead of their own lists. Separately, and only if the operator wants the discipline in-repo, port the mutation sweep to scripts/tooling/mutation-sweep.mjs. The task-record renderer stays in the EOS repo by design (check-derived-views.mjs:122).
- **Operator decision:** Changes the documented contributor checklist (CONTRIBUTING.md, PR template) and ties CI step lists to a repo file; .github/workflows/ is a policy 'sensitive' path (R2). Also decide whether census:lines and the design census join CI, and whether the mutation sweep is vendored into the repo.

### Refuted, and why

- **tooling-06 · design-lint.baseline.json is a 907-line growth log for a gate that reads zero.** The proposal edits an append-only ledger, which governance forbids. T-0025, the record that created the mechanism, calls it 'an append-only __growth__ log' and says the reason is written into the baseline file beside the number it excuses. design-lint.mjs describes it as the log 'of every growth ever allowed through'. Compacting past entries rewrites that record. The saving also moves no measure, because line-census counts only .ts/.tsx/.css/.mjs files. The only legitimate variant is a mechanism change so that future entries stop writing per-file 'grew' arrays, which removes nothing now.
- **tooling-26 · Test and harness tooling is spread across six root-level locations.** The move saves zero lines and moves no measure, and it carries a governance cost. docker/ is a sensitive path pattern in org/policy.json. Moving docker/TestHarness.dockerfile under test-harness/ silently drops it below the R2 tier floor unless the protected policy.json is amended by ADR. It also rewrites paths across knip.json, package.json, two compose files, the Python install harness and docs, just to go from 43 root entries to 39. The runbook figure is also inflated: 6 tracked files reference ops/runbooks/deploy.md, not 13 docs.
- **tooling-27 · data/ mixes tracked seed content with the runtime database that prebuild writes.** The finding is pointless for its cost. It saves about 2 .gitignore lines but moves a runtime-read path through src (catalog-seed.ts), user-facing copy (restore page), the output canary's seed walk (likely needing a re-bless), docs extraction, the seed-pack generator, the sensitive Dockerfile and docs, and the five-spellings rule applies to every one. The operator's local backup clutter is not a repo change.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- Three more Node 20 base images the Node-EOL finding (tooling-04) did not count: docker/TestHarness.dockerfile:3, mock-hermes/Dockerfile:5 and mock-llm/Dockerfile:3 all use `FROM node:20-bookworm-slim` (`grep -n FROM`). The operator's host already runs v24.15.0 (`node --version`), so local development and CI/Docker run different runtimes today.
- scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs:29,139 exports ensureProfilesToolsParity and applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade, and src/lib/db/apply-profiles-tools-upgrade.ts:14 (43 lines) exports the same applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade. A JS twin of a TS migration step inside the policy-sensitive prebuild path. It is two consumers, so fold it into tooling-13's one-applier decision rather than extracting a shared helper.
- prebuild runs twice in every CI build job, not only the e2e jobs: an explicit step (ci.yml:78, :147, :224, :284), then the npm lifecycle before each `npm run build` (:102, :162, :226, :286). Locally, every `npm run build` also rebuilds the Help site and re-seeds ROOT/data.
- Dockerfile:52-53 still justifies copying data/seed with 'without it a fresh deploy can't seed the benchmark catalog', although commit 4935ac31 deleted the benchmark subsystem. The drift sits in a policy-sensitive file, so a one-line comment fix is still R2 by path.
- `npm run test:restart-recovery` (tests/integration/runtime/restart-recovery.mjs) has zero references outside package.json and org/TASKS.md, and no CI job runs it. That is either an orphan command or an unwired regression test the operator should place in CI or the gate (`git grep -l -F test:restart-recovery -- ':!package.json' ':!org/tasks'`).
- scripts/lib/ps-rename-migrate.sh (43 lines) is invoked by nothing: `git grep -n rename-migrate -- scripts src tests package.json .github Dockerfile` returns only ps-deploy.mjs:205, which is its Node port. The .sh survives only as a manual script named in docs/running/data-storage.md:31 and deploy.md:39. It can be removed independently of the wider CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ alias decision, subject to the operator.
- The design-lint growth log carries 870+19 per-file 'grew' entries, 608 of them naming src/components paths, many since deleted. Past entries are an append-only ledger (T-0025), but design-lint.mjs's --allow-growth writer could record only totals and keys in future entries, so the file stops growing by hundreds of lines per allowance. It saves nothing now and prevents the next 900-line entry.
- The .npmrc comment (lines 3-11) says 'This .npmrc is tracked in git so all developers + CI get the same resolution', but npm ignores all three keys (reproduced warnings), so CI resolution has never depended on it. Any fix belongs with tooling-17.

### Not examined

- Did not run npm run lint, eslint over src, the jest suite, next build, Playwright, docker builds or CI (review rules). So per-step gate durations, the time saved by tooling-07 and tooling-15, and the violation counts for the two globally disabled react-hooks rules (tooling-28) are unmeasured.
- Did not read the large script bodies line by line: ps-deploy.mjs (510), install.sh (446), setup-hindsight.sh (442), setup.sh (327), output-canary.mjs (748), design-lint.mjs (884), the docs pipeline (scripts/docs, 2,057 lines) and scripts/lib/*.sh (769). Duplication within them, and between setup.sh/install.sh and ps-deploy.mjs, was assessed only from headers and grep.
- The three mock servers (mock-hermes, mock-llm, mock-hindsight) were not compared for shared HTTP-server boilerplate. Three consumers could justify a helper, but it was not measured.
- Did not measure what widening knip's project to tests/ and test-harness/ would report (it needs a config change); tooling-16 flags that it will likely surface more.
- tests/integration/test_full_install_update_process.py, tests/scripts/run-shell-custom-tests.sh and tests/scripts/boot-smoke.mjs belong to the tests dimension and were examined only where CI or package.json call them.
- The Docker runtime image's content and size were not measured, including whether copying all of scripts/ into the runner stage (Dockerfile:46) is needed.
- GitHub repository settings (branch protection, required checks) are not visible from the repo; tooling-30 relies on docs/contributing/testing.md:180 and ci.yml:422-425.
- Did not verify whether hermes-registry-import.mjs stores provider key values or only references in the build-time repo-local database (tooling-13); only its header was read.
- Did not check whether prerendered routes need a database at build time, which decides how much of tooling-13 is removable.
- The Node 20 end-of-life date (2026-04-30) in tooling-04 comes from the published Node.js release schedule, not from anything in the repo.

## Dimension: lib-data

src/lib/db holds 41 SQL migrations (001 to 042, 010 absent) driven by 38 applier calls in runMigrations. 18 of those calls are rows of the table driver and 20 are still apply-*.ts wrappers (1,124 lines); 13 of the wrappers (552 lines) are the U15 stanza plus a few trailing statements and could become rows without editing any shipped .sql. The schema-version read is spelled in three src files. db-schema.ts is the shared copy, pulled out of @/lib/db so migration files avoid the Jest mock. upgrade.ts and apply-mission-repeat-migration.ts predate that move and never adopted it. Scripts hold four more copies and tests seven. The baseline-rebuild path (upgrade.ts, 353 lines) fires only above schema_version 103. The head is 42 and the pre-baseline chain ended at 032, so no database either chain wrote can reach it, and the WARNING the docs promise is never printed. Across 34 repository files (307 prepare sites) the consistency helpers are under-used: parse-json has 8 importers against 12 inline JSON.parse lines, and stored run usage JSON is parsed in 6 places with 3 different total rules. In the sync layer, sync_registry is write-only, LogSync re-inserts the same error lines every minute (and the dashboard counts them), and two sources parse .env with different rules. Three tables (benchmark_runs, benchmark_item_results, bench_gateways) are created on every install and read by nothing. The largest wins are folding wrappers into the driver (about -465), removing or fixing the rebuild path (about -365, operator decision), replacing the .mjs parity twin (about -200) and merging the two rebuild wrappers (about -115).

### Measurements

- **SQL migration files**: 41 (numbers 001-042, 010 absent). Method: `ls src/lib/db/migrations | wc -l`
- **apply-*.ts wrappers in src/lib/db**: 20 files, 1,124 lines (plus apply-sql.ts, 47 lines). Method: `ls src/lib/db | grep ^apply- ; wc -l src/lib/db/*.ts`
- **SQL_MIGRATIONS table rows**: 18. Method: `Read src/lib/db/sql-migrations.ts:24-43`
- **Applier calls in runMigrations**: 38. Method: `sed -n 171,340p src/lib/db/index.ts | grep -cE '^\s+apply[A-Za-z]+\(database'`
- **Wrappers that are stanza plus trailing statements**: 13 files, 552 lines. Method: `wc -l on apply-mission-repeat, mission-queue, benchmark-config, bench-gateways, mission-phases, retire-mission-phases, composer, memory-providers, research-options, models-api-style, research-composer-link, composer-group-link, frameworks`
- **Schema-version read spellings**: src 3, scripts 4, tests 7. Method: `Grep 'FROM meta WHERE key' across repo excluding node_modules`
- **Schema probe helper definitions**: 11 in src, 2 in scripts. Method: `Grep 'function (tableExists|columnExists|addColumnIfMissing|getTableColumns)' src scripts`
- **Repository files**: 34 (32 *-repository.ts = 7,997 lines; 2 repository.ts = 270 lines). Method: `git ls-files 'src/**/*-repository.ts' 'src/**/repository.ts' | xargs wc -l`
- **.prepare( sites in repositories**: 307; 50 Row interfaces; about 40 row mappers. Method: `per-file grep -c summed over the 34 repository files`
- **Transaction styles**: inTransaction: 30 sites in 15 files; raw database.transaction(): 6 sites in 4 files. Method: `grep -c 'inTransaction(' and '\.transaction(' per repository file`
- **Inline JSON.parse in repositories**: 12 lines in 7 files; parse-json.ts importers: 8. Method: `grep -nE 'JSON\.parse' over repository files; grep -rl 'db/parse-json' src`
- **SELECT * in repositories**: 73 sites in 19 files (composer-repository.ts 17). Method: `grep -c 'SELECT \*' per repository file`
- **Tables created by migrations**: 52 total; 3 with no src reference outside src/lib/db beyond comments (benchmark_runs, benchmark_item_results, bench_gateways); 1 write-only (sync_registry). Method: `scratch script: regex CREATE TABLE names from migrations, word-match across src/scripts/tests; then grep for SELECT on candidates`
- **In-scope repeated 6-line window lines**: at least 189 of the 1,000 src total, across 10 in-scope files in srcDup.byFile. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, filtered to scope paths`
- **In-scope exports with no src importer**: 141 (0 unused everywhere; most are signature types or test-only). Method: `scratch script matching export names across src/tests/scripts`
- **Stored run usage JSON parse sites**: 6 sites in 5 files, 3 different totalTokens rules. Method: `grep JSON.parse in runs-repository, stats-repository, agent-stats, run-aggregates, spend-window; read each`
- **Shipped seed data**: data/seed 38 files, 55,044 bytes JSON/MD/YAML; SQL seeds: 1 file, 10 lines. Method: `git ls-files data/seed | xargs wc -c; wc -l src/lib/db/seeds/*.sql`
- **Doc references to apply-* file names**: 10 in docs/running/migration.md, 1 in org/plans/2026-08-consolidation.md. Method: `grep -rno 'apply-[a-z-]*\.ts' docs org/plans`

### Findings that survived verification (26 of 27)

#### lib-data-01 · Fold 13 stanza-plus-statements wrappers into the SQL_MIGRATIONS driver

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, -400, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/sql-migrations.ts`, `src/lib/db/index.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-mission-repeat-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-mission-queue-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-benchmark-config-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-bench-gateways-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-mission-phases-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-retire-mission-phases-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-composer-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-memory-providers-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-research-options-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-models-api-style-migration.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l on the 13 wrappers sums to 552; the 024/025/026 no-op markers and the blanket catches (apply-composer-migration.ts:26-30) are confirmed. Who runs this code: a scratch tsx probe (runMigrations on :memory:, nothing written to the repo) gives 'fresh: after pass1 = 3 final = 42 passes = 3', so every fresh install and every suite that calls runMigrations climbs 003-027. That is wider than the operatorDecision's 'installs at or past v27 run none of this'. Safety, re-derived: the same probe replayed the whole chain with the 13 steps rebuilt as file + 'then' statements through execIdempotent (015 per statement, 022/027 seeds as INSERT...SELECT WHERE NOT EXISTS). It threw nothing on the fresh or the v2 path, reached v42, and the schema matched the real chain except for whitespace in the meta DDL; memory_providers and frameworks each got 1 row. Invariant: u15-one-driver-for-sql-migrations.test.ts:41 asserts Number(file.slice(0,3)) === version, but 003 sets v4 and 004 sets v5 (apply-mission-repeat-migration.ts:6, apply-mission-queue-migration.ts:5). Also, 004 is guarded by a columnExists precondition (apply-mission-queue-migration.ts:24), which is not a 'then' statement, so the entry type needs a skip-guard or those two stay out. Coupling: 27 distinct files under tests/ and scripts/ name the wrapper paths (grep -rlE over the 13 names | wc -l); summing per name gives 45. Net: folding the 11 wrappers that keep the prefix invariant removes 465 lines; the rows with 'then' arrays cost about 45, the driver about 12, 11 at() exports 11, and the VESTIGIAL notes about 10, so about -385.
