Adding 003/004 (87 lines) needs the u15:41 assertion relaxed, about -440.
- **Sceptic:** The wrappers and line counts check out, and the change itself is safe. Two things are wrong. The risk is misdescribed: every fresh install runs all 13 wrappers on its second pass, not only databases below v27. And 003/004 break an invariant that u15 pins. Recomputed net is about -400, not -465.
- **Proposal:** Extend the entry type to { version, file, then?: readonly string[], perStatement?: boolean }. applySqlMigration runs the file (split per statement when perStatement, for 015), then runs each 'then' statement through execIdempotent, then calls setSchemaVersion. The seed rows in 022/027 become 'INSERT INTO memory_providers (...) SELECT ... WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memory_providers)'. Keep the one-line at(n) exports so test call sites keep their names and only their import path moves. Shipped .sql files are NOT edited (migration.md:85), so the statements live in the TS table. In the same commit, amend u15's length literal to the new count (updating a pinned number, not weakening a check) and rewrite migration.md step 2 as 'add a row'. Optional second pass: apply-runs-schedules (55) and apply-legacy-column-repair (67, no file) fit the same shape as 'then' ALTERs.
- **Operator decision:** Sign-off needed on one behaviour change on the upgrade path below v28: the wrappers' blanket catch {} becomes execIdempotent, so a genuine failure (for example a missing table) now stops boot instead of recording the version. Installs already at or past v27 run none of this code.

#### lib-data-05 · The baseline-rebuild path in upgrade.ts cannot trigger for any database either chain wrote

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -375, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/upgrade.ts`, `src/lib/db/index.ts`, `tests/unit/db-upgrade.integration.test.ts`, `docs/running/migration.md`, `src/lib/db/backup.ts`, `docs/running/backup.md`
- **Evidence:** upgrade.ts:284-287 returns 'version > BASELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION + 100'. git show a02c0e15 changes '!== BASELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION' to '> ... + 100'. The old chain set the version to the file number (git show 31804e8b^:src/lib/db.ts:137 'setSchemaVersion(database, num)'), and its files end at 032_remove_goals.sql (git ls-tree 31804e8b^ src/lib/db/migrations), so >103 is unreachable. The head is 42. Correction: scripts/lib/ps-migrate.sh:51-52 and :73-79 count *.pre-baseline-* files before and after db:migrate and print ps_warn 'A baseline REBUILD occurred...'. So docs/running/migration.md:194's WARNING is real on the ps-migrate path, and upgrade.ts rightly has 0 console calls. For option (a), also remove or edit ps-migrate.sh:43,51-52,73-79, docs/running/data-storage.md:82,98, docs/reference/api.md:102 (the /api/backup listing documents pre-baseline files), backup.ts:78-82,141, tests/unit/b6-backup-route.test.ts:106-107, tests/unit/b6-database-backup.test.ts:11,170, tests/integration/test_full_install_update_process.py:516 and tests/unit/db-upgrade.integration.test.ts (126 lines, wc -l). The operatorDecision should also cover the /api/backup listing contract in api.md:102.
- **Sceptic:** The rebuild path really is unreachable. But the promised WARNING does exist, printed by ps-migrate.sh. Removal also touches a script, three more docs, the backup API listing and four more test files that the finding does not list.
- **Proposal:** Operator chooses. (a) Remove: delete upgrade.ts, the index.ts branch with its _bootstrapped reset and reopen, the integration suite, the migration.md section 'If a database can't be migrated in place' and its preserved-table list. Decide whether backup.ts keeps listing existing .pre-baseline- files (backup.ts:78-82, :141). (b) Keep and make it true: detect a pre-baseline shape (for example version <= 32 without agent_root), print the promised warning, and replace importMissionRow with importRows plus defaults (about -60 lines).
- **Operator decision:** Removes a documented behaviour (baseline rebuild with a pre-baseline backup, docs/running/migration.md:192-196) and possibly the pre-baseline row in docs/running/backup.md:57. The operator must confirm no live install carries schema_version > 103 or a database from before 2026-05-20.

#### lib-data-20 · prebuild runs a hand-copied .mjs twin of the v2-to-v3 parity migration

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -190, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs`, `src/lib/db/profiles-tools-parity-ensure.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-profiles-tools-upgrade.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs = 167. Importers: prebuild-db.mjs:11-14 and scripts/tooling/hermes-registry-import.mjs:11 (columnExists), which the finding missed; comments naming it sit at apply-neutral-column-names.ts:9, composer-cancel-is-a-decision.test.ts:442, composer-reject-is-not-a-cliff.test.ts:363 and neutral-column-names-migration.test.ts:8. prebuild-db.mjs:61-84 forces v>=3 (unlinks the DB and applies the baseline) before calling applyProfilesToolsParityUpgrade at :89, so db-schema-ensure.mjs:139-161's scan never runs from prebuild. The .mjs warns and the TS swallows (db-schema-ensure.mjs:152-156 vs apply-profiles-tools-upgrade.ts:32-36). The operatorDecision should add that prebuild currently deletes any build DB below v3 (prebuild-db.mjs:62-70), which migrate-db.ts never does.
- **Sceptic:** The twin is real, but it has a second importer, so it cannot simply be deleted. Its directory-scan branch is also unreachable from prebuild. The prebuild's destructive reset is a further behaviour the operator should see.
- **Proposal:** Have prebuild run scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts through tsx, the same chain the app runs (migration.md:66 says the two 'can never drift'), then seed, and delete db-schema-ensure.mjs. If the build database must stay at v3 (apply-sql.ts:40-43 mentions prebuilt databases shipped without the .sql files), import the TS parity step through tsx rather than copying it, and say why in prebuild.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the build step: the prebuilt data/patterstage.db would be migrated to the head rather than v3 (unless the TS parity path is chosen). Confirm with the Docker and CI images that run prebuild.

#### lib-data-02 · One rebuild step for 035 and 037, and the 037 error message that names 035

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -100, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/apply-composer-rejected-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-composer-node-cancelled-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/sql-migrations.ts`
- **Evidence:** Census byFile gives both files 21 lines. assertRebuildIsLossless is identical at apply-composer-rejected-migration.ts:47-62 and apply-composer-node-cancelled-migration.ts:34-49, and :43 of the second file throws 'Migration 035 refuses to rebuild' from migration 037. Both headers claim to be the head (:26 T-0069, :22 T-0076); the real head is 42 (db-schema.ts:35). The two files total 164 lines. Missed couplings: u15-one-driver-for-sql-migrations.test.ts:69 asserts apply-composer-rejected-migration.ts still exists, and run-migrations-upgrade.integration.test.ts:21-22 imports both version constants from those file paths. Net inside the table: 164 minus about 25 for the driver rebuild branch, about 12 for expected-column rows, about 10 for the kept rationale and 4 for exports/constants, so about -110. Without lib-data-01, a shared helper for two consumers saves about 30.

- **Proposal:** Give table entries an optional rebuild: Record<table, readonly string[]> of expected columns. applySqlMigration asserts each table's shape, turns foreign_keys OFF, runs the file in a transaction and restores foreign_keys in finally. Delete both files; keep at(35)/at(37) exports and the two version constants the tests read. The error message takes its number from the entry. Without lib-data-01, a shared helper for two consumers saves only about 30 lines.

#### lib-data-12 · triggerSyncOnce debounces a call that is already idempotent, and never runs a sync

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** naming, low, -60, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/sessions/sessions-api-helpers.ts`, `src/app/api/sessions/route.ts`, `tests/unit/sessions-api-helpers.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b1-read-only-reads-do-not-write.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** sessions-api-helpers.ts:34-56 (pendingSync, 30_000 ms timer). src/lib/sync/index.ts:37-39 'if (_initialized) return;'. The only caller is src/app/api/sessions/route.ts:61; src/instrumentation.ts:61-62 already calls ensureSyncLayer() at boot. The inline sync is guarded separately (session-repository.ts:384-412, 2_000 ms). Tests: sessions-api-helpers.test.ts:150 onward and the mock at b1-read-only-reads-do-not-write.test.ts:47-51.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. ensureSyncLayer is idempotent and already runs at server boot, so the debounce only suppresses calls that do nothing.
- **Proposal:** Call ensureSyncLayer() directly in the route when writes are allowed. Delete triggerSyncOnce, pendingSync and _resetSyncDebounceForTests, move the b1 mock onto ensureSyncLayer, and delete the triggerSyncOnce describe block.

#### lib-data-11 · Sync sources parse .env twice with different rules and restate a repository type

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -50, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/sync/sources/EnvSync.ts`, `src/lib/sync/sources/ProcessSync.ts`, `src/lib/sync/sources/MissionQueueSync.ts`, `src/lib/sync/sync-repository.ts`
- **Evidence:** EnvSync.ts:17-29 drops '' and 'changeme'. ProcessSync.ts:84-119 uses envContent.includes('DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN=') inside the per-gateway loop that starts at :79. The access probe appears twice (EnvSync.ts:43-49, ProcessSync.ts:89-95). ParsedProcess at ProcessSync.ts:19-28 is the same shape as AgentProcessInput at sync-repository.ts:134-143 (census: sync-repository 15, ProcessSync 14). EnvSync.ts:62-89 gives enabled and bot_token_present the same expression for all four platforms. Delete and insert run as separate calls (ProcessSync.ts:161-165), and each insert is its own transaction (sync-repository.ts:168-198). The SyncResult literals are at MissionQueueSync.ts:22-37.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived every sub-claim from the source files.
- **Proposal:** ProcessSync imports 'type AgentProcessInput'. EnvSync maps a table of [platform, tokenKeys[]] once. ProcessSync builds its label from readGatewayPlatforms(), or from an exported parseEnvVars read once before the loop. Add replaceAgentProcesses() in sync-repository that runs the delete and insert in one inTransaction. Leave MissionQueueSync until a second source needs success-with-error.
- **Operator decision:** The gateway label on /api/agents can change for a .env token line that is empty or 'changeme'. Dropping the redundant gateway_platforms.bot_token_present column would be a schema change and is not proposed.

#### lib-data-19 · Mission categories are the only seed kept as SQL: run by two paths and counted by string split

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, low, -45, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/seeds/001_mission_categories.sql`, `src/lib/seed/catalog-seed.ts`, `src/lib/seed/seed-repository.ts`, `scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs`, `tests/unit/ensure-default-categories.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** src/lib/db/seeds/001_mission_categories.sql is 10 lines. It runs from prebuild-db.mjs:96-123 and from catalog-seed.ts:90-99 via seed-repository.ts:33-35 execSeedScript, and is counted by split('ch.cat.') at catalog-seed.ts:420-422. Missed: mission-category-repository.ts:299-310 declares DEFAULT_CATEGORY_SEED_SQL, the same 8 rows, which ensureDefaultCategories() at :313-320 runs through getDb().exec. The path literal is at catalog-seed.ts:91, :421, prebuild-db.mjs:25 and ensure-default-categories.test.ts:43. data/seed has 38 files, 55,044 bytes (git ls-files data/seed | xargs cat | wc -c). Folding all three into one manifest removes about 12 more lines.
- **Sceptic:** Real, but the finding undercounts. The eight category rows exist in three copies, not one file run by two paths, and the third copy also executes raw SQL from inside a repository.
- **Proposal:** Move the rows to data/seed/categories/manifest.json, upsert them through mission-category-repository in seedCategories, and count them with countInManifest. Then delete execSeedScript, src/lib/db/seeds/ and prebuild's seeds loop.

#### lib-data-04 · tableExists / columnExists / addColumnIfMissing defined eleven times in src

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -40, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/apply-legacy-column-repair.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-mission-queue-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-neutral-column-names.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-runs-schedules-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/index.ts`, `src/lib/db/profiles-tools-parity-ensure.ts`, `src/lib/db/upgrade.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-category-schema-repository.ts`, `src/lib/sessions/session-sync-repository.ts`
- **Evidence:** Grep 'function (tableExists|columnExists|addColumnIfMissing|getTableColumns)' over src finds exactly the 11 sites listed. Probe (scratch tsx, :memory:) prints '(a) PRAGMA table_info on missing table: no throw, rows = 0'. So the try/catch around PRAGMA in apply-legacy-column-repair.ts:29-34 and apply-neutral-column-names.ts columnExists is dead code, and apply-mission-queue-migration.ts:7 and apply-runs-schedules-migration.ts:17 do not throw. addColumnIfMissing is duplicated at apply-runs-schedules-migration.ts:22-34 and apply-legacy-column-repair.ts:37-49 (census: legacy-repair 16 lines). Net after lib-data-01/05 remove three copies: about 73 lines go, a new schema-probe.ts costs about 22 plus about 6 import lines, so about -45.
- **Sceptic:** The eleven definitions exist, but the stated reason is false. The copies do not disagree: PRAGMA table_info on a missing table returns zero rows without throwing, so every copy already returns false. What remains is plain deduplication.
- **Proposal:** Add src/lib/db/schema-probe.ts with a type-only better-sqlite3 import, so it keeps the no-runtime-import constraint mission-category-schema-repository.ts relies on. Export tableExists, columnExists (false on a missing table) and addColumnIfMissing, and import them at each site. Sequence after lib-data-01, which deletes two of the copies. session-sync keeps its D6 self-heal call and only swaps the probe.

#### lib-data-23 · A mission-categories health check lives in the connection module

#### lib-data-03 · Schema-version read spelled three times in src and seven more outside it; baseline version 3 declared five times

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -30, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db-schema.ts`, `src/lib/db/upgrade.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-mission-repeat-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/index.ts`, `src/lib/db/profiles-tools-parity-ensure.ts`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/tooling/prebuild-db.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs`, `tests/jest.setup.ts`
- **Evidence:** grep -rn 'SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key' src scripts tests finds schema_version reads at db-schema.ts:40, upgrade.ts:80, apply-mission-repeat-migration.ts:11, migrate-db.ts:53 and :90, prebuild-db.mjs:42, db-schema-ensure.mjs:125 and jest.setup.ts:125. The meta readers at system-repository.ts:29 and seed-repository.ts:48 read other keys. The census top window 'x3: const row = database | .prepare("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = ?")' is confirmed. Correction: the third '3' constant is profiles-tools-parity-ensure.ts:23 (PROFILES_TOOLS_PARITY_SCHEMA_VERSION), not :103, which is an ALTER. The others are index.ts:139, upgrade.ts:19, prebuild-db.mjs:59 and db-schema-ensure.mjs:23. Overlap: the mission-repeat copy disappears with lib-data-01 and the upgrade.ts copy with lib-data-05(a), which leaves about -8 (migrate-db versionOf plus one constant). Independently, migrate-db.ts:100-106 re-loops runMigrations right after getDb() at :86, and getDb has already looped to convergence at index.ts:107-114.
- **Sceptic:** The duplicated reads are real, but one location is wrong, and most of the saving overlaps lib-data-01 and lib-data-05.
- **Proposal:** Delete the two src copies and import getSchemaVersion/setSchemaVersion from @/lib/db-schema. Export BASELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION once from db-schema.ts and import it in index.ts, upgrade.ts and profiles-tools-parity-ensure.ts. In migrate-db.ts, replace versionOf() (:88-93) with the imported getSchemaVersion. The two .mjs copies go with lib-data-20.

#### lib-data-13 · Stored run usage JSON is parsed in six places with three different totals

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -30, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/runs/runs-repository.ts`, `src/lib/stats/stats-repository.ts`, `src/lib/stats/agent-stats.ts`, `src/lib/analytics/run-aggregates.ts`, `src/lib/spend/spend-window.ts`, `src/lib/models/usage-shape.ts`
- **Evidence:** runs-repository.ts:51-63 'totalTokens: Number(o.totalTokens ?? 0)'. stats-repository.ts:101-122 'num(o.totalTokens) || input + output'. agent-stats.ts:57-69 uses the same rule. run-aggregates.ts:~95 'Number(u.totalTokens ?? input + output)'. run-aggregates.ts:~138 'Number(u.totalTokens ?? 0)'. spend-window.ts:~162 reads input/output only. function num is defined at stats-repository.ts:101 and agent-stats.ts:57. models/usage-shape.ts:32-48 reads the provider vocabularies (promptTokens/prompt_tokens/input_tokens), not the stored inputTokens.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived all six parse sites and the three different total rules.
- **Proposal:** Add parseStoredUsage(raw: string | null): RunUsage | null next to RunUsage, where total is a finite totalTokens or else input + output, and non-finite values count as absent. Use it at all six sites; spend-window keeps its skip-unparseable rule by checking for null.
- **Operator decision:** Displayed totals change for runs stored without totalTokens: run detail and top-missions token totals will read input + output instead of 0.

#### lib-data-06 · runMigrations returns after the baseline, so getDb and db:migrate both loop to converge

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, medium, -26, confirmed

- **Evidence:** Scratch probe-e.ts (tsx, :memory:): baseline plus setSchemaVersion(3) plus ONE runMigrations pass prints 'version 42 fresh 42 diff only-one: [] only-fresh: []' against a DB converged by repeated passes. index.ts:188-192 returns after the baseline. The getDb loop is at index.ts:107-114; migrate-db.ts:100-106 loops again after getDb() at :86. The pragma trio is at index.ts:95-97 and :199-201, and ensureDb's flag at :346-356. grep -rl 'ensureDb()' src | wc -l = 36. The pinning test is at run-migrations-upgrade.integration.test.ts:183-204, the doc at migration.md:69. Separately, migrate-db.ts:84's 'Smoke-tested ... (0 -> 30)' is stale.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived: one pass after the baseline reaches the head with an identical schema, and both loops exist as described. The operatorDecision correctly flags the documented multi-pass behaviour.
- **Proposal:** Drop the return after the baseline so one pass reaches the head. Delete both loops: getDb runs runMigrations once, and migrate-db.ts calls getDb() and reads the version. Fold the pragma trio into one openDatabase(path). ensureDb becomes a plain getDb() call without the flag. Rewrite the pinning test to assert one pass reaches MIGRATION_HEAD_SCHEMA_VERSION. Simplest after lib-data-05 removes the reopen branch.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the documented 'A fresh database converges over several passes' behaviour (docs/running/migration.md:69) to a single pass. Nothing visible to users changes.

SQL_MIGRATIONS at sql-migrations.ts:24-43 jumps 14->16->19->28 because workers interleave. #### lib-data-10 · LogSync re-inserts the same error lines every minute, and the dashboard counts the copies

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** performance, low, 3, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/sync/sources/LogSync.ts`, `src/lib/sync/sync-repository.ts`, `src/lib/stats/stats-repository.ts`, `src/lib/db/migrations/001_baseline.sql`

Plain INSERT at sync-repository.ts:91-93. 001_baseline.sql:254-263 has error_log_entries with only idx_errors_timestamp. SyncScheduler.ts:55 'logs: 60_000'. Prune at sync-repository.ts:121-129. errors24h is 'COUNT(*) ... WHERE ingested_at >= datetime('now', '-1 day')' (stats-repository.ts:270-272). LIMIT 10 recent read at sync-repository.ts:112-118.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived: deduplication happens only within one run, the insert is plain, there is no unique constraint, the logs sync is due every 60 s, and errors24h counts by ingested_at.
- **Proposal:** Code-only fix: insert with 'INSERT INTO error_log_entries (...) SELECT ?,?,?,?,? WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM error_log_entries WHERE source = ? AND timestamp = ? AND message = ?)' (the timestamp index keeps the probe cheap), and add a two-run test asserting the row count stays stable. Schema alternative: a UNIQUE index plus INSERT OR IGNORE via a migration row. Correct the header comment either way.
- **Operator decision:** Changes the 'errors in the last 24h' figure the dashboard shows (it drops to real distinct lines). The UNIQUE-index variant is a schema change.

#### lib-data-08 · Three benchmark tables are created on every install and read by nothing

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, 12, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/db/migrations/014_benchmarks.sql`, `src/lib/db/migrations/015_benchmark_config.sql`, `src/lib/db/migrations/017_bench_gateways.sql`, `src/lib/db/apply-bench-gateways-migration.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-benchmark-config-migration.ts`, `tests/unit/run-migrations-upgrade.integration.test.ts`, `src/lib/stats/agent-experience.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/endpoint-registry.ts`
- **Evidence:** grep -rnw over src scripts tests, excluding migrations: benchmark_item_results appears only in apply-bench-gateways-migration.ts:5,39 and the test; benchmark_runs only in comments (apply-benchmark-config-migration.ts:12, stats/agent-experience.ts:37); bench_gateways only in comments (endpoint-registry.ts:18). Tests assert them at run-migrations-upgrade.integration.test.ts:76-88 and again at :208, in the fresh-convergence test's arrayContaining. :75-88 is also the v14 'footgun guard' and would need a replacement table. org/decisions/ADR-0004-brain-and-body.md:137-143 records that benchmark_runs, benchmark_item_results (and bench_gateways) stay because of the strictly increasing chain. A drop migration does not edit the ADR but reverses a recorded consequence, so the operator may want a superseding ADR.
- **Sceptic:** The tables are dead. But the finding misses that ADR-0004, in the protected set, records keeping them, and a second test site that must flip.
- **Proposal:** Append 043_drop_benchmark_tables.sql (DROP TABLE IF EXISTS for the three) as a SQL_MIGRATIONS row, raise MIGRATION_HEAD_SCHEMA_VERSION, and flip the three upgrade-test assertions to not.toContain. Shipped 014/015/017 stay as history.
- **Operator decision:** Schema change: drops benchmark_runs, benchmark_item_results and bench_gateways (and any rows an old install still holds) from every database.

### Refuted, and why

- **lib-data-22 · The baseline test fixture re-spells migration steps one column at a time.** The fixture's minimal surface and its v3 pin are deliberate, and the file says why. No step list exists as data for a migrateTo helper to walk, and running runMigrations would create every table and move the version. The -35 is not demonstrated.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- The eight mission-category rows exist three times: src/lib/db/seeds/001_mission_categories.sql, DEFAULT_CATEGORY_SEED_SQL at src/lib/missions/mission-category-repository.ts:299-310 (run through getDb().exec by ensureDefaultCategories at :313-320), and catalog-seed.ts's seedCategories via execSeedScript. Folding all three into one data/seed manifest is about 12 lines more than lib-data-19 counts.
- src/lib/seed/seed-repository.ts:48 and :56 read and write the meta table directly (key 'catalog_seeded'). That contradicts sync-repository.ts:11-12, which says meta has one repository (system-repository.ts, whose getSystemStat/setSystemStat at :27-48 already do this). About -6 lines. Re-run: grep -rn 'INTO meta\|FROM meta' src --include=*.ts | grep -v 'src/lib/db/\|db-schema.ts'.
- scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts:95-106 re-runs runMigrations in a loop right after getDb() at :86, which already loops to convergence (src/lib/db/index.ts:107-114). It is redundant today, needs no decision, and saves about 12 lines. Its comment at :84 ('0 -> 30') is also stale.
- mission-category-schema-repository.ts:22-29 missionCategoriesTableExists duplicates the hasMissionCategoriesTable() that mission-category-repository.ts:314 already calls, so two probes for one table exist in the missions domain.
- Dead try/catch: PRAGMA table_info never throws on a missing table (verified against better-sqlite3 on :memory:), so the catch blocks in apply-legacy-column-repair.ts:29-34 and in apply-neutral-column-names.ts's columnExists are unreachable, and their comments ('Table absent on a partial/minimal schema') describe behaviour that cannot happen.
- scripts/tooling/db-schema-ensure.mjs:139-161 (directory scan and schema-version bump) is unreachable from its only schema caller, because prebuild-db.mjs:61-84 forces v>=3 first. Only ensureProfilesToolsParity and columnExists are live, and hermes-registry-import.mjs:11 imports the latter.
- scripts/lib/ps-migrate.sh:43, :51-52 and :73-79 (pre-baseline rebuild detection and warning) become dead together with upgrade.ts's unreachable rebuild. It belongs in any lib-data-05 removal, and tests/integration/test_full_install_update_process.py:516 filters on the same file pattern.
- Header drift in the sync layer: sync-repository.ts:6-7 says sync_registry holds 'every source's own status row', but only SessionSync writes it. SessionSync.ts:4-6 says 'The sessions API route now just reads from the DB', but src/app/api/sessions/route.ts:76 passes syncIfActive and listSessions still runs syncHermesSessionsToDb inline (session-repository.ts:405-412).

### Not examined

- Column-level dead-data analysis: only table names were matched against code; individual columns were not traced from writer to reader (for example sessions.provider, sync_registry.source_mtime).
- Nothing was executed: no jest suite, no app run, no live database. The LogSync duplicate rows, the missing idx_runs_story on a v2 climb and the unreachable rebuild branch come from reading code and git history, not from observation.
- Whether any live install carries schema_version > 103 or a database from before 2026-05-20 cannot be determined from the repository (lib-data-05).
- Repository bodies checked only through grep metrics, not read line by line: composer, models, credentials, schedules, chat, deep-research, retention, agent-progression, analytics, stats/derive.ts, retention-prune.ts.
- src/app/api/mission-categories/route.ts was not read, so how it uses getSchemaHealth's result is unverified (lib-data-23).
- SyncScheduler.ts beyond its constants and method outline, and the hermes module's ConfigSync source (src/modules/hermes), were not reviewed.
- Dockerfile and CI use of prebuild-db.mjs were not checked (lib-data-20).
- The ADR record beyond the ADR-0004 and ADR-0009 references in code comments was not read for rulings on dropping tables.
- src/lib/analytics/event-types.ts and categories.ts (taxonomies embedded in TypeScript) were not assessed for a move to JSON.

## Dimension: components

Scope is 204 files and 26,009 lines. Of those, src/components/ui holds 39 primitive files and 3,610 lines. The primitive set still has more than one implementation of several things: three names for one overlay (Modal 14 src importers, Sheet 3, Dialog 11); two custom dropdowns (field Select 16, Picker 4) plus two native select chromes (InlineSelect 8, NativeSelect 1); three switches (InlineToggle 4, ui/Input.tsx Toggle 1, field Toggle 1); two homes for text controls (ui/Input.tsx, which holds six controls, 12 importers; field/Input 28); and two surfaces (Card 72, dashboard/Panel 25). LoadingSpinner/PageLoading/Skeleton and Picker/ProfilePicker each do a different job and should stay, while TemplateCard survives only as a wrapper with a dead branch behind TemplatePill. Design-lint reports 0 on every rule, but its raw-control rule cannot see an opening tag that ends its line, and that hides 83 raw controls in 33 component files. The source also shows 28 of 46 <label> elements that point at nothing, 10 field Selects and 8 AutoTextareas with no accessible name, and 13 expand/collapse buttons without aria-expanded. The five largest components (MissionCreateForm 808, WorkflowCanvas 701, MissionsList 494, SchedulePicker 464, AutomationList 433) mix pure logic, API write sequences, dead modes and duplicated blocks in with rendering. The biggest single duplication is the directories-and-references editor, repeated in MissionCreateForm and TemplateEditorModal: about 205 lines that fold to about -105 net. The skills row handler bundle, passed through four files, accounts for 88 of the census's 1,000 repeated src window lines. Of the 103 one-importer components, 11 exist only because a barrel file is their importer, 57 are page sections and 35 are pieces of one parent; only TemplateCard, field/Toggle and StatusBadge read as wrappers. Taking every finding would net roughly -1,200 lines in scope, about 40% of the 2,881-line srcLines miss. The main risk is 81 test references to component files by path that break on a move or rename.

### Measurements

- **Files in scope**: 204 (src/components 201, src/kit 2, src/types 2 incl. PROVENANCE.md). Method: `git ls-files src/components src/kit src/types | wc -l`
- **Lines in scope**: 26,009. Method: `git ls-files src/components src/kit src/types | xargs wc -l (total)`
- **ui primitive set**: 39 files, 3,610 lines. Method: `git ls-files src/components/ui | xargs wc -l`
- **Components over 350 lines**: 11: MissionCreateForm 808, WorkflowCanvas 701, MissionsList 494, SchedulePicker 464, AutomationList 433, Sidebar 422, TemplateEditorModal 421, MissionEditorPanel 421, ModelEditor 407, ui/Input 375, SimpleMarkdown 363. Method: `git ls-files src/components | xargs wc -l | sort -rn`
- **Importers of each primitive pair (src/tests)**: Modal 14/4; Sheet 3/4; Dialog 11/2 (incl. Modal, Sheet, SplitPane); ui/Input.tsx 12/3 (SearchInput 6, InlineToggle 4, Select 2, Toggle 1, NumberInput 1, TextInput 1); ui/Select.tsx InlineSelect 8/1; ui/field index 41/1 (Input 28, Field 16, Select 16, Textarea 16, Toggle 1); field/Select.tsx NativeSelect 1/0; Card 72/1 (StatusDot 4); GlowSurface 3 (incl. Card)/0; dashboard/Panel 25/0 (PanelHeader 5, only 6 importers in components/dashboard); LoadingSpinner 12/2; PageLoading 10/1; Skeleton 5/1; Picker 4/1; ProfilePicker 4/7; TemplateCard 1/0 (TemplatePill); TemplatePill 2/0. Method: `Scratchpad resolver importers.mjs: resolves every import/re-export/jest.mock/require specifier (@/ alias and relative) in src, tests, scripts to a file and records named imports per importer`
- **One-importer components by kind**: 103 of 176: 11 barrel artefacts (field Input/Toggle/Field via field/index.ts; achievements x3 via achievements/index.ts; viz TopList/ActivityHeatmap/RadialActivityClock via viz/index.ts; templates Manager/Editor modals via missions/TemplateModals.tsx), 57 imported once by an app page, 35 imported once by a parent component. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report (oneImporter) joined with importers.json srcImporters`
- **Design-lint violations**: 0 on every rule, including no-raw-control-outside-ui. Method: `node scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs --report`
- **Raw controls whose opening tag ends its line (invisible to the per-line rule)**: 83 in src/components outside ui, across 33 files; 127 across src/components + src/app + src/modules. Method: `rg -n "<(button|input|select|textarea)\s*$" src/components -g '*.tsx' -g '!src/components/ui/**' | wc -l`
- **Labels that point at nothing**: 28 of 46 <label> elements in src/components have no htmlFor and wrap no control. Method: `Scratchpad a11y-scan.mjs: JSX opening-tag parse, checks htmlFor and whether a control appears before </label>`
- **Controls with no accessible name**: 10 field-kit <Select> with no ariaLabel (5 in scope); 8 <AutoTextarea> with no ariaLabel (name falls back to placeholder). Method: `a11y-scan.mjs over files importing Select from @/components/ui/field, and every AutoTextarea`
- **Expand/collapse buttons without aria-expanded**: 13 (plus ui/CategoryAccordion.tsx:41, missed by the scan regex). Method: `a11y-scan.mjs: <button>/<Button> whose onClick flips a boolean state and whose tag has no aria-expanded`
- **Exported names with no importer outside their file**: 85 (mostly *Props interfaces). Method: `Scratchpad unused-exports.mjs over importers.json; default exports re-exported through barrels excluded as false positives`
- **Non-component modules inside src/components**: 24 .ts files, 1,450 lines (4 data hooks = 525 lines). Method: `git ls-files 'src/components/**/*.ts' | xargs wc -l`
- **Test/script references to scope files by source-text path**: 81 references from 46 tests/scripts. Method: `importers.mjs textRefs: tests and scripts containing a scope file's path string or join() segments`
- **Comment references to paths that do not exist**: 4. Method: `node scan of comment lines in scope for @/lib/... and src/...ext paths, checked with fs.existsSync`
- **Hand-written plural ternaries**: 17 in src/components, while pluralise (src/lib/utils.ts:126) is used by 7 component files. Method: `rg -n '=== 1 \? "" : "s"|=== 1 \? "[a-z]+" : "[a-z]+"' src/components`
- **Comment density in scope**: 4,339 of 26,155 lines (17%); Sidebar.tsx 142 of 423. Method: `node count of lines starting with //, *, /* or {/* (wider than the census regex, which ignores JSX comments)`
- **Direct useTwoStepConfirm wirings beside ConfirmButton**: 11 (components 4, app pages 4, hooks 3). Method: `rg -l useTwoStepConfirm src, excluding the hook, ConfirmButton and a comment-only match in FallbackChainList.tsx`

### Findings that survived verification (33 of 33)

#### components-02 · Two custom dropdowns: field-kit Select duplicates Picker and is the less accessible one

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -110, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/ui/field/Select.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Picker.tsx`, `src/components/ui/field/index.ts`, `src/components/models/ModelSelectDropdown.tsx`, `src/components/composer/WorkflowCanvas.tsx`, `src/components/composer/ComposerRunForm.tsx`
- **Evidence:** `rg -n "import .*\bSelect\b.*from \"@/components/ui/field\"" src | wc -l` = 16. `rg -l "ui/Picker\"" src` = AgentRuntimeDefaultsCard, ProfilePicker, MissionCreateForm, SkillsPicker (4). Picker label: `label: string` (Picker.tsx:36), rendered only as aria-label (:234, :299); no id prop in PickerBase (:34-58). Unnamed sites inside Field: ComposerRunForm.tsx:147-148, WorkflowCanvas.tsx:435-436, :644, :652-653. ModelSelectDropdown importers: DefaultsGrid, BulkAuxiliaryUpdater (2). HEIGHT md = h-8 (Picker.tsx:78-82) vs field Select py-2.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. field Select (field/Select.tsx:21-135) has no aria-activedescendant, no Home/End, no Tab-close and no id prop, and it closes on a hand-rolled mousedown (:42-49). Picker has aria-controls/activedescendant (:235,:289), Home/End/Tab (:180-198), useDismissable (:127) and a required label (:36). There are 16 import sites of field Select. Corrections: Picker has 4 src importers, and ProfilePicker is one of them. Picker also takes no id, so a <Field label> around it still points htmlFor at nothing; the name comes from Picker's required aria-label (:234). The net is about -110 (135 lines go, plus about 16 split import lines and a few label props).
- **Proposal:** Survivor is Picker. Replace `Select` from @/components/ui/field with Picker at the 16 sites (ariaLabel -> label, 1:1; the 10 unnamed sites gain a label). Point ModelSelectDropdown at Picker; with 2 consumers it keeps its thin wrapper. Delete field/Select.tsx:14-135 and its index export, keeping NativeSelect until components-13.
- **Operator decision:** Visible change: Picker's md trigger is h-8 (Picker.tsx:78-82) where field Select is py-2 beside py-2 Inputs, and option highlight colours differ, across 16 screens. Pick size lg or add a size, and re-baseline the design census.

#### components-10 · Three more two-click delete buttons beside ConfirmButton, one with armed state owned by the page

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -90, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/memory/hindsight/RowActionButtons.tsx`, `src/components/models/PerRowDeleteButton.tsx`, `src/components/dashboard/ActiveMissionsPanel.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ConfirmButton.tsx`, `src/components/models/CredentialsPanel.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateManagerModal.tsx`
- **Evidence:** RowActionButtons.tsx:58-77 and PerRowDeleteButton.tsx:33-71 share aria-label `Click again to confirm deleting ...`/`Delete ...` and the ring-semantic-danger armed class. PerRowDeleteButton.tsx:39 `const deleteConfirm = useTwoStepConfirm({ autoDismissMs: 4000 });`. ActiveMissionsPanel.tsx:25-28 (onCancel, isArmedFor) and :82-92 (`<button onClick=...` with no type). app/page.tsx:106 `const { isArmedFor, arm, confirm: confirmArmed } = useTwoStepConfirm(...)`. `rg -l useTwoStepConfirm src` = 14 files including the hook and ConfirmButton.
- **Sceptic:** Real. RowDeleteButton and PerRowDeleteButton are near-identical. PerRowDeleteButton's header (:11-15) says the hook lives in the parent row, but it is created at :39. ActiveMissionsPanel's cancel takes page-owned armed state and has no type=. Corrections: 12 files wire useTwoStepConfirm directly outside the hook and ConfirmButton; the reviewer omitted FallbackChainList. ActiveMissionsPanel's cancel is a text button, which ConfirmButton already supports without an icon mode. In-scope net: -116 (PerRowDeleteButton 71 + RowDeleteButton :33-77) + about 20 for an icon mode + about 5 in ActiveMissionsPanel, giving about -90. The app/page.tsx confirm state (:106, :144-161) is app scope.
- **Proposal:** Give ConfirmButton an icon-only mode: an idle and armed accessible label built from a name prop, with IconButton sizing. Replace RowDeleteButton, PerRowDeleteButton and ActiveMissionsPanel's cancel with it, and drop the isArmedFor prop from ActiveMissionsPanel and the dashboard page's confirm state. The app pages and hooks follow in their own dimensions.

#### components-15 · TemplateCard's full-card branch is dead, and template icons are registered twice

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -90, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/ui/TemplateCard.tsx`, `src/components/ui/TemplatePill.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`
- **Evidence:** TemplatePill.tsx:25-34 `<TemplateCard ... compact`. TemplateCard.tsx:45 `iconMap[icon] || Zap`, :47-57 the compact button with no type=, :59-79 dead. `rg -n Bot src/components/ui/TemplateCard.tsx | wc -l` = 0. TemplateEditorModal.tsx:30-49 (TEMPLATE_ICONS includes "Bot" at :47) and :53-72. `rg -l "TemplateCard|TemplatePill" src tests` = TemplatePill, TemplateCard, MissionsList, DispatchStrip (no tests).
- **Sceptic:** The dead branch is confirmed: TemplatePill always passes compact, and it is TemplateCard's only importer. The icon claim is wrong in a way that matters. TemplateEditorModal offers 18 icons, but 'Bot' is not in TemplateCard's 38-entry map, so a template saved with Bot renders as the Zap fallback in the dashboard and missions pills. Unifying the registry fixes that bug but changes what those templates show. Net: TemplateCard -80, the pill button +12, the new icon module about +30, the editor's imports, list and map about -52, giving about -90.
- **Proposal:** Fold the compact button into TemplatePill and delete TemplateCard.tsx. Move the icon map to one module (e.g. src/lib/missions/template-icons.ts) that exports the full map and the editor's 18-name pick list, so the picker does not change.

#### components-08 · Two surface primitives: dashboard/Panel duplicates Card's chrome for 25 importers

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -85, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/dashboard/Panel.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Card.tsx`, `src/components/ui/GlowSurface.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Panel.tsx:27-80 (two 8-case switches), :105 `rounded-ps-lg border ${panelBorderClass(accent)} ${panelTintClass(tint)} overflow-hidden`, :106-118 `{...props}`. Card.tsx:72 `rounded-ps-lg border border-ps-edge-hairline ${fill} min-w-0 ...` with no rest spread. `rg -l "dashboard/Panel\"" src | wc -l` = 25; `rg -l "ui/Card\"" src | wc -l` = 72. role="alert" at app/help/[[...slug]]/page.tsx:95 and config/ConfigYamlErrorAlert.tsx:35. LogTerminal.tsx:13-16: 'THE REF GOES ON THE DIV THAT SCROLLS. It used to go on <Panel>'. No test imports dashboard/Panel (the two `PanelProps` in tests are local aliases). design-lint.mjs:418 'A card is <Surface>'.
- **Sceptic:** Real, with an understated prerequisite. Card forwards no rest props: CardProps (Card.tsx:10-45) carries only children, className, glow*, hover, padding, variant, as, id and data-testid. Two Panel sites pass role="alert". Card also lacks Panel's overflow-hidden and wraps GlowSurface. The LogTerminal claim is stale: its comment says the scroll ref no longer goes on Panel. Net: Panel.tsx is 145 lines, and PanelHeader (about 30) moves rather than disappears. Card gains accent and tint Records (about 20) plus rest forwarding (about 5), giving about -85. Visual parity across 25 sites needs the design census.
- **Proposal:** Survivor is Card. Add accent and tint as Record<AccentColor,string> maps (the switches compress to about 20 lines), forward rest props (role, ref, onScroll: LogTerminal relies on them), and move PanelHeader into ui. The 25 sites import Card with padding="none". Delete Panel.tsx. Fix the lint law's wording in the tooling pass.

#### components-05 · Modal and Sheet are thin renames of Dialog, which is the survivor

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -72, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Sheet.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Dialog.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Modal.tsx:25-41, Sheet.tsx:27-43, Dialog.tsx:12-13 and :88-91. `rg -l "from \"@/components/ui/Modal\"" src | wc -l` = 14; Sheet importers = app/work/missions/page.tsx, app/results/artifacts/page.tsx, ComposerNodeRunDetail.tsx. `rg -l "ui/Modal|ui/Sheet" tests` = category-manager-modal, feedback-is-perceivable, modal-a11y, sheet-a11y, u11-the-dialog.
- **Sceptic:** Modal passes placement="center" and closeLabel="Close dialog", both Dialog's defaults (Dialog.tsx:88,91). Sheet adds placement, ariaLabel={title ?? "Panel"}, closeLabel and dismissOnOverlay. Importers are Modal 14 and Sheet 3. Dialog.tsx:12-13 keeps the aliases only to spare consumers a rename; no contract or plan decision requires them. Two cautions. Each Sheet site must also pass ariaLabel. Modal's required `title: string` is a compile-time guard against an unnamed dialog that plain Dialog (title optional) lacks, so consider a title|ariaLabel union on Dialog. Net: 84 lines deleted, about 12 added at the 3 Sheet sites.
- **Proposal:** Change the 14 Modal sites to Dialog (import line and tag name, 0 net lines). Give the 3 Sheet sites (app/results/artifacts, app/work/missions, ComposerNodeRunDetail) placement="sheet" closeLabel="Close panel" dismissOnOverlay. Delete Modal.tsx and Sheet.tsx, and point the 5 suites at Dialog with those props.

#### components-06 · MissionCreateForm and TemplateEditorModal repeat the working-directories and references editors

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -60, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/missions/MissionCreateForm.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/components/missions/LocalDirRow.tsx`
- **Evidence:** MissionCreateForm.tsx:590-622 (dirs), :624-676 (references), :416-424 and :435-440 (helpers; :407-415 and :426-434 are comments). TemplateEditorModal.tsx:288-322 (dirs, <label> at :289 with '(optional)' span), :323-368 (references; placeholder 'URL or file path…' at :359), :171-175 (addReference). Both use LocalDirRow, Card variant="raised", IconButton label `Remove reference ${ref}`, Input aria-label "Reference to add".
- **Sceptic:** The duplication is real, but the two copies differ in more than copy. Labels: ComposerFieldLabel vs a <label> with an '(optional)' span. Keys: a `tmpl-` prefix. Wrappers: `flex items-center gap-2` vs `flex gap-2`, and a Button with vs without shrink-0. Setters: setFormField(key,v) vs updater functions. Of MissionCreateForm's helpers (:407-440), 28 lines are narrating comments that a comment pass removes anyway. About 178 duplicated code lines become about 95 in two components plus about 35 of adapters.
- **Proposal:** Add LocalDirsEditor({dirs,onChange,draft,onDraftChange,label}) and ReferencesEditor({refs,onChange,input,onInputChange,label,placeholder}) to components/missions, with labels and placeholders as props so no copy changes, and adapt each consumer in 2-3 lines. Two consumers normally do not pay for a new component (39 new lines to remove 48 in C8), but here each block is about 85 lines.

#### components-07 · Skills row handler bundle passed through four files: 88 of the census's repeated src window lines

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -55, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/components/skills/SkillsSections.tsx`, `src/components/skills/SkillsSearchResults.tsx`, `src/components/skills/SkillCategoryList.tsx`, `src/components/skills/SkillRowList.tsx`

The six-field bundle appears in 12 six-line blocks across the four skills files (SkillsSections 4, SkillsSearchResults 3, SkillCategoryList 3, SkillRowList 2), and app/agent/skills/page.tsx passes it twice. Census byFile gives 30+23+19+16 = 88 of the 1,000 repeated lines. With four consumers a shared type pays for itself. #### components-11 · ui/Input.tsx holds six controls beside ui/field/Input.tsx; its labelled wrappers re-implement Field without the label link

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, -45, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/ui/Input.tsx`, `src/components/ui/field/Input.tsx`, `src/components/ui/field/Field.tsx`, `src/components/config/ConfigField.tsx`, `src/components/memory/MemoryProviderSettings.tsx`
- **Evidence:** ui/Input.tsx:101 and :196 are `<label className="text-body font-medium text-ps-text-secondary">` with no htmlFor; :365-372 Select wrapper passes `ariaLabel={label}`. `rg -n "from \"@/components/ui/Input\"" src` = 12 files (SearchInput 6, InlineToggle 4, ConfigField imports Toggle, Select, NumberInput, TextInput; MemoryProviderSettings imports Select). Tests importing it: b6-config-field-unset-and-range.test.tsx:40, b7-memory-empty-states.test.tsx:31, field-kit.test.tsx:11. Field label style is text-micro uppercase (Field.tsx:68).
- **Sceptic:** Real, with three corrections. The ui/Input.tsx Select wrapper is named (ariaLabel={label} at :368). The unnamed controls are TextInput (no aria-label, :105-112) and NumberInput (:200-210), so ConfigField's numeric fields are unnamed too, not just its text fields. field-kit.test.tsx imports TextInput/NumberInput (:11) and does not read Input.tsx as source text. Importers: 12 files. Net: TextInput (36 lines) and the Select wrapper (33) go, and Field wrappers at 3-4 call sites add about 24, giving about -45.
- **Proposal:** Inline TextInput and the Select wrapper as <Field label hint><Input/Select/></Field> in ConfigField and MemoryProviderSettings. Move NumberInput, which has real null/clamp behaviour, and SearchInput into ui/field. Move the toggles per components-12. Delete ui/Input.tsx. Three suites to repoint: b6-config-field-unset-and-range, b7-memory-empty-states, and field-kit.test.tsx, which reads Input.tsx as source text.
- **Operator decision:** Visible change: Field's label is text-micro uppercase (Field.tsx:68) where Input.tsx's is text-body font-medium, so Settings and Memory provider labels restyle.

#### components-17 · MissionCreateForm carries pure dispatch logic; TemplateEditorModal takes 44 props where the form takes formState plus one setter

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, medium, -45, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/missions/MissionCreateForm.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/lib/missions/mission-submit-requirement.ts`
- **Evidence:** TemplateEditorModal.tsx:74-124 (interface, 43 members) and :126-170 (destructure). MissionCreateForm.tsx:67-71 formState/setFormField, :113-121 comment about disabled submits above :122 DISPATCH_MODES, :274 `export function MissionComposerActions`. `rg -n useState src/hooks/useMissionTemplatesState.ts` gives :44-67. tests/unit/mission-composer-actions.test.tsx:12 imports dispatchSubmitLabel.
- **Sceptic:** Real, with wrong counts. TemplateEditorModal declares 43 props, not 44. Only 25 of them mirror mission-form fields (about 12 value/setter pairs plus onModelChange); the other 18 are template and modal props that stay. The template draft is 19 separate useState calls in useMissionTemplatesState.ts:44-67, so a formState/setFormField interface also means rewriting that hook, which is hooks scope. Moving :122-206 to lib is a move with zero net lines. In-scope net: the interface and destructure shrink by about 45.
- **Proposal:** Move lines 122-206 to src/lib/missions (e.g. mission-dispatch-label.ts). The tests mission-composer-actions and mission-dispatch-gate import dispatchSubmitLabel, so update their imports. Give TemplateEditorModal the same MissionFormState type and a setFormField setter on its own instance: b10-template-editor-has-its-own-draft pins the separate draft, so keep a separate instance. Move the misplaced comment.

#### components-03 · 28 labels point at nothing; ModelEditor re-implements Field without the label link; 8 AutoTextareas are named by placeholder

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** accessibility, low, -40, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/models/ModelEditor.tsx`, `src/components/missions/MissionComposerLayout.tsx`, `src/components/missions/MissionCreateForm.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/components/ui/AutoTextarea.tsx`, `src/components/schedule/CustomScheduleBuilder.tsx`, `src/components/schedule/SchedulePicker.tsx`, `src/components/config/ConfigField.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Label scan (scratchpad labels.mjs: /<label\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/label>/ with no htmlFor and no control tag in the body) gives {total:45, orphan:27}: ConfigField:68,86; LogFilePicker:46; CategoryCombobox:103; CategoryManagerModal:103; MissionComposerLayout:8; TemplateEditorModal:213,225,238,250,262,289,324,371,388; BulkAuxiliaryUpdater:89; FallbackConfigPanel:68,90; ModelEditor:136; ModelsAgentDefaultSection:73; CustomScheduleBuilder:74,86,101; SchedulePicker:308; ui/Input.tsx:101,196,365. AutoTextarea.tsx:64 `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? placeholder ?? undefined}`; its props (:20-35) have no id. `rg -n "<AutoTextarea" -A8 src/components` shows no ariaLabel at MissionCreateForm:517,528,700,711,722 or TemplateEditorModal:241,253,265. ModelEditor `<Input` at :292,314,332,347,376,388 has no aria-label or id.
- **Sceptic:** The defect is real, but the counts and the scope need correcting, and the change is visible. A label scan finds 27 of 45 <label> elements in src/components with neither htmlFor nor a wrapped control. One site in the reviewer's evidence (ui/Input.tsx:365) is named anyway through ariaLabel={label} (:368). The 8 AutoTextarea sites and the 6 ModelEditor Inputs are confirmed unnamed. Replacing FieldRow (text-body font-medium) and ComposerFieldLabel (text-micro mono) with Field (text-micro uppercase tracking-wider, Field.tsx:68) restyles the labels in ModelEditor, the composer and the template editor. That is the same visible change the reviewer flagged for components-11 but left unflagged here. Net: FieldRow -26, TemplateEditorModal's 9 stanzas about -2 each, AutoTextarea id forwarding +3, giving about -40.
- **Proposal:** Make AutoTextarea forward id and aria-describedby. Widen Field's label to ReactNode for the '(optional)' markers. Replace FieldRow (ModelEditor) and ComposerFieldLabel (MissionComposerLayout) with Field, and wrap the TemplateEditorModal and CustomScheduleBuilder stanzas in Field. Give SchedulePicker's label htmlFor={id}. Render ConfigField's two read-only captions as <p>, since there is no control.

#### components-14 · Dead accent props, an all-empty focus map, and three lint pragmas whose stated reasons are false

- **Files:** `src/components/ui/Input.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Select.tsx`, `src/lib/ui/theme.ts`, `src/components/models/ModelSelectDropdown.tsx`, `src/components/config/ConfigField.tsx`, `src/components/models/DefaultsGrid.tsx`
- **Evidence:** theme.ts:205-214 (all ""), :229-230 (pragma; line 230 has no outline-none), :244-246. ui/Input.tsx:48-54 and :72-73. ui/Select.tsx:48 and :59-60. accentColor sites: app/agent/settings/page.tsx:206, app/agent/skills/page.tsx:365, app/results/sessions/[id]/page.tsx:278, HindsightBrowser.tsx:90, SessionFilterBar.tsx:54 (SearchInput); story-weaver/create/page.tsx:667,681,696, ChatModelSelector.tsx:55, CharacterCard.tsx:71 (InlineSelect). ConfigField.tsx:144 `color=`. ModelSelectDropdown.tsx:29-31 tone, passed at DefaultsGrid.tsx:140. inputFieldClasses("cyan") at app/work/chat/page.tsx:424, AutoTextarea.tsx:70, HelpSearch.tsx:61.

The three pragmas sit on lines with no outline-none and cite a focus border that does not exist; design-lint has no stale-pragma check, so they are never reported. There are 5 SearchInput and 5 InlineSelect accentColor call sites, plus ConfigField:144 color (the :117 color feeds Toggle, which does use it) and DefaultsGrid:140 tone. inputFieldClasses(accent) is also a dead parameter at 3 callers.

#### components-12 · Three switches: the field-kit pill Toggle has one consumer and looks unlike InlineToggle

#### components-30 · Two markdown renderers: SimpleMarkdown (JSX) for skills and renderMarkdown (HTML string) for chat

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -25, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/components/skills/SimpleMarkdown.tsx`, `src/components/chat/MessageBubble.tsx`, `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`

- **Sceptic:** The evidence reproduces. SimpleMarkdown (363 lines) has 1 importer. renderMarkdown escapes the text and substitutes 5 constructs into an HTML string (chat-utils.ts:94-109), injected through dangerouslySetInnerHTML under a pragma (chat/MessageBubble.tsx:111-116). Copy uses document click delegation (useChatPage.ts:108-117). It is correctly framed as an evaluation with an operator decision and low confidence. It would also remove one raw-HTML sink.
- **Proposal:** Evaluate rendering chat replies through SimpleMarkdown, with a CodeBlock component that owns its copy button. That removes one dangerouslySetInnerHTML site, the escape string and the document click delegation. Only proceed if chat rendering is meant to gain lists and tables.
- **Operator decision:** Changes how chat replies render: lists, tables and headings would start rendering in chat, and the copy-button mechanism changes.

#### components-04 · Hand-rolled menus and expand/collapse toggles skip what useDismissable, Picker and CollapsibleSection already do

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** accessibility, medium, -15, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/schedule/SchedulePicker.tsx`, `src/components/models/FallbackChainList.tsx`, `src/components/layout/BranchDropdown.tsx`, `src/components/help/ConceptHint.tsx`, `src/components/dashboard/DispatchStrip.tsx`, `src/components/dashboard/ProcessesPanel.tsx`, `src/components/missions/MissionCreateForm.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CategoryAccordion.tsx`, `src/components/missions/MissionEditorPanel.tsx`
- **Evidence:** `rg -n 'addEventListener\("mousedown"' src` = SchedulePicker.tsx:145, ui/field/Select.tsx:47, help/ConceptHint.tsx:55, layout/BranchDropdown.tsx:41. DispatchStrip.tsx:67-92: `<button onClick={toggle}` (no type) containing `<LinkButton href="/work/missions" ...>`. ProcessesPanel.tsx:39-42 `<RefreshCw ... onClick={onRefresh} />`. CategoryAccordion.tsx:41-44 `<button onClick=...` (no type, no aria-expanded). MissionCreateForm.tsx:551-564 dispatch buttons (no aria-pressed); `rg -l "ui/SegmentedControl\"" src | wc -l` = 8. TemplateEditorModal.tsx:376-383 IconButton variant is the only selection cue; colour swatches :393-413 are named by title only.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. There are 4 document mousedown listeners (SchedulePicker:145, field/Select:47, ConceptHint:55, BranchDropdown:41), even though useDismissable.ts:1-4 says it replaced ten. None of the 13 listed toggles carries aria-expanded. CategoryAccordion's button has no type=. DispatchStrip nests a LinkButton in a <button> that has no type=. ProcessesPanel puts onClick on a <RefreshCw> svg. The dispatch-mode buttons and the template icon and colour pickers expose no selected state. The net is overstated: folding SchedulePicker's two menus is the same deletion components-16 already counts. Once that is taken, the remainder is about +14 lines of aria-expanded on multi-line tags against about -25 from moving BranchDropdown and ConceptHint onto useDismissable.

FallbackChainList registry menu: Picker, or useDismissable plus aria-expanded. Move BranchDropdown and ConceptHint to useDismissable. Add aria-expanded to the 14 toggles. DispatchStrip: move 'full control' beside the toggle, as CollapsibleSection does. ProcessesPanel: IconButton. Dispatch modes: SegmentedControl. Template pickers: aria-pressed.

ModelEditor.tsx:37 `export type ModelEditorRecord = ModelRow;` is used in ModelEditor.tsx:41,74, models/types.ts:6,53 (toModelEditorRecord), ModelsTableSection.tsx:15,29 and hooks/useModelActions.ts:24.

The 10 sampled names are referenced only in their own file, but dropping `export` saves 0 lines and contradicts no failing check, so it is not worth a batch. #### components-01 · The raw-control lint cannot see an opening tag that ends its line; 83 raw controls in components are invisible to it

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `src/components/memory/hindsight/Modals.tsx`, `src/components/schedule/SchedulePicker.tsx`, `src/components/models/CredentialsPanel.tsx`, `src/components/session/SessionFilterBar.tsx`, `src/components/schedule/CustomScheduleBuilder.tsx`
- **Evidence:** design-lint.mjs:436 `pattern: /<(?:button|input|select|textarea)(?=[\s/>])/`, with files() excluding src/components/ui/ and src/kit/ (:431-435); lines split at :652. `rg -n "<(button|input|select|textarea)\s*$" src/components -g '*.tsx' -g '!src/components/ui/**' | wc -l` = 83 (`rg -l` = 33 files). The same pattern over src/components src/app src/modules = 127, of which src/components/ui + src/kit = 22, so rule-visible = 105. `node scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs --report` prints `no-raw-control-outside-ui  (0 in 0 files)`. Buttons without type= hidden by the same gap include DispatchStrip.tsx:67, CategoryAccordion.tsx:41 (inside ui, so exempt anyway), RowActionButtons.tsx:23, ActiveMissionsPanel.tsx:82 and session/MessageBubble.tsx:111,152.
- **Sceptic:** The blind spot is real. The rule is a per-line regex whose lookahead needs a character after the tag name, and the file is split on /\r?\n/. The 83 hits in 33 component files reproduce. The 127 figure includes 22 hits in src/components/ui and src/kit, which the rule's files() excludes, so the gate would expose 105 violations, not 127.
- **Proposal:** Change the lookahead to (?=[\s/>]|$), or test the joined tag. Ratchet the 127 as a baseline, then burn them down through components-02/-10/-21/-31. The rule lives in scripts/tooling (tooling reviewer's scope); every raw control it would expose here lives in this dimension.
- **Operator decision:** Fixing the pattern turns a reported 0 into 127 violations (83 in src/components). Choose between ratcheting a baseline and burning them down before the fix merges, since the gate is enforced by exit code.

#### components-29 · The one-importer census counts barrel files as importers; only three of the 103 are wrappers with nothing of their own

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`, `src/components/dashboard/StatusBadge.tsx`, `src/components/ui/TemplateCard.tsx`, `src/components/ui/field/Toggle.tsx`

StatusBadge.tsx:9-17 imports lucide icons and missionStatusLabel; `rg -n icon src/components/ui/Badge.tsx` has no hits. Census oneImporter.count = 103.

TemplateCard and field/Toggle are already in components-15 and -12. StatusBadge is not a wrapper that 'says nothing': it carries icons and the shared status-label vocabulary, and ui/Badge has no icon prop. The '95-98' projection was not computed. - **Proposal:** Tooling reviewer: resolve membership through barrels, so field/Input and Field stop counting (-2). Fold the three wrappers; StatusBadge goes onto Badge if Badge carries an icon (not verified). Expected count about 95-98, which is at or near the missed 95 target without folding any page section.
