- **Evidence:** Scratchpad imports.mjs: for each tests/ or scripts/ file, count targets from `git ls-files src/components src/kit src/types` whose path or `"src", "components", ...` segment spelling appears. Result: textrefs 82, fromFiles 46. Top targets: layout/Sidebar.tsx 4, layout/PageHeader.tsx 3, viz/RadialActivityClock.tsx 3, composer/WorkflowCanvas.tsx 2, memory/hindsight/utils.ts 2. c3-one-way-to-write.test.ts:72-77 pins the hindsight hooks.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived with my own scan: 82 references from 46 test and script files to files in src/components, src/kit and src/types, matching either the repo path or its join() segments. This agrees with the reviewer's 81/46. The planted fake paths in design-lint tests (e.g. src/components/Planted.tsx) are not real targets and are excluded by the scan.
- **Proposal:** Before the moves and renames in components-02/05/11/12/18/19/24/25, list the suites that pin each target file and, in the same batch, convert them to behavioural assertions or path-agnostic imports. The census baseline makes the line cost visible.

#### components-18 · WorkflowCanvas mixes an editor state machine, three write sequences and a duplicated node type with rendering

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, medium, 10, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/components/composer/WorkflowCanvas.tsx`, `src/lib/composer/canvas-graph.ts`
- **Evidence:** WorkflowCanvas.tsx:72-79, :240 `isTerminal: kind === "custom" ? false : false`, :528, :544, :559, :575. canvas-graph.ts:20-28, schema.ts:115. The census --report srcDup.top[0] is the label/kind/gate/isStart/isTerminal/config window x3.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. There are three runWrite flows (:327, :365, :405), the constant ternary at :240, the plural ternary 4 times, and census top window 'x3: label: string; | kind: string; | gate: NodeGate; ...'. One detail: WorkflowNodeData must extend Record<string, unknown> for react-flow (:72), and canvas-graph.ts:20-28 declares the shape inline rather than as a named export, so sharing it needs a small exported alias.
- **Proposal:** Import the node-data type from canvas-graph. Extract a useWorkflowEditor hook (state, mutations, writes) and WorkflowInspector / WorkflowNode components. Replace the ternary with `false` and the plurals with pluralise. b2-confirm-button.test and viz-chrome-tokens.test read this file's source text, so check them before splitting.

#### components-20 · Pure logic and write sequences inside components: AutomationList, Sidebar, ModelEditor, MemoryProviderSettings

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, 10, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/components/automation/AutomationList.tsx`, `src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`, `src/components/models/ModelEditor.tsx`, `src/components/memory/MemoryProviderSettings.tsx`
- **Evidence:** useModelActions.ts:142, :171, :203, :219 (the /api/models and /api/credentials URLs). ModelEditor.tsx:186-189 `update("credentialLabel", ...)` vs :203-204 `form.credentialLabel.trim() || `${form.provider} key``. MemoryProviderSettings.tsx:116-132 is the copy-into-state effect.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. AutomationList.tsx:94-136 is a pure row merge. Sidebar.tsx:155-168 is an inline useMutation over safeApiCall. ModelEditor.tsx:186-189 sets credentialLabel state that the request never reads, because :203-204 recomputes the same fallback from the closed-over form. useModelActions already posts to /api/credentials and /api/models. MemoryProviderSettings has two runWrite flows (:147, :171).
- **Proposal:** Move the row merge to src/lib/schedule (pure, unit-testable). Move the preference write to a hook in src/hooks. Move ModelEditor's two-write sequence into hooks/useModelActions.ts, which already owns the model writes, and delete the no-op label update. Leave rendering in the components. Sidebar's source text is read by u4/u6/u7/u14 suites.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- Real bug, not only duplication: TemplateEditorModal offers the 'Bot' icon (TemplateEditorModal.tsx:47, :70), but TemplateCard's iconMap has no Bot (`rg -n Bot src/components/ui/TemplateCard.tsx` = 0), so a template saved with Bot renders as the Zap fallback (TemplateCard.tsx:45) in DispatchStrip and MissionsList pills.
- Two model pickers on two different select implementations: missions/ModelPicker.tsx wraps the native InlineSelect (:5, :106) and is imported by AgentRuntimeDefaultsCard; models/ModelSelectDropdown.tsx wraps the custom field Select and is imported by DefaultsGrid and BulkAuxiliaryUpdater. Choosing a model looks and behaves differently on Missions and on Models. components-02/-13 touch both files without naming this.
- Card forwards no rest props (CardProps at Card.tsx:10-45 carries only id, data-testid and as), while Panel sites pass role="alert" (app/help/[[...slug]]/page.tsx:95, config/ConfigYamlErrorAlert.tsx:35). This prerequisite for components-08 is also why authors keep reaching for Panel over Card.
- NumberInput in ui/Input.tsx renders an unnamed spinbutton: its <label> (:196) has no htmlFor and its FieldInput (:200-210) has no aria-label, so every numeric setting ConfigField renders through it has no accessible name.
- design-lint has no stale-pragma check (`rg -n "unused|stale" scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs` finds no such rule). A disable comment on a line the rule would never flag stays silent, which is how the three false no-bare-outline-none pragmas survived (ui/Input.tsx:72, ui/Select.tsx:59, lib/ui/theme.ts:229). Tooling scope.
- inputFieldClasses(accent) keeps a dead accent parameter at 3 call sites (app/work/chat/page.tsx:424, ui/AutoTextarea.tsx:70, help/HelpSearch.tsx:61), because focusColorMap is all empty strings. It extends components-14 beyond the AutoTextarea caller the reviewer named.
- Folding Modal into Dialog (components-05) removes a compile-time guard: Modal requires `title: string` (Modal.tsx:17), while Dialog's title and ariaLabel are both optional (Dialog.tsx:36-50), so a bare <Dialog> can render unnamed. Make title|ariaLabel a required union in the same batch.
- achievements/index.ts is a third barrel (3 re-exports) beside TemplateModals.tsx and viz/index.ts. components-25 leaves it out, although components-29 counts its three components as barrel artefacts.

### Not examined

- No rendering, Playwright run or design census. Visual-equivalence claims for the proposed folds (components-02, -08, -11, -12, -21, -31) are read from class strings, not pixels.
- Did not run jest. Test impact comes from import and source-text scans (importers.json textRefs), not from executing suites.
- About 45 of 204 files were read line by line. The rest were covered only by the scans (importers, labels, raw controls, toggles, plurals, comment paths, exports): PageHeader, SettingsSection, CategoryCombobox, CategoryManagerModal, ComposerRunForm, ComposerNodeRunDetail, WorkflowRunCanvas, ResearchReport, the Quest/Agent/Help/Log/Session/Script/Skill row and modal families, SegmentedControl, IconButton, LinkButton, Badge, EmptyState, Popover, SplitPane, button-chrome, the rest of DataList, the viz charts, motion/index.tsx, DeployControls, AchievementShowcase.
- Whether ui/Badge supports an icon slot was not checked; it decides the StatusBadge fold in components-29.
- The export scan is regex-based: namespace imports and exports re-exported through barrels may be misclassified (default re-exports through barrels were excluded).
- Twenty regex hits for <button> without type= include comment matches (Card.tsx:31, GlowSurface.tsx:9, PerRowDeleteButton.tsx:29, RowActionButtons.tsx:19); HindsightBrowser.tsx:126, SessionFilterBar.tsx:66/78, ErrorBoundary.tsx:50 and SegmentedControl.tsx:103 were not opened, nor was whether any sits inside a <form>.
- ConceptHint.tsx's hand-rolled listener was seen only by grep (line 55); its Escape handling was not read.
- The census's repeated-window lines were attributed only for the four skills files; other component contributions to srcRepeatedWindowLines were not traced window by window.
- src/kit/BloomField.tsx is a vendored, hash-pinned copy (src/kit/PROVENANCE.md, tests/unit/kit-provenance.test.ts); it was examined for scope only and no change is proposed.
- scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs and line-census.mjs are outside this scope; components-01 and components-29 describe their effect on components and leave the fix to the tooling reviewer.
- CreateProfileModal and EditProfileModal (101/106 lines) were compared and not reported: two consumers, different state ownership, and a shared form would not pay for its interface.

## Dimension: app

src/app holds 16,928 TS/TSX lines: 99 API route files (7,550 lines, 169 handlers), 27 pages (9,071 lines) and 5 layout, error and not-found files (307 lines). C1 holds in part: 112 of 169 handlers export through route() and the census reads 13 own-try files. But that measure counts only serverErrorFromCatch, so six plain log-and-500 catches in sync, update and mission-categories escape it. Seven of the thirteen keep their try only because the wrapper cannot see a query, body or path value for the log line. Seven routes (322 lines) have no caller anywhere in src, scripts or e2e, though api.md documents all seven; U15's gate counts every route.ts as an entrypoint, so it cannot see them. C3 and C6's one way to read and write is incomplete: the two Story Weaver pages make 10 bare fetch calls that both design-lint rules miss, because the rules match only three helper names, and the reader shows 'not found' when a load fails. The pages carry the most debt. Profiles and the story reader each spend 391 and 402 lines on state and handlers before any JSX, and the story create page's inner component spans 643 of 744 lines. The missions page forwards 45 renamed props that its hook re-exports one at a time, which is the census's largest src window pair (36 lines each). Loading, error and not-found are drawn four ways. Route-level read-only checks sit on 17 write sites the proxy has already refused, while 69 other write handlers have none. 52 temporary redirects wait for a v1.0.0 that has not shipped (package.json 0.1.0, no git tags). Net reduction in reach is about 590 lines without operator rulings, and about 910 with the route removals and the read-only rule; part of that lands in src/hooks, src/components and src/lib rather than src/app.

### Measurements

- **src/app TS/TSX lines**: 16,928. Method: `git ls-files 'src/app/*.ts' 'src/app/*.tsx' 'src/app/**/*.ts' 'src/app/**/*.tsx' | sort -u | xargs cat | wc -l`
- **API route files and lines**: 99 files, 7,550 lines. Method: `git ls-files 'src/app/api/**/route.ts' | wc -l ; | xargs cat | wc -l`
- **Pages**: 27 page.tsx, 9,071 lines. Method: `git ls-files 'src/app/**/page.tsx' src/app/page.tsx | xargs cat | wc -l`
- **Route handlers by method**: 169 (GET 83, POST 48, PUT 19, DELETE 16, PATCH 3). Method: `grep -hoE 'export (const|async function|function) (GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS)\b' over route files | sort | uniq -c`
- **Handlers exported through route()**: 112 handlers in 76 files; 23 route files import no route(). Method: `grep -hoE 'export const (GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE) = route\(' | wc -l ; grep -L 'from "@/lib/api/api-route"'`
- **Census routesWithTryCatch**: 13 files, 14 sites. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, key routes (counts only files matching serverErrorFromCatch()`
- **Route files containing any try block**: 35. Method: `xargs grep -c 'try {' over route files | grep -v ':0$' | wc -l`
- **Response envelope usage**: 31 direct NextResponse.json( sites in 15 files; ok( 147, created( 13, badRequest( 90. Method: `grep -o counts over route files`
- **Body parsing helpers**: parseJsonBody 24 files, parseAndValidateJsonBody 24, parseOptionalJsonBody 3, raw request.json().catch 3. Method: `grep -l per helper over route files; grep -n 'request\.json()'`
- **Validation spellings**: api-schemas.ts imported by 6 route files; zod imported directly by 14; 12 typeof body checks in 6 files. Method: `grep -ln 'api-schemas' / 'from "zod"' / grep -oE 'typeof \(?(body|bodyResult)'`
- **Routes with no caller in src, scripts or tests/e2e**: 7 routes, 322 lines. Method: `scratchpad orphans2.mjs (per-route regex over non-comment src lines, template holes and sibling static segments handled) then git grep over src, scripts, tests/e2e, docs; wc -l of the 7 files`
- **Composer feature-flag guard repeats**: 12 identical guards and message literals. Method: `grep -c 'isFeatureEnabled("composer")' and 'Composer is not enabled. Set PS_COMPOSER=1' over route files`
- **Route context type declarations**: 23 (19 interface Ctx, 4 type Ctx) plus 32 inline params: Promise<{ id: string }>. Method: `grep -hA2 '^interface Ctx\|^type Ctx' | uniq -c ; grep -ohE 'params: Promise<\{[^}]*\}>' | uniq -c`
- **Route-level read-only checks on write handlers**: 17 sites (requireNotReadOnly in 9 files; isReadOnly() at 8 write sites) against 86 write handlers. Method: `grep -c 'requireNotReadOnly(' and 'isReadOnly()' per route file, read in context; grep -hoE 'export (const|async function|function) (POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE)' | wc -l`
- **405 stubs**: 11 stubs in 9 files, 44 lines. Method: `grep -n 'methodNotAllowed(' ; awk over handler spans containing methodNotAllowed`
- **Page prelude lines before first return (AST)**: profiles 391/592, story reader 402/506, story create inner component 643/744, skills 236/418, tools 186/495, sessions 158/307, dashboard 149/434, scripts 148/357, composer 147/539, logs 140/377, restore 138/528. Method: `scratchpad pageshape.mjs (TypeScript AST: default export start to its first return or if statement)`
- **Loading and error idioms on pages**: PageLoading 9 pages, LoadingSpinner 8, raw Loader2 2, Skeleton 1; LoadErrorBanner 20; EmptyState 5; AppPageShell rendered more than once in 4 pages. Method: `grep -l per primitive over page.tsx files; grep -c '<AppPageShell'`
- **Bare fetch calls in pages**: 10 (9 in story-weaver/[id], 1 in story-weaver/create). Method: `grep -nE '\bfetch\(' over page.tsx files`
- **toastElement renders on pages**: 18 renders in 17 pages. Method: `grep -l 'toastElement' ; grep -o '{toastElement}'`
- **Temporary redirects**: 52 (22 literal, 27 settings sections, 3 Story Weaver); package.json 0.1.0; 0 git tags. Method: `read next.config.ts redirects(); grep '"version"' package.json; git tag --list`
- **Prerendered routes in the last build**: 2 (/_global-error, /favicon.ico). Method: `.next/prerender-manifest.json routes keys, file dated 2026-09-10 19:48`
- **Census repeated-window lines in src/app**: work/missions/page.tsx 36, agent/models/page.tsx 18, api/schedules/route.ts 15, api/schedules/[id]/route.ts 15. Method: `line-census --report srcDup.byFile filtered to src/app`
- **Suites naming a large page file by path**: models 8, tools 7, skills 7, create 6, profiles 6, composer 6, reader 5, chat 5, dashboard 5, restore 3, missions 2. Method: `grep -rlE '<page path>' tests/unit tests/e2e | wc -l`
- **Unit suites setting PS_READ_ONLY and importing src/app/api**: 20. Method: `grep -rlE 'PS_READ_ONLY' tests/unit | xargs grep -lE 'app/api/' | wc -l`

### Findings that survived verification (24 of 24)

#### app-06 · Route-level read-only checks guard 17 write sites the proxy has already refused, and skip the other 69

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** security, medium, -45, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/proxy.ts`, `src/app/api/backup/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/cancel/route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/[id]/cancel/route.ts`, `src/app/api/prefs/route.ts`, `src/app/api/seed/route.ts`, `src/app/api/skills/[name]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/skills/[name]/toggle/route.ts`, `src/app/api/tools/route.ts`, `src/app/api/cron/hardware/route.ts`, `src/app/api/scripts/[name]/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** src/proxy.ts:238 `const readOnlyRefusal = !isSafe && isReadOnly();`. It is returned at :241 (auth none), :303 (bearer) and :323 (cookie), and the matcher at :329 is `/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)`. `git grep -l 'requireNotReadOnly(' -- src/app/api` gives 9 files. `git grep -n 'isReadOnly()' -- src/app/api` shows write guards at backfill-status:48, cron/hardware:37, :48 and :59, scripts/[name]:37 and :54, scripts/run:21, and sessions:94. Counting `git grep -cE '^export (async function|const) (POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE)\b'` gives 86. cron/hardware/route.ts:24-26 says 'No route in this directory carries either check' above the checks at :37, :48 and :59. sessions/route.ts:95 has no remedy, unlike read-only.ts:43-48. tools/route.ts:34-35 guards a methodNotAllowed. scripts/tooling/check-read-only-guards.mjs:3 reads 'A read-only guard may not sit in a GET, HEAD or OPTIONS handler.'
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. The proxy refuses unsafe methods under read-only on all three authentication paths, and its matcher covers every path, so these route checks cannot fire over HTTP. The counts are right: requireNotReadOnly in 9 files, isReadOnly() guarding writes at 8 sites, 86 write exports, 20 suites. The contradictions are real: the cron/hardware header, the sessions sentence without its remedy, and a guard in front of a 405. check-read-only-guards.mjs only forbids guards inside read handlers, so option (a) passes it. Moving the 20 suites onto proxy assertions will add test lines. The operatorDecision is correctly set.
- **Proposal:** The operator picks one rule for every write handler. (a) Proxy only: delete the 17 write-site checks and their comments, and move the suites that call handlers directly under PS_READ_ONLY to assert through proxy(). (b) Belt everywhere: add a route() option naming the resource, so no write handler has to remember the check. Either way, keep requireAuthenticatedHostWrites (7 sites), which the proxy's HOST_SIDE_EFFECT_PREFIXES comment names as deliberate, and correct the cron/hardware header.
- **Operator decision:** Security posture. (a) removes defence in depth for callers that bypass the proxy and changes the 503 body a direct handler call returns. (b) adds a check to about 69 write handlers. The operator chooses.

#### app-02 · Missions page forwards 45 renamed props that its hook re-exports one at a time

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -130, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/work/missions/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useMissionsPage.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionTemplatesState.ts`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/app/agent/models/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts`, `tests/unit/b10-template-editor-has-its-own-draft.test.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Line counts (wc -l): work/missions/page.tsx 292, useMissionsPage.ts 245, TemplateEditorModal.tsx 421. `grep -cE '^\s+\w+: templateState\.\w+,?$' src/hooks/useMissionsPage.ts` gives 44. page.tsx:245-289 passes the renamed props. The census report's srcDup.byFile lists work/missions/page.tsx 36 and hooks/useMissionTemplatesState.ts 36. The b10 suite reads result.current.templateState at lines 124, 153 and 181-183 and imports useMissionTemplatesState (line 40). `git grep -l useMissionsPage -- tests` lists 13 files, including u9-the-missions-board, u19-the-board-comes-first, record-surfaces and helpers/fixtures.ts. TemplateEditorModal has one importer: page.tsx, through the TemplateModals.tsx re-export.
- **Sceptic:** The duplication is real. Line 18 of the missions page begins the 45 renamed props, the hook re-exports 44 fields, and the census pair is 36/36. The test claim is wrong: the b10 suite runs useMissionTemplatesState through renderHook and reads result.current.templateState. It never renders TemplateEditorModal, so it would not have to move. The risk is elsewhere: 12 unit suites plus the e2e spec and tests/helpers/fixtures.ts use useMissionsPage and may read the pass-through names. The -150 also counts every destructure line as removable, so about -130 is more honest.
- **Proposal:** Give TemplateEditorModal a `draft` prop (the useMissionTemplatesState return, typed with ReturnType) plus `onSave`, and have it read draft.templateX and draft.setTemplateX. Then delete the 44 pass-through lines from useMissionsPage and the template names from the page's destructure. Apply the same change to the Models handler list if a single component consumes it. The b10 suite renders the editor and must move to the new props.

#### app-01 · Seven API routes have no caller, and no gate asks whether a route is called

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -74, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/api/admin/sessions/backfill-status/route.ts`, `src/app/api/agents/progression/route.ts`, `src/app/api/memory/route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/[id]/cancel/route.ts`, `src/app/api/runs/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/runs/reconcile/route.ts`, `docs/reference/api.md`, `tests/unit/u15-nothing-lives-only-for-its-test.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** Line counts (wc -l): backfill-status 93, agents/progression 51, memory 87, missions/[id] 22, missions/[id]/cancel 33, runs/[id] 19, runs/reconcile 17. Callers of runs/reconcile: `git grep -n 'runs/reconcile' -- tests/integration` finds composer-smoke.mjs:110 and :136, and full-stack-smoke.mjs:105 and :214. src/lib/orchestration/run-reconcile.ts:205 says 'POST /api/runs/reconcile is public and the smokes poll it every second'. So six uncalled routes, 305 lines. The three duplicate routes total 74 lines (22+33+19). src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/cancel.ts:25 and :33 name the REST cancel route in comments. The U15 entry regex is at tests/unit/u15-nothing-lives-only-for-its-test.test.ts:39-40. The plan's 'the rest have callers' is at org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md:305-306. Unit suites naming each route path (git grep -l over tests/unit): agents/progression 3, missions/[id]/cancel 4, missions/[id] 1, runs/reconcile 1, backfill-status 0, runs/[id] 0. That differs from the reviewer's counts.
- **Sceptic:** One of the seven routes does have callers. POST /api/runs/reconcile is called four times by the runtime smoke scripts, and its own library says the smokes poll it. The reviewer searched only src, scripts and tests/e2e, so it missed tests/integration. That leaves six uncalled routes, not seven. The other facts hold: the file sizes, the api.md rows, U15 counting every route.ts as an entrypoint, and the plan's 'the rest have callers'. The proposed gate must count tests/integration as a caller source. Every deletion still needs its own operator sign-off.
- **Proposal:** Put each route to the operator separately. Likely split: delete the three with no UI whose twin the UI uses (missions/[id], missions/[id]/cancel, runs/[id]). Keep the four with a human use (backfill-status, runs/reconcile, agents/progression, memory) and label them operator API in api.md. Add a census measure or unit gate: every route path has a caller in src or scripts, or sits on an allowlist that states its reason.
- **Operator decision:** Removes documented API routes and URLs (api.md lines 56, 66, 71, 72, 87, 89, 94). Each route needs its own sign-off, since an external script or curl habit may call it. Deleting only the three duplicates is -74 lines.

#### app-08 · Root layout wraps pages in a class error boundary that app/error.tsx already covers

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -65, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/app/layout.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx`, `src/app/error.tsx`
- **Evidence:** src/app/layout.tsx:146 has `<ErrorBoundary>{children}</ErrorBoundary>`. `git grep -l ErrorBoundary -- src tests` finds only src/app/layout.tsx and src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx (64 lines). ErrorBoundary.tsx:47 renders {this.state.message}, and :50-56 is a raw <button>. src/app/error.tsx:21-22 logs the error and does not display it. `ls src/app/global-error.tsx src/app/not-found.tsx` finds neither. .next/prerender-manifest.json routes are ['/_global-error','/favicon.ico']. docs/contributing/design-tokens.md:400 mentions ErrorBoundary.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. The root layout wraps children in the class ErrorBoundary. In the App Router, app/error.tsx already sits around the page inside that tree. ErrorBoundary has one importer, no test names it, and its label and className props are never passed. Its fallback prints error.message and uses a raw button, while error.tsx deliberately hides the message. There is no global-error.tsx and no root not-found.tsx. One more edit: docs/contributing/design-tokens.md:400 names ErrorBoundary as the layout's.
- **Proposal:** On the isolated instance, confirm that a page throw lands on error.tsx. Then delete ErrorBoundary.tsx and the wrapper. Separately, decide whether to add a styled global-error.tsx for a throw inside the layout's own providers (Sidebar, HelpProvider, FeedbackProvider).

#### app-07 · The route context type is declared 23 times

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -60, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-route.ts`, `src/app/api/schedules/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/workflows/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/runs/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/laboratory/research/[id]/events/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -n 'interface Ctx {' -- src/app/api | wc -l` gives 19 (18 with id, 1 with id and nodeId). `git grep -n 'type Ctx = ' -- src/app/api` gives 6: chat/[id]/approval, chat/[id]/messages/[messageId], chat/[id]/messages, chat/[id], chat/[id]/stop, scripts/[name]. `git grep -ohE 'params: Promise<\{[^}]*\}>' -- src/app/api | sort | uniq -c` gives 32 id, 4 name, 2 key, 1 id+messageId, 1 id+nodeId and 1 `path: string[]`, 41 in all. api-route.ts:28 has `type Params = Record<string, string>` (not exported).
- **Sceptic:** The counts are off. There are 25 Ctx declarations (19 interfaces and 6 one-line type aliases), not 23. The '32 inline annotations' figure includes the declarations themselves: there are 41 `params: Promise<...>` occurrences in all, so about 16 are inline. Inline annotations change text but save no lines. The catch-all skills/[...path] has `path: string[]`, which a Record<K, string> helper does not fit. Removing 19 three-line interfaces and 6 one-line aliases, minus the exported type, gives about -60.
- **Proposal:** Export `type RouteCtx<K extends string = 'id'> = { params: Promise<Record<K, string>> }` from api-route.ts, imported on the line that already imports route, so most files add no import line. Next 16's generated RouteContext<'/api/x/[id]'> is the alternative, but CI would need `next typegen` to run before tsc.

#### app-22 · Story Weaver's four vendored serif fonts await a keep-or-delete ruling

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, -58, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/layout.tsx`, `src/app/fonts/Literata.woff2`, `src/app/fonts/EBGaramond.woff2`, `src/app/fonts/Lora.woff2`, `src/app/fonts/Merriweather.woff2`
- **Evidence:** ls -l src/app/fonts: Literata 52,496, EBGaramond 44,336, Lora 37,788, Merriweather 97,548 (sum 232,168). src/app/recroom/story-weaver/layout.tsx is 52 lines, with the 'may be deleted' passage at :8-12. src/modules/rec-room/components/ReaderSettings.tsx:29 (`fontFamily: "EB Garamond"`) and :35-38 (the four font entries).
- **Sceptic:** The sizes and the open ruling re-derive: 232,168 bytes across the four fonts and a 52-line layout. The delete path also touches ReaderSettings.tsx, whose default fontFamily is 'EB Garamond' and whose font list holds the four faces. A reader's saved setting would need a fallback. Still an operator ruling.
- **Proposal:** Put the ruling to the operator. If delete: remove the four files, the layout, and the reader settings in src/modules/rec-room that choose among the fonts (not measured here). If keep: record the ruling and cut the 'may be deleted' paragraph.
- **Operator decision:** Deleting the fonts removes a reader feature: the choice of four serif typefaces.

#### app-05 · Seven of the thirteen own-try routes keep a try only for the wording of their log line

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, -35, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-route.ts`, `src/app/api/missions/route.ts`, `src/app/api/scripts/logs/route.ts`, `src/app/api/scripts/run/route.ts`, `src/app/api/models/sync/push/route.ts`, `src/app/api/agent/files/[key]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/sessions/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/models/fallbacks/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -l 'serverErrorFromCatch(' -- 'src/app/api/**/route.ts'` lists 13 files. Log-only catches: missions/route.ts:82 (`id ? `mission ${id}` : ...`), scripts/logs/route.ts:22 (name), scripts/run/route.ts:52 (name), models/sync/push/route.ts:68-73 (modelId), models/fallbacks/route.ts:136-141 (parsed.action), agent/files/[key]/route.ts:198-204 and :339-345 (resolved.path), sessions/[id]/route.ts:291-296 (sanitizedId). src/lib/api/api-route.ts:29 is `RouteText = string | ((params: Params) => string)`. Richer catches: backup :55-61 (audit line), composer/workflows/[id] :67-77 (409), credentials :55-63 and models :41-48 (rollback), models/defaults :103-106 (404), orchestration/chat (describeGatewayFailure then status). sessions/[id] has `try {` only at :51 and :230. `git grep -nE 'pushing model|reading session|fallback action|listing missions' -- tests` finds nothing.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. The census names 13 files. The seven named catches only log, and each uses a query, body or path value that route()'s params-only RouteText cannot reach. The six richer catches do what the reviewer says. No test pins the log contexts, and sessions/[id] has only two try blocks, so Step 2 sits outside both.
- **Proposal:** Pass the request to RouteText as a second argument, (params, request) => string, so missions GET and scripts/logs GET convert with their logs unchanged. For the body- or path-derived five, accept a log line naming the param or nothing, for example 'writing file ${p.key}'. Log text is not API contract. sessions/[id] converts with (p) => `reading session ${p.id}` and gains a JSON 500 for the Step-2 throw. Census goes from 13 to 6, each of which does more than log. tests/unit/b1-sync-routes-answer-with-the-outcome.test.ts describes POST /api/models/sync/push; read it for a pinned log context before converting that route.

Each site becomes `const problem = scheduleProblem(x); if (problem) return badRequest(problem);`. With four consumers it pays for its interface, unlike a two-consumer fold.

Two overclaims. First, error.tsx's `function Error` shadows only the global Error VALUE in the module; the props annotation `Error & { digest?: string }` resolves in the type namespace and is unaffected, so the rename is tidiness, not a bug. Second, api.md:72 never says 'first'. It says the mission stops via runtime.stopRun and that local state is finalised even if the backend fails. That is stale because it omits the background stop, not because it contradicts the route.
- **Proposal:** Correct each comment in a comment-only commit. Rename error.tsx's component RootError. Rewrite api.md's conventions paragraph around route(), runWrite and the response factories.

#### app-16 · instrumentation.ts wraps its optional boot steps in try, but not the steps whose throw skips crash recovery

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, low, -14, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/instrumentation.ts`
- **Evidence:** src/instrumentation.ts: try blocks at :20-37, :43-48, :52-58, :77-83 and :89-95. Unwrapped calls: ensureSyncLayer :61-62, ensureBackgroundScheduler :65-66, ensureCatalogSeededOnce :71-72. `git grep -l instrumentation -- tests/unit` finds boot-says-how-it-is-configured.test.ts and u15-nothing-lives-only-for-its-test.test.ts.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. Five of register()'s steps have their own try. The three ensure* calls at lines 61-72 have none. A throw from ensureCatalogSeededOnce would therefore skip both recovery sweeps at 77-95. The operatorDecision correctly covers changing a failed boot from a throw to a warning.
- **Proposal:** Add a local step(name, fn) that awaits the step, logs a failure and continues. Run the recovery sweeps before the catalog seed. The operator decides whether a failed scheduler or sync boot should stop the server or log and carry on.
- **Operator decision:** Wrapping ensureSyncLayer, ensureBackgroundScheduler and ensureCatalogSeededOnce turns a failed boot from an unhandled throw in register() into a logged warning. The operator chooses.

#### app-03 · Story Weaver pages make 10 bare fetch calls that neither lint rule can see, and a failed load reads as 'not found'

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, medium, -10, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`, `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `src/lib/api/api-write.ts`
- **Evidence:** `grep -n 'fetch(' src/app/recroom/story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx` hits lines 98, 115, 133, 162, 269, 301, 333, 374 and 408. create/page.tsx has one at :469. The swallowed catch is at [id]/page.tsx:126 (`} catch {} finally { setLoading(false); }`), and :434 renders ReaderNotFound. Failures go inline through setError at :173, :190, :276, :287, :317, :321, :348 and :352, rendered through `error={error}` at :443 and onDismissError at :446. In design-lint.mjs, rule no-raw-fetch-in-component starts at line 468 with its regex at :490, and rule no-raw-write-outside-the-helper starts at :500 with its regex at :522. Both match only apiFetch, safeApiCall and safeApiCallData. RunWriteOptions (src/lib/api/api-write.ts:36-75) has no signal field and reports through showToast. story-weaver/page.tsx:49-53 already reads /api/stories through useApiResource with a body.
- **Sceptic:** The ten bare fetch calls, the blind spot in both lint rules, and the swallowed load failure that shows ReaderNotFound are all real. Three corrections. The catch is at line 126, not 127. LoadErrorBanner is on 19 page files. Three of the ten calls (load, sync-titles and spend) are reads sent as POST, not writes. Moving the write calls onto runWrite changes behaviour. The reader shows failures inline through setError and an error prop with a dismiss control, while runWrite reports through showToast. runWrite also has no signal option, and Stop depends on the AbortController. Loading the story through useApiResource with LoadErrorBanner is the clear win. The write calls need a UX decision, or runWrite needs an error callback. Wrapping the calls saves few lines.
- **Proposal:** Load the story through useApiResource({ body: { action: 'load', storyId } }) and show LoadErrorBanner on failure. Route the eight action POSTs through runWrite, using its `request` option where a call needs the AbortController held in inFlightRef. Extend both lint rules to catch a bare `fetch(` in src/app, src/components and src/modules; streaming callers in src/lib are already outside the rules' file predicate.

Moving code into src/lib saves no lines in the src census; only the narration cut does. The source-text suites that name these routes by path make it a medium-risk move with close to zero net lines.

#### app-20 · Fast-mode chat builds its own gateway fetch beside the shared client

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -6, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/api/orchestration/chat/route.ts`, `src/lib/models/gateway-client.ts`
- **Evidence:** src/app/api/orchestration/chat/route.ts:21 has `async function fetchGateway(apiUrl, gatewayBody, isStreaming)`, building the bearer at :30-38. src/lib/models/gateway-client.ts:28-43 is the exported fetchGateway, where `const timeoutMs = init?.timeoutMs ?? 3000` and `signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs)`. Imported by gateway/health/route.ts, gateway/models/route.ts and src/hooks/useGatewayHealth.ts. chat/route.ts:94-95 has the 'third raw fetch' comment.
- **Sceptic:** Real, but mis-stated. The chat route declares its own local function also called fetchGateway, which is why a grep for fetchGateway matches the file. It is a same-named duplicate, not a caller of the library helper. The library helper always sets a 3-second AbortSignal.timeout, so it needs an opt-out before a streaming completion can use it. The saving is small: about 8 lines of header building go and about 2 are added to the library. Renaming the local function is a cheap fix on its own.
- **Proposal:** Let fetchGateway opt out of its timeout (timeoutMs: null meaning no signal, or pass the caller's signal through). Call it from the chat route with /v1/chat/completions, keeping the route's 503 mapping.

#### app-18 · Request bodies are parsed and validated three different ways

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, -5, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-schemas.ts`, `src/app/api/models/sync/push/route.ts`, `src/app/api/models/sync/pull/route.ts`, `src/app/api/prefs/route.ts`, `src/app/api/update/route.ts`, `src/app/api/admin/sessions/backfill-status/route.ts`, `src/app/api/mission-categories/route.ts`, `src/app/api/memory/hindsight/route.ts`, `src/app/api/agent/profiles/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** `git grep -l api-schemas -- 'src/app/api/**/route.ts' | wc -l` gives 6, and the same for zod imports gives 14. Schemas built inside the handler: models/sync/push/route.ts:18-23 and models/sync/pull/route.ts:21-26. `git grep -n 'request.json()' -- src/app/api` finds backfill-status:42, prefs:22 (`.catch(() => null)`), update:106 and a comment in templates:33. Files using each helper: parseJsonBody 23, parseAndValidateJsonBody 23, parseOptionalJsonBody 3. src/lib/api/parse-optional-json-body.ts:28-40 returns {} for a non-object. backfill-status/route.ts:41-45 is a try around a call that already has .catch.
- **Sceptic:** The counts mostly hold: api-schemas imported by 6 route files and zod by 14. The two schemas built per request are real, as are the three raw request.json().catch sites and backfill-status's try that can never fire. The parseJsonBody and parseAndValidateJsonBody helpers are each used in 23 files, not 24. Hoisting the schemas saves no lines. Swapping prefs onto parseOptionalJsonBody is not a like-for-like change: prefs catches to null, while the helper returns {} for any non-object. The honest saving without converting to zod is about -5, mostly the dead try in backfill-status.
- **Proposal:** Set the rule that a schema lives beside its domain and a route calls parseAndValidateJsonBody. Hoist the two in-handler schemas to module scope. Replace the three raw json-catch sites with parseOptionalJsonBody. Leave spend's hand validation alone: its header says the worded refusals are the point (spend/route.ts:7-10).
- **Operator decision:** Converting a hand-validated body to zod changes its documented 400 body from a sentence to 'Invalid request body' plus details. Sign off per route, or keep each sentence through the schema's custom messages.

#### app-15 · 52 old-URL redirects kept 'for one release' are waiting on a release that has not shipped

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, 1, adjusted
- **Files:** `next.config.ts`, `src/lib/config/config-sections.ts`, `src/lib/modules/registry.ts`, `tests/unit/b3-old-paths-redirect.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/b3-old-paths-redirect.spec.ts`, `tests/unit/config-section-redirect.test.ts`, `src/app/agent/settings/[section]/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/profiles/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** next.config.ts:55-58 has the 'for one release' comment. :69-108 is redirects(): 22 temporary() literals, `...settingsSectionIds().map(...)` at :98 and 3 Story Weaver entries. package.json:3 is `"version": "0.1.0"`, and `git tag --list | wc -l` gives 0. wc -l: tests/unit/b3-old-paths-redirect.test.ts 74, tests/e2e/b3-old-paths-redirect.spec.ts 52, tests/unit/config-section-redirect.test.ts 237. src/app/agent/settings/[section]/page.tsx:4-7 says 'the twenty-seven real slugs answer a 307 to their anchor from next.config.ts before this file is involved'. src/app/page.tsx:329 and src/components/layout/RailFooter.tsx:62 link /agent/settings/system, which is a page.
- **Sceptic:** The facts re-derive: 22 literal entries, 27 generated and 3 Story Weaver, 52 in all. package.json is 0.1.0 with no git tags, and the three suites are 74, 52 and 237 lines. The settings/[section] page's own header confirms that the 27 slugs answer 307 before it runs. The net figure mixes now and later. The 'now' step (collapsing the two-hop chain) adds lines. The deletion depends on a release and on the operator, and most of it lands in next.config.ts and tests, outside the src census. Two /agent/settings/<x> links do exist (system, restore), but those are pages, not section redirects, so the 'nothing links' claim holds for sections.
- **Proposal:** Now: collapse the two-hop chain by generating `/config/${id}` -> `/agent/settings#${id}` entries beside the existing ones. After v1.0.0 plus one release, on the operator's word: delete redirects() and its comment, the two redirect suites, the registry comment and the redirect reason in the profiles comment.
- **Operator decision:** Removes 52 old URLs: bookmarks to /orchestration/*, /laboratory/*, /operations/*, /config/*, /sessions, /logs, /insights, /memory and the 27 section paths. Only after v1.0.0 has shipped with the redirects in place.

#### app-09 · Loading, error and not-found are each drawn several ways

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 5, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/agent/skills/[...path]/page.tsx`, `src/app/results/sessions/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/app/work/missions/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/models/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/settings/[section]/page.tsx`, `src/app/error.tsx`, `src/app/help/[[...slug]]/not-found.tsx`, `src/lib/feature-flags-guard.ts`, `src/components/ui/PageLoading.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Over `git ls-files 'src/app/**/page.tsx' 'src/app/page.tsx'`: PageLoading 9 files, LoadingSpinner 8, Loader2 2 (agent/models, work/missions), Skeleton 3 (src/app/page.tsx, results/insights, work/composer). Per-file `grep -c '<AppPageShell'` gives profiles 2, help 2, sessions/[id] 3, missions 2. skills/[...path]/page.tsx:73-95 has two min-h-screen grid-bg frames, 'Skill Not Found' and a raw Link. PageLoading.tsx:7-8 records the whole-page spinner that was removed. The only not-found file is src/app/help/[[...slug]]/not-found.tsx. src/lib/feature-flags-guard.ts:16 calls notFound(). LoadErrorBanner appears in 19 page files.
- **Sceptic:** Mostly re-derived, with two counts corrected. Skeleton is imported directly by three pages (dashboard, insights, composer), not one. LoadErrorBanner is on 19 page files. The rest holds: PageLoading 9, LoadingSpinner 8, raw Loader2 2 (models and missions), and AppPageShell rendered more than once on profiles 2, help 2, sessions/[id] 3 and missions 2. The full-screen frames on skills detail, the four not-found shapes and the missing root not-found.tsx also hold.
- **Proposal:** Add one src/app/not-found.tsx using help's Card and LinkButton body. Put skills detail and session detail inside AppPageShell with their PageHeader, using PageLoading and LoadErrorBanner. Move missions and models onto PageLoading. Leave error.tsx as the one full-screen frame.

#### app-14 · The largest pages keep state and handlers inline where a page hook would hold them

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, 50, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/app/agent/profiles/page.tsx`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/skills/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/tools/page.tsx`, `src/app/results/sessions/page.tsx`, `src/app/work/scripts/page.tsx`, `src/app/work/composer/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/settings/restore/page.tsx`, `src/app/results/logs/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Measured with wc -l and grep -c: agent/profiles 592 lines, 16 useState, 13 handle*. story-weaver/[id] 506 lines, 24 useState, 14 `useCallback(`. story-weaver/create 744 lines, 28 useState; CreateStoryPage starts at line 102, spanning 643 lines. agent/skills 418, 15 useState. agent/tools 495, 9 useState. results/sessions 307, 12 useState. work/scripts 357, 11 useState. work/composer 539, 13 useState. settings/restore 528. results/logs 377. src/app/page.tsx 434. `ls src/hooks` shows useChatPage, useMissionsPage, useModelsPage and useDashboard.
- **Sceptic:** The page sizes and state counts mostly re-derive. The story reader has 14 useCallback calls, not 15. The AST 'lines before first return' measure was not re-derived. The existing page hooks are useChatPage, useMissionsPage, useModelsPage and useDashboard. The proposal's +50 lines runs against the programme's shrink-only census (npm run census:lines), so each extraction must be offset by cuts in the same batch or it fails the gate. It also breaks up to 7 source-text suites per page.
- **Proposal:** Extract a page hook wherever the handlers share one subject: useStoryReader (in-flight count, abort set, edit and continue drafts), useStoryDraft for create, useProfilesPage, useSkillsPage, useToolsetsEditor. Do not move the page-local subcomponents back out, since C6 folded them in deliberately. Move a page only where a behaviour test replaces each source-text suite the move breaks.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- Route-level ensureDb() is redundant. There are 46 calls in 30 route files (`git grep -c 'ensureDb();' -- 'src/app/api/**/route.ts'`). ensureDb() only sets a flag and calls getDb() (src/lib/db/index.ts:352-356), and getDb() opens and migrates on its first call (index.ts:91-103), so the first repository call already does the same work. No suite asserts it was called (`git grep -nE 'expect\(ensureDb' -- tests` finds nothing); 14 suites only mention it in mocks. Saving is about -46 lines, more where it is a file's only @/lib/db import. Check first that no route calls it before non-DB work only to make a migration error surface early.
- Thirteen route files export plain `export async function` handlers that are outside route() and have no try. A throw in them answers Next's bodiless 500, the failure T-0080 describes. The files: agent/profiles/[id], agent/profiles/[id]/toolsets, agent/root (2), chat/[id]/messages, composer/runs/[id]/events, cron/hardware (4), laboratory/research/[id]/events, laboratory/research/[id]/export, models/sync/pull, skills/[name]/toggle (2), stories, templates, tools (2). Found with `git grep -l -E '^export async function (GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE)' -- 'src/app/api/**/route.ts' | xargs grep -L 'try {'`. Some are deliberate (the SSE event streams, the 405 stubs, a constant GET). Several delegate to lib handlers (cron/hardware, stories, templates), whose own catches were not checked. Each needs a verdict: wrap it in route(), or record why not.
- The caller gate app-01 proposes must count tests/integration/runtime/*.mjs as callers. The smokes call POST /api/runs/reconcile (composer-smoke.mjs:110 and :136, full-stack-smoke.mjs:105 and :214). A gate that searches only src, scripts and e2e would have flagged a route that is in use.
- Every file in src/app starts with the ═══ rule-line banner: 224 such lines across 106 files (`git grep -c '═══' -- src/app`). No convention requiring them turned up in docs/contributing, org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md or scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs; org/CONSTITUTION.md was searched and nothing turned up. The same banner is repo-wide, and line-census.mjs itself opens with one. Removing them is a style call for the operator. It would be about -224 comment lines in src/app alone, as one comment-only commit.
- docs/contributing/design-tokens.md:400 says ErrorBoundary 'is the layout's and has one caller by nature'. Deleting it under app-08 needs a matching doc edit, which app-08's file list does not include.

### Not examined

- Pages read only through AST and grep metrics, not line by line: work/composer, work/chat, agent/settings/restore, the dashboard page.tsx, results/insights, results/logs, results/sessions (list), work/scripts, work/research, agent/skills and agent/tools. The handler lists quoted for them come from grep extracts, not a full read.
- Route files counted by grep but not read in full: config, credentials/[id], models/import, models/[id]/diff, monitor, logs, sessions (list), chat and chat/[id]/*, composer runs/*, laboratory/research/*, artifacts, skills toggle and [...path], agent/root, agent/profiles/[id] and sync/*, fs/*, analytics/timeseries, status, schedules/[id]/run.
- No build, Playwright or jest run, per the review rules. app-08 (the layout ErrorBoundary is redundant) and app-15 (the two-hop redirect chain) are reasoned from the code and Next's segment conventions, not observed on a running instance. app-23 relies on the .next build artefact from 2026-09-10.
- Orphan detection is literal and template-string search only. An endpoint assembled from a variable (a URL map, or a path fragment joined at runtime) would evade it, and external operator scripts or curl habits cannot be seen from the repo.
- src/app/globals.css (808 lines) and the favicon: UI-overhaul scope, not examined here.
- proxy.ts's inline 401 HTML page with hard-coded hex colours (#05080d, #0d1420, #eaf2f8) was not measured against the design tokens; it looks intentionally standalone.
- Not checked: which tests pin the 400 sentences that app-18's zod conversions would change, and which tests assert log contexts for the seven routes in app-05 (beyond b1-sync-routes-answer-with-the-outcome.test.ts).
- The Story Weaver reader settings that pick among the four serif fonts (src/modules/rec-room) sit outside this scope and were not measured for app-22.
- Scratchpad measurement scripts (orphans.mjs, orphans2.mjs, pageshape.mjs) live in the session scratchpad only; a re-run needs them recreated from the methods above.

## Dimension: hooks

The scope is 84 files and 9,593 lines across src/hooks, src/lib/api, src/lib/ui and src/lib/dashboard. Reads are mostly consolidated: about 75 useApiResource reads keyed on their endpoint. The exception is the missions board, which still reads four endpoints by hand on a 15s useInterval beside the dashboard's cached copies of three of them. All three read gates report 0 (census handRolledReadHooks, design-lint no-raw-fetch-in-component, the u15 test) because none of them can see a hook. Writes still go two sanctioned ways: runWrite at 57 sites in 30 files and useMutation at 7 sites in 4 files. Another 18 client writes bypass both, and no-raw-write-outside-the-helper reads 0 because it only matches apiFetch/safeApiCall and skips src/lib (10 raw fetch POSTs in Story Weaver, 1 in the deploy footer, 7 in chat-utils). The useToast single-slot fallback is dead in the product, since layout.tsx wraps every page in FeedbackProvider, yet {toastElement} still renders as null 21 times in 20 files; removing it costs test work, not src risk. The largest line savings are structural: the missions and models composition roots re-list 197 lines of slice fields, and two mission hooks hold the same 14-field draft (about -370 lines between them, medium to high risk). If all 25 findings land, the net is about -730 lines, most of it from hooks-01, hooks-07 and hooks-08.

### Measurements

- **Scope size**: 84 files, 9,593 lines. Method: `wc -l src/hooks/* src/lib/api/* src/lib/ui/* src/lib/dashboard/*`
- **useApiResource reads**: about 75 (72 matched, plus 3 with nested generics: useConfig, useToolsetCatalog, useMissionComposer.ts:362). Method: `rg -n 'useApiResource(<[^>]*>)?\(\s*["'']' src, then a manual check of multi-line generics`
- **runWrite call sites**: 57 in 30 files. Method: `rg -c 'runWrite\(|runWrite<' src gives 59 hits in 31 files; minus the 2 in src/lib/api/api-write.ts (doc example and declaration)`
- **useMutation call sites**: 7 in 4 files (useSchedules 4, useScripts 1, useOperatorPrefs 1, Sidebar 1). Method: `rg -n 'useMutation\(' src`
- **Client writes invisible to no-raw-write-outside-the-helper**: 18 (10 fetch POSTs to /api/stories, 1 fetch POST in useVersionFooter, 6 safeApiCall writes and 1 streaming POST in src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts). Method: `grep -rnE '(^|[^A-Za-z._])fetch\(' src/hooks src/components src/app src/modules, plus grep method: in src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`
- **design-lint data rules**: no-raw-write-outside-the-helper 0 in 0 files; no-raw-fetch-in-component 0 in 0 files. Method: `node scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs --report`
- **Census read/write counters vs hooks that read by hand in effects**: handRolledReadHooks 0 and writeHooksWithoutMutation 0; at least 3 hooks read by hand (useMissionsData, useChatSend, useVersionFooter). Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, then reading line-census.mjs:108-157 against the three hooks`
- **Poll cadences**: 19 refetchInterval literals in 11 files, plus 2 hand-rolled timers (useMissionsData 15s, useVersionFooter 2s); 1 named constant (SESSIONS_LIVE_POLL_MS). Method: `rg -n 'refetchInterval' src; rg -n 'useInterval\(|setInterval' src/hooks`
- **/api/stats observers**: 6, of which 2 are mounted on every page (FeedbackProvider, Sidebar). Method: `rg -nw useStats src`
- **useToast() calls / {toastElement} renders**: 28 calls / 21 renders in 20 files, all null under the shell. Method: `rg -n '\buseToast\(\)' src | wc -l; rg -n '\{toastElement\}' src`
- **Unit test files that may depend on the toast fallback (upper bound)**: at most 23. Method: `grep of tests for ByRole status|alert or ByTestId('toast') in files with no FeedbackProvider; LoadErrorBanner shares those roles, so the real count needs a branch run`
- **showToast function type declarations**: 16. Method: `rg -n 'type (ToastFn|ShowToastFn|ShowToast)\b' src`
- **Cross-file repeated 6-line windows in scope**: useMissionTemplatesState.ts 36, useMissionsData.ts 18; their pages: work/missions/page.tsx 36, agent/models/page.tsx 18. Method: `line-census --report srcDup.byFile`
- **Tests reading hook source text**: 10 files (useMissionsPage.ts is named only in comments in 4 of them). Method: `grep -rlE 'src/hooks/|hooks/use[A-Za-z]+\.ts' in tests/unit files that use readFileSync`

### Findings that survived verification (25 of 25)

#### hooks-07 · The missions and models composition roots re-list 197 lines of fields their slices already return

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -170, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useMissionsPage.ts`, `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts`, `src/app/work/missions/page.tsx`, `src/app/agent/models/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useMissionTemplatesState.ts`, `src/hooks/useModelActions.ts`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`, `src/components/missions/MissionsList.tsx`
- **Evidence:** line-census --report srcDup.byFile: missions/page.tsx 36, useMissionTemplatesState.ts 36, agent/models/page.tsx 18, useMissionsData.ts 18; useMissionsPage.ts and useModelsPage.ts absent. srcDup.top lines are bare shorthand keys ('handlePush, | handlePull, ...'). AST return-key walk: missions slices 52+48+24+8+7+14=153 with no repeats; models 15+19+14+6=54 with no repeats.
- **Sceptic:** Spreads are safe: an AST walk finds no repeated key across the 6 mission slices (153 keys) or the 4 model slices (54). The census claim is wrong, though. useMissionsPage.ts and useModelsPage.ts write key: slice.key and are not in srcDup.byFile. The 36-line windows pair missions/page.tsx's destructure (24-80) with useMissionTemplatesState.ts's return (173-222). The handlePush..busyCredentialId window pairs useModelActions.ts:349-369 with agent/models/page.tsx's destructure, not with useModelsPage. So spreading removes src lines but no repeated-window lines; only changing the page destructures, or passing the draft as one object, moves that metric. Spreading also widens the view model from 139 keys to 153, while 23 of today's 139 are never read (see missedByFinder). The TemplateEditorModal object prop also touches TemplateModals.tsx and b10-template-editor-has-its-own-draft.test.tsx.
- **Proposal:** Return { toastElement, showCreate, setShowCreate, editingId, setEditingId, openCreate, closeComposer, ...composer, ...templateState, ...data, ...dispatch, ...templateActions, ...filtering }, and the same pattern for models. Pass the template draft to TemplateEditorModal as one object prop. The view model gains the keys the current return leaves out (dispatchPayload, applyTemplateToForm and similar).

#### hooks-08 · Two hooks hold the same 14-field mission draft in 41 useState calls and six hand-written setter sequences

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, high, -100, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useMissionComposer.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionTemplatesState.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionTemplateActions.ts`, `src/components/missions/MissionCreateForm.tsx`, `src/components/missions/templates/TemplateEditorModal.tsx`
- **Evidence:** useMissionComposer.ts:58-87 (22 useState), useMissionTemplatesState.ts:44-70 (22 useState). Setter sequences at useMissionComposer.ts:201-220,267-306,314-351 and useMissionTemplatesState.ts:79-98,101-132,135-154. Source-text pin: tests/unit/mission-composer-finishes.test.tsx:195-203. The special case to keep: setFormField's newDispatch setter also sets dispatchAcknowledged (126-129).
- **Sceptic:** Real, but the arithmetic is off. Each hook declares 22 useState calls (useMissionComposer.ts:58-87, useMissionTemplatesState.ts:44-70), 44 in all, not 41. The 14 shared fields are confirmed. Pure mappers from a template or mission to a draft take one object-literal line per field, the same count as the setter calls they replace, so the six sequences barely shrink. The saving is in the useState declarations, the formState object (89-109), the setter map in setFormField (117-146) and the raw-setter return (376-399), with a generic useDraft hook at about +20. Three suites pin the current shape. mission-composer-finishes.test.tsx:195-203 slices the composer source from 'const clearMissionFormFields' to 'const setCategoryId' and expects setNewSchedule(. schedule-reaches-the-wire.test.tsx renders the hook in 5 cases. b10-template-editor-has-its-own-draft.test.tsx:112 uses useMissionTemplatesState. The view-model lines overlap hooks-07.
- **Proposal:** Write one useMissionDraft(defaults) hook: a single state object, setField and reset(seed). Use it twice, so the template editor's draft stays separate from the composer's by construction (T-0104, D72). The seed functions become pure mappers from a template, or a mission, to a draft. The template editor takes the draft object and setField.

#### hooks-01 · The missions board reads four endpoints by hand, beside the cache that already holds three of them

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, -90, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useMissionsData.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionsApi.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionCategories.ts`, `src/hooks/useDashboard.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionTemplateActions.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionDispatch.ts`
- **Evidence:** rg -n invalidateQueries src -> useSchedules.ts:35, useScripts.ts:72, useOperatorPrefs.ts:63, Sidebar.tsx:161 (no template/category key). useDashboard.ts:124-133 staleTime Infinity. Local list patching: useMissionsData.ts:83-90 updateMission, used at useMissionDispatch.ts:275,278. Injected fetcher pinned by tests/unit/b10-category-write-failures.test.tsx:49-51. The census already counts src/hooks/useMissionsData.ts at 18 repeated-window lines (node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, srcDup.byFile).
- **Sceptic:** The core is real. useMissionsApi.ts:10-28 reads four endpoints by hand. useMissionsData.ts:154-265 polls them every 15s. useDashboard.ts:124-133 caches /api/templates and /api/mission-categories with staleTime Infinity. Only four invalidateQueries calls exist in src, and none covers templates or categories. The 16-19 header does cite a clause useApiResource.ts:1-24 no longer has. The estimate leaves out two costs. First, the board patches its own list ahead of the server: handleCancel marks the row failed and puts it back on error (useMissionDispatch.ts:272-300). On the cache that needs setQueryData against useApiResource's private envelope (useApiResource.ts:61-64), and no helper exposes that. Second, useMissionCategories receives fetchCategories as an argument, and b10-category-write-failures.test.tsx:49-51 mounts it with a fake, so that suite must be rewritten. The page suites (u9, b10-board, u19, record-surfaces) use the fixtures view model and are unaffected. The operatorDecision is correctly flagged.
- **Proposal:** Read the four endpoints through useApiResource: the lists with refetchInterval 15_000, the detail keyed on the expanded id with enabled. Keep the ?mission= and ?template= deep-link latches as effects over the read data. Turn fetchData and loadCategories into refetches. Delete useMissionsApi.ts and the catalogue state in useMissionCategories. Writes then refresh the same cache entries the dashboard reads.
- **Operator decision:** Moving these reads onto the cache turns the per-poll error toasts for categories, templates and mission detail into banner-only failures. That changes behaviour documented at useMissionCategories.ts:38-39 ('the banner stays, the toast says it happened').

#### hooks-06 · useToast's single-slot fallback and toastElement are dead in the product and exist only for tests

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -45, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/feedback-context.ts`, `src/components/providers/FeedbackProvider.tsx`, `src/hooks/useChatPage.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionsPage.ts`, `src/hooks/useModelsPage.ts`, `tests/helpers/render-with-query.tsx`, `tests/unit/b2-toasts-stack.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/feedback-is-perceivable.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/composer-reject-is-not-a-cliff.test.ts`, `tests/unit/use-models-page-fallback-persist.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** rg -n '\{toastElement\}' src (21 renders in 20 files, excluding Toast.tsx:188). Pinning tests: b2-toasts-stack.test.tsx:109-115, feedback-is-perceivable.test.tsx:75-93, composer-reject-is-not-a-cliff.test.ts:435, use-models-page-fallback-persist.test.ts:9. rg -l for toast testid or status/alert roles in tests/unit: 28 files, 27 without a provider, 1 using the toast testid (b6-restore-page.test.tsx). All 28 useToast() calls pass no duration.
- **Sceptic:** Src facts re-derived. layout.tsx:124 mounts FeedbackProvider. {toastElement} renders 21 times in 20 files (rg also hits the Toast.tsx:188 comment). Three composition roots return it. The tests that pin the fallback are as stated. The test-impact number needs correcting: 28 unit files query the toast testid or status/alert roles and 27 of them mount no FeedbackProvider or Context, but only b6-restore-page.test.tsx uses the toast testid; the rest likely query LoadErrorBanner roles. ComposerNodeRunDetail.tsx:67-69 also documents the bare-render fallback and needs its comment changed. Net -45 is plausible; the breakage still needs a branch run.
- **Proposal:** Give tests a feedback provider: tests/helpers/render-with-query.tsx mounts FeedbackContext.Provider and the ToastView stack, without FeedbackShell's stats poll. Then delete from useToast the fallback state, ToastState, handleClose, the toastElement memo and the unused duration parameter. Remove the 21 renders, the three return keys and the matching page destructures, and rewrite the two tests that assert the fallback.

#### hooks-15 · theme.ts carries a focus-colour map of empty strings, an identity copy and two aliases

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -35, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/ui/theme.ts`, `src/components/ui/Select.tsx`, `src/app/work/chat/page.tsx`, `src/components/help/HelpSearch.tsx`, `src/components/ui/AutoTextarea.tsx`, `src/components/ui/GlowSurface.tsx`, `tests/unit/lockbook-tokens.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** theme.ts:205-214 (8 empty strings), 223 makeMap, 201-202 aliases. rg -n accentColor src -> InlineSelect callers at story-weaver/create/page.tsx:667,681,696 and CharacterCard.tsx:71; SearchInput at sessions/[id]/page.tsx:278; Input.tsx:48-54 empty focusBorder. makeMap( has one use (theme.ts:223).
- **Sceptic:** focusColorMap is all empty strings, and the glowSurfaceRgbMap identity copy and pill aliases are confirmed. The caller evidence is wrong. The accentColor prop belongs to InlineSelect (Select.tsx:32-48), and 4 call sites do pass it: story-weaver/create/page.tsx:667,681,696 and rec-room/components/CharacterCard.tsx:71. The reviewer's regex searched for <Select, which is not the component. Removing the prop edits those 4 lines. The finder also missed a second empty map: SearchInput's focusBorder at Input.tsx:48-54 (accentColor='orange' at results/sessions/[id]/page.tsx:278). Three design-lint pragmas are stale: Select.tsx InlineSelect className, Input.tsx:72 and theme.ts baseInputStyles justify an outline-none those lines no longer carry. Keep the names tests read by regex: u6-colour-that-is-there.test.ts:151-152 (pillBorderMap[color], pillBorderHoverMap[color]), u4-surfaces-and-edges.test.ts:277 (baseInputStyles) and lockbook-tokens.test.ts:223-224 ('const GLOW_RGBS').
- **Proposal:** Delete focusColorMap and Select's accentColor prop. Replace inputFieldClasses('cyan') with baseInputStyles. Export GLOW_RGBS under the public name and drop makeMap, ALL_COLORS and ColorEntry. Export the two pill maps under their own names.
- **Operator decision:** surfaceClasses, edgeClasses and measureClasses have no src reader, but org/LOCKBOOK.md:81 names theme.ts as the tokens' second home. Removing them needs the operator's ruling; they are not in the estimate.

#### hooks-19 · Small lib/api tidies: a split body parser, a one-line wrapper, and one route's field special-cased in the shared client fetch

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, low, -22, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/parse-json-body.ts`, `src/lib/api/parse-optional-json-body.ts`, `src/lib/api/audit-log.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-fetch.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-write.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/promote.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-promote-handler.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l src/lib/api/parse-json-body.ts parse-optional-json-body.ts = 43/43; rg -l parse-optional-json-body -> 3 routes, 1 test, lib-moves.json. audit-log.ts:10-12,31. rg -n cronPushError -> api-fetch.ts:134-135, mission-handlers/promote.ts:75, mission-promote-handler.ts:33, tests/unit/api-fetch-format.test.ts:32-38.
- **Sceptic:** Merging the two parsers is fine: 43+43 lines, 3 routes, 1 test and lib-moves.json, and most of the saving is the duplicate header. The audit-log 'build the path with join' part saves 0 lines and runs against repo-guide.md:180-182, which prefers string concatenation for paths in NFT-traced module code; drop it. Moving cronPushError server-side changes the error string /api/missions promote returns, and tests/unit/api-fetch-format.test.ts:32-40 pins the client-side append. isApiSuccessFalse is used in api-write.ts:120 and imported by api-write.test.ts:11,195-208, and API_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS by two tests. Un-exporting them rewrites tests for no line gain.
- **Proposal:** Move parseOptionalJsonBody into parse-json-body.ts, checking all five path spellings per the lib-move rule. Inline ensureLogsDir and build the path with join. Have the promote handlers compose the push error into `error`, and delete the client-side branch. Un-export the test-only names if knip allows.
- **Operator decision:** Needed only if cronPushError is dropped from the /api/missions response body, which is an API shape change. Composing it into `error` while keeping the field needs no sign-off.

#### hooks-09 · The deploy footer polls a cached endpoint with its own setInterval and posts with bare fetch

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, medium, -20, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useVersionFooter.ts`
- **Evidence:** useVersionFooter.ts:152-155 and 329-377; AbortSignal.timeout(8000) at 343; api-fetch.ts:54 API_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 45_000; node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/query.js:183-193; useApiResource.ts:125-139 (no dataUpdatedAt or timeout exposed).
- **Sceptic:** The duplicate read is real: useApiResource at 152-155 and setInterval with fetch at 329-377 hit the same URL. The rewrite is harder than stated. The poll aborts after 8s (343), but safeApiCall's default deadline is 45s (api-fetch.ts:54,103), and useApiResource exposes no timeout option. Query.fetch hands back the in-flight promise (query.js:183-193), so one request hung by the restart would stall a 2s refetchInterval for up to 45s. Counting ticks in an effect on data misses ticks: results are structurally shared (useApiResource.ts:17-19), so repeated identical 'running' answers leave data unchanged. That needs dataUpdatedAt, which the hook does not expose, or a wall-clock deadline. The default retry: 1 (FeedbackProvider.tsx:122) adds requests during the restart. The isMountedRef effect (138-147) and the forward refs can go on their own.
- **Proposal:** Hold the awaited action in state. Read /api/update?deploy=1 with refetchInterval: () => awaiting ? 2000 : false. React to deploy.state in one effect that counts ticks for the 450-poll cap. Send the POST through apiFetch. Keep the rule that a failed poll during the restart window is not a failed deploy.

#### hooks-17 · useRunProgress keeps its own list of run-event names beside the chat client's, and the two lists already disagree

The hook lacks reasoning.delta and the four tool.* events, and it has response.output_item.added (useRunProgress.ts:51), which RUN_SSE_EVENT_NAMES (chat-utils.ts:233-250) lacks. RunProgress.tsx:28 renders '{events.length} events', so switching to openRunEventStream changes that visible count: tool and reasoning.delta events get counted and output_item.added is dropped. The EventSource URL also gains encodeURIComponent. chat-failure-truth-streams.test.tsx:210-240 mounts the hook with a fake EventSource. About -20 is more realistic.
- **Proposal:** Build useRunProgress on openRunEventStream and classifyRunEvent, keeping its own onerror message.

The local names ToastFn and ShowToast appear in option interfaces, so imports need an alias or rename edits. #### hooks-03 · Two sanctioned ways to write: runWrite at 57 sites and useMutation at 7, and runWrite can absorb the seven

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, medium, -10, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-write.ts`, `src/hooks/useSchedules.ts`, `src/hooks/useScripts.ts`, `src/hooks/useOperatorPrefs.ts`, `src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`, `src/hooks/useModelActions.ts`, `src/hooks/useModelFallbackChain.ts`, `src/components/automation/AutomationList.tsx`, `src/app/work/scripts/page.tsx`, `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `docs/contributing/repo-guide.md`, `tests/unit/c3-one-way-to-write.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** node + typescript AST over src: runWrite calls 57 in 30 files, useMutation 7 in 4 files, dispatchMission 7 in 2 files. The convention is documented at org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md:136-142, docs/contributing/repo-guide.md:162-172, api-write.ts:10-12 and tests/unit/c3-one-way-to-write.test.ts:12,46. Behaviour that runWrite cannot express: AutomationList.tsx:156-157 (inline form error), Sidebar.tsx:149-153 (silent).
- **Sceptic:** The counts hold: an AST walk finds 57 runWrite calls in 30 files and 7 useMutation calls in 4 files. The saving does not. C3 planned a useApiMutation hook (org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md:125-129) and deliberately corrected it to runWrite plus useMutation (136-142); this proposal re-opens that decision. runWrite always toasts on failure (api-write.ts:131) and requires a successMessage (api-write.ts:52). AutomationList shows failures inline (AutomationList.tsx:156-157, failWith), and Sidebar is silent by design (Sidebar.tsx:149-153). A useWrite hook would therefore need a quiet mode, a per-variable pending key and an invalidates list, well above the 35 lines claimed. The two reload wrappers (useModelActions.ts:61-72, useModelFallbackChain.ts:47-58) have exactly two consumers, and a shared version costs about 12 lines. The honest net is about -10, which is not worth re-opening a documented convention.
- **Proposal:** Add useWrite() beside useApiResource. It calls runWrite with showToast from the feedback context, takes an invalidates: string[] list resolved through apiQueryKey, and exposes a busy key so a row can tell that its own write is pending. Move the 7 useMutation sites and the two reload wrappers onto it. Then narrow the lint rule's law text, api-write.ts:10-12, repo-guide.md:167-172 and the c3 test to one way. runWrite stays as the function useWrite calls. Keeping both ways is also defensible; the numbers above describe the choice either way.
- **Operator decision:** Retiring useMutation as the second sanctioned way changes the convention documented at docs/contributing/repo-guide.md:167-172, in the law text of design-lint's no-raw-write-outside-the-helper, and in tests/unit/c3-one-way-to-write.test.ts. It also has to decide whether Sidebar's preference write stays silent on failure.

One more cast at 158 (overrides.dispatchMode as typeof newDispatch) should use the same type. The composer already imports from dispatch-mode (line 31), so this is no new import line.

#### hooks-02 · A failing board read raises a new persistent alert toast every 15 seconds

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** accessibility, low, 0, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useMissionCategories.ts`, `src/hooks/useMissionsData.ts`, `src/components/providers/FeedbackProvider.tsx`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`
- **Evidence:** useMissionCategories.ts:37-45; useMissionsData.ts:193,214-216,226-231,258-265; Toast.tsx:96-107,114-115; FeedbackProvider.tsx:52-58. The dashboard's polled reads raise no toast (useDashboard.ts:83-113).
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. loadCategories toasts on every failure (useMissionCategories.ts:37-45). fetchData awaits it (useMissionsData.ts:193), and the 15s useInterval calls fetchData (258-265). The same tick toasts a failed templates read (214-216) and a failed detail read (226-231). Error toasts never auto-dismiss (Toast.tsx:96-107) and use role=alert, aria-live=assertive (114-115). pushToast has no dedupe; once all three slots hold errors it evicts the oldest error (FeedbackProvider.tsx:52-58), so a new alert is announced on every tick. Folding the fix into hooks-01 costs 0 lines.
- **Proposal:** hooks-01 fixes this: a read failure becomes the board's banner, as missionsLoadError already is (useMissionsData.ts:184-191). If hooks-01 is deferred, toast only when a read goes from ok to failed, or have pushToast skip a message already on the stack.
- **Operator decision:** useMissionCategories.ts:38-39 documents this toast. Removing or deduplicating it changes documented behaviour.

#### hooks-22 · Small seams around useApiResource: conditional option spreads, a renamed option, and a caller reading the hook's internal select shape

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useSessions.ts`, `src/hooks/useSessionDetail.ts`, `src/hooks/useChatConversations.ts`, `src/hooks/useApiResource.ts`

- **Sceptic:** Real, but not a line saving. tsconfig.json:7 sets strict without exactOptionalPropertyTypes, so passing undefined through is type-safe, and react-query treats enabled: undefined as enabled. Replacing the conditional spread with a plain refetchInterval line is the same number of lines. Making useApiResource's refetch resolve to the selected value adds a wrapper of about 4 lines. The refetchIntervalMs rename touches 2 hooks and 2 pages. The net is about 0.
- **Proposal:** Pass the options straight through. Rename refetchIntervalMs to refetchInterval (2 hooks and 2 page call sites: results/sessions/page.tsx:156 and sessions/[id]/page.tsx:42). Make useApiResource's refetch resolve to the selected value so no caller depends on the internal shape.

#### hooks-21 · useTwoStepConfirm returns a new object and a new isArmedFor on every render, so its callers' callbacks rebuild every render

It is a +4-line tidy with no measurable effect.

#### hooks-04 · The write rule reads 0 while 18 client writes bypass both sanctioned ways

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 8, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useVersionFooter.ts`, `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`, `src/hooks/useChatConversations.ts`, `src/hooks/useChatSend.ts`

- **Sceptic:** The rule's blind spot is real (design-lint.mjs:503-506,522), but two of the ten Story Weaver POSTs are reads. Action 'load' (story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx:98) runs from the effect at 129, and action 'spend' (133) runs from the effect at 150-153. The count is 16 writes plus 2 POST reads, not 18 writes. Widening the pattern to fetch-with-a-method would flag those reads as writes; they belong on useApiResource's body option (useApiResource.ts:47-52). 'src/lib modules that import api-fetch' is 13 files, most of them server code, plus api-write.ts itself, which must be excluded. The filter needs an explicit client list, and the baseline grows.
- **Proposal:** Widen the call pattern to include fetch( with a method, and add client modules under src/lib that import api-fetch to the file filter. Baseline what it finds, with reasons (the streaming POST keeps a pragma). Then convert the Story Weaver and chat writes.
