- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** performance, low, 10, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useApiResource.ts`, `src/hooks/useStats.ts`, `src/components/providers/FeedbackProvider.tsx`, `src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`, `src/hooks/useArtifacts.ts`, `src/hooks/useComposer.ts`, `src/hooks/useDeepResearch.ts`, `tests/e2e/requests.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/queryObserver.js:207-218 (focusManager.isFocused gate at 215); rg -n 'useStats\(' src -> 6 observers; useArtifacts.ts:16; useComposer.ts:35,60; useDeepResearch.ts:20,37; tests/e2e/requests.spec.ts:20-28.
- **Sceptic:** Mostly confirmed. query-core 5.101.0 sets a timer per observer (queryObserver.js:207-218) and dedupes only in-flight fetches. /api/stats has 6 observers. useArtifacts polls every 5s without a gate, and the composer and research lists every 4s. The detail polls every 3s beside useEventStream (composer/page.tsx:159-162, research/page.tsx:108-111). There are 19 refetchInterval literals in 11 files. Two nuances. FeedbackProvider and Sidebar mount in the same commit and share a phase; only page-level observers mounted later poll out of step, and requests.spec.ts:20-28 already budgets stats at '1 or 2' in 30s. The interval only fires while the window is focused (queryObserver.js:215), so 12-15 requests a minute is a focused-tab figure. The proposal does not address the out-of-step timers at all.
- **Proposal:** Gate the three list polls on a live run, as the sessions page does. Drop the detail poll to a slow fallback while useEventStream reports connected. Name the cadences in one module. Extend requests.spec.ts to /work/composer and /results/artifacts.
- **Operator decision:** Gating these polls changes how soon a run started elsewhere appears on Artifacts, Composer and Research; sign off per page.

#### hooks-05 · Hooks that read by hand inside effects are invisible to all three read gates

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 25, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs`, `tests/unit/u15-reads-go-through-the-hook.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useMissionsData.ts`, `src/hooks/useChatSend.ts`, `src/hooks/useVersionFooter.ts`
- **Evidence:** Scratchpad probe gate-miss.cjs (same FETCHERS and useEffect walk as line-census.mjs:107-146, following imports and destructured hook results one level): depth 0 = 0; depth 1 = useHindsightCrudTab.ts, useHindsightMemories.ts, useChatSend.ts, useMissionsData.ts; with --fetch also story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx. The census report shows counts.handRolledReadHooks 0. Rule law text: 'A read in a click handler is on demand and is not this' (design-lint.mjs no-raw-fetch-in-component).
- **Sceptic:** The census does follow only same-file functions (line-census.mjs:119-123,130). A one-level cross-file probe using the census's own AST walk finds 4 misses, not 3: useMissionsData.ts, useChatSend.ts, components/memory/hindsight/useHindsightCrudTab.ts and useHindsightMemories.ts. Counting platform fetch adds story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx for 5. useVersionFooter is not a miss. Its poll starts from a click, via runDeployAction to pollDeployStatusRef (useVersionFooter.ts:249), and the rule excludes on-demand reads in its own law text. Adding src/hooks/*.ts to the lint rule's and u15's file filters finds nothing by itself: those regexes only see a fetcher named textually inside the effect body, and the depth-0 probe finds 0. The work is resolving imports and destructured hook results in the census, so about +25 lines, not +15.
- **Proposal:** In the census, follow callees bound by destructuring, and callees imported from src/hooks and src/lib, one level deep. Add src/hooks/*.ts to the lint rule's and the u15 test's file filters. Baseline the three hooks by name until hooks-01, hooks-09 and a chat read move land.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- useMissionsPage returns 23 keys that neither of the view model's two src readers ever names (src/app/work/missions/page.tsx, src/components/missions/MissionsList.tsx): setShowCreate, setEditingId, setNewCategoryId, setShowTemplateEditor, setEditingTemplateId, setNewInstruction, setNewContext, setNewGoals, newOutputFormat, setNewOutputFormat, newConstraints, setNewConstraints, setNewProfile, setNewModel, setNewProvider, setModelAndProvider, setNewMissionTime, setNewTimeout, setNewLocalDirs, setLocalDirDraft, setNewReferences, setReferenceInput, setNewSkills. Method: AST return keys of useMissionsPage.ts checked against the identifier set of those two files. tests/helpers/fixtures.ts:69-108 casts `as unknown as MissionsPageViewModel`, so no test pins them. About -23 lines, low risk. This cuts against hooks-07's spread, which would widen the surface to 153 keys. The models view model has 0 unused keys.
- useMissionsData.ts destructures useMissionCategories' nine keys at 107-115 and returns the same nine at 292-300, identical to useMissionCategories.ts:108-116. Those lines are the 18 repeated-window lines the census reports for useMissionsData.ts (line-census --report srcDup.byFile). Keeping the slice as one object (categoriesSlice.loadCategories inside fetchData) and spreading it removes about 16 src lines and those windows at low risk. Two consumers, but no new interface.
- Hand-rolled effect reads that every gate and the finder missed. src/components/memory/hindsight/useHindsightMemories.ts:92-94 runs loadRecentMemories through hindsightGet to safeApiCall; useHindsightCrudTab.ts:64-66 runs loadHindsightList through hindsightGet. src/app/recroom/story-weaver/[id]/page.tsx:96-129 (action 'load') and 131-153 (action 'spend') are POST reads in effects via platform fetch, and useApiResource's body option (useApiResource.ts:47-52) was written for exactly these story routes. Found with a scratchpad probe using the census's AST walk extended one level across files, plus platform fetch.
- useApiResource passes a fresh `pick` closure as select on every render (useApiResource.ts:85,108). query-core re-runs select whenever options.select !== its memoized #selectFn (node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/queryObserver.js:287-294). So every one of the roughly 75 reads re-selects on every render of its host, including the 200-row missions and templates maps, not only when data changes. A ref or useCallback over opts.select, selectMeta and fallback is about +3 lines, low risk.
- Stale design-lint pragmas and a second dead focus map in the theme area. The no-bare-outline-none pragmas at Select.tsx (InlineSelect className), Input.tsx:72 and theme.ts (baseInputStyles) justify outline-none by an accent focus class, but none of those lines carries outline-none and the focus classes are all empty strings. SearchInput's focusBorder map at Input.tsx:48-54 is five empty strings behind an accentColor prop (passed at results/sessions/[id]/page.tsx:278). About -10 lines alongside hooks-15.

### Not examined

- Nothing was run: no jest, build or app. The per-poll toasts (hooks-02), the stale dashboard cache entry (hooks-01) and the staggered /api/stats fetches (hooks-10) come from reading the code and TanStack's query-core source.
- The exact number of unit suites that fail when the useToast fallback is removed (hooks-06) needs that change tried on a branch; only a grep upper bound (23) is given.
- The client-side hooks under src/components/memory/hindsight/ (useHindsightCrudTab, useHindsightMemories, useHindsightModels, useHindsightDirectives) were only checked for their ShowToast type, not for their read and write paths.
- TemplateEditorModal, MissionCreateForm and MissionsList bodies were not read, so the prop-reshape savings in hooks-07 and hooks-08 are estimates from the page's call sites.
- The server modules in src/lib/api (api-auth, auth-token, auth-throttle, read-only, api-schemas, api-response) were reviewed for duplication and usage counts only, not for security correctness.
- Converting the Story Weaver pages' 10 raw fetch calls is page-layer work; only the tooling gap is reported here.
- Client reads and writes under src/modules/** (rec-room) were not examined.
- src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts was read only for its API wrappers and run-event handling, not for its markdown and export helpers.

## Dimension: docs

The docs scope is 75 markdown pages (10,202 lines), seven root documents (870 lines) and 2,057 lines of docs tooling. Both docs gates pass on this Windows host: check-doc-links exits 0 and docs:check reports "75 pages, 21 routes". Sampling the prose against the tree after C6/C7 found it mostly accurate: 364 repo-path mentions with 5 stale, 279 /api mentions with 1 stale, 267 identifiers with 1 contradicted, and no stale npm scripts, env vars or page routes. The gates check far less than the docs index claims. Six links resolve only on a case-insensitive filesystem, so they should fail on the Linux CI runner. Three links in TRADEMARK.md and branding/ broke in C7 because root documents are never link-checked. Only 2 of the 9 generated-block extractors fence anything, while the facts they would generate are typed by hand and drifting. The contributor pages disagree with the code and with each other: the lint chain is "nine" in CONTRIBUTING.md and repo-guide.md, "twelve" in testing.md, and 12 steps in package.json. repo-guide.md still says there is no route wrapper, but 76 of 99 route files contain route(. CHANGELOG.md is one 555-line Unreleased section of 124 bullets. It describes in-between states that no longer exist (six pills and three pills; a Settings index and "Settings is one page"), says schema version 41 when the head is 42, and promises 26 redirects "for this release only" with no task tracking their removal. Duplication is in a few places, not everywhere: README.md and start-here/install.md share 116 twelve-word runs of text, runtime-architecture.md says four of its own sections twice, and the docs tooling reads the docs folder in five separate places. All 21 screenshots are referenced, but each recapture adds about 3 MB, and 24 commits have put 45.3 MB of PNGs into history.

### Measurements

- **Docs pages / routes (docs:check)**: 75 pages, 21 routes, exit 0. Method: `npx tsx scripts/docs/check.mts`
- **check-doc-links on this host**: exit 0 ('every relative link in docs/ resolves'). Method: `node scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs (Windows, git core.ignorecase=true)`
- **Docs markdown lines**: 10,202. Method: `git ls-files 'docs/*.md' | xargs cat | wc -l`
- **Root document lines**: README 155, CHANGELOG 555, AGENTS 31, CLAUDE 31, NOTICE 8, TRADEMARK 55, REBRANDING 35; branding/**/*.md 96. Method: `wc -l`
- **AGENTS.md vs CLAUDE.md**: byte-identical, 31 lines (cap 40). Method: `cmp AGENTS.md CLAUDE.md; wc -l`
- **Docs tooling lines / comment lines**: 2,057 lines; 526 comment lines (lib.mjs 194/786, build-site.mjs 107/349, extract.ts 103/462, check.mts 56/200, lib.d.mts 42/157, serve.mjs 24/103). Method: `wc -l; grep -cE '^\s*(//|\*|/\*\*|\*/)' per file`
- **Repo-path mentions in docs + root docs (outside generated fences)**: 364 checked, 15 unresolved, 5 genuinely stale (rest are globs, Hermes-side paths, examples). Method: `scratchpad docclaims.mjs: regex over src|scripts|tests|org|data|public|.github|branding paths, existsSync at repo root`
- **/api route mentions in prose**: 279 checked, 1 stale (catalog-and-profiles.md:83), 3 intentional tombstones. Method: `docclaims.mjs: matched against every route.ts dir under src/app/api with [param] wildcards`
- **camelCase identifiers in backticks**: 267 checked, 2 absent from src/scripts/tests/data (withApiRoute; PatterTech_WebKit is an external repo name). Method: `docclaims.mjs: substring search over code corpus`
- **npm run / env var / page route mentions**: 115 npm-run (0 missing), 257 env var (0 missing; 1 tombstone, 1 compose comment), 62 page routes (0 stale). Method: `docclaims.mjs against package.json scripts, code corpus + .env.example, src/app page tree`
- **Bold UI labels in guides and start-here**: 762 checked, 29 not found in src (mostly bold headings or templated labels such as 'Write chapter N'). Method: `scratchpad uilabels.mjs: **Label** of 5 words or fewer, case-insensitive search of src/**/*.ts(x)`
- **Link targets that only resolve case-insensitively**: 6 (api.md:60, cross-platform.md:94, env-reference.md:15/113/114, migration.md:213). Method: `uppercase .md link targets resolved and compared exactly against git ls-files`
- **Broken links in root docs (ungated)**: 3 (TRADEMARK.md:27; branding/guidelines/COLORS.md:16 x2). Method: `docclaims.mjs + ls src/lib/*.ts (6 files, no theme.ts); docs/design-tokens.md absent`
- **Link texts naming retired UPPERCASE filenames**: 61 across 15 files. Method: `grep -rnoE '\[[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\.md\]\([^)]+\)' docs README.md | wc -l`
- **Generated-block extractors vs fences**: 9 extractors, 2 fenced (event-types in analytics-events.md:184, quests in quests.md:49). Method: `GENERATED_BLOCK_IDS lib.mjs:55-65; grep -rn '<!-- generated:' docs`
- **Lint chain step count**: package.json 12; CONTRIBUTING.md:31 says nine; repo-guide.md:25,33 says nine; testing.md:164 says twelve. Method: `split package.json scripts.lint on &&; read pages`
- **docs/images**: 21 PNG, 21 referenced (0 orphans), 2,947,070 bytes, all last changed in 8c61d674 (T-0143). Method: `grep -rl images/<name>.png per image; stat -c %s; git log -1 per file`
- **PNG history under docs/images**: 24 commits, 347 blobs, 45.3 MB. Method: `git log --oneline -- docs/images | wc -l; git rev-list --objects --all -- docs/images | git cat-file --batch-check`
- **UI churn since screenshots were captured**: 221 files changed under src/app, src/components, src/modules in 11 commits. Method: `git diff --stat 8c61d674 HEAD -- src/app src/components src/modules; git log --oneline 8c61d674..HEAD | wc -l`
- **CHANGELOG shape**: 555 lines, 35,790 bytes, 124 bullets (Added 16, Changed 55, Fixed 44, Security 2, Removed 7), 1 [Unreleased] section, 0 git tags, 0 link references, 23 blank-line-separated bullets. Method: `awk over '### ' and '- **' lines; git tag | wc -l; grep -cE '^\[[^]]+\]:'`
- **Temporary redirects promised 'for this release only'**: 26. Method: `grep -c 'temporary(' next.config.ts`
- **Cross-page duplication**: README<>install.md 116 shared 12-word shingles and 5 verbatim sentences; README<>start-here/index.md 44; 6 verbatim >=10-word sentences across all files. Method: `scratchpad docdup.mjs (front matter and generated fences stripped)`
- **Inbound links per docs page**: 0 pages with no inbound link; 6 pages with exactly one. Method: `docdup.mjs: relative .md links from docs, README.md and CHANGELOG.md`
- **Front matter consistency**: type 47/75, tags 47/75, compiled_from 29/75, audience 74/75; H1 differs from title on 10 pages. Method: `git ls-files 'docs/*.md' | xargs grep -l '^key:'; compare first '# ' line to title:`
- **Unit suites that read docs files or the manifest**: 26. Method: `grep -rlE docs-path patterns tests/unit | wc -l`
- **Built-site CSS minimum contrast**: 4.51:1 (--ink-faint #6b7f95 on --panel #0a141d). Method: `WCAG relative-luminance formula over SITE_CSS tokens in lib.mjs:360-365`

### Findings that survived verification (22 of 22)

#### docs-03 · CHANGELOG Unreleased reads as an engineering diary, contradicts itself and has a wrong schema version

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** doc-drift, low, -150, adjusted
- **Files:** `CHANGELOG.md`
- **Evidence:** `wc -l` = 555; `wc -c` = 35790; `grep -c '^- '` = 124; `git tag | wc -l` = 0. CHANGELOG.md:21-22 'taking the schema to version 41'; src/lib/db-schema.ts:35 `MIGRATION_HEAD_SCHEMA_VERSION = 42`; the newest migration is src/lib/db/migrations/042_fallback_identity.sql. Superseded pairs: :54-57 against :301-306; :62-64 and :507-508 against :200-206; :307-311 'Six status pills' against :164-171 'three pills', and src/app/page.tsx:325/333/341 renders three labels (Scheduler, Spend, Processes). An awk count over lines 93-367 finds 18 loose and 37 tight bullets.
- **Sceptic:** The headline numbers, the wrong schema version and most of the superseded pairs check out. Two details are wrong. The 161-163 / 391-394 pair is not a contradiction: one bullet adds the error bubble and the other removes a duplicate toast, so they can be merged but do not conflict. The loose-list count is 18 blank-line-separated bullets in Changed, not 23. The -180 is a guess that depends on the operator's editorial call, which the finding flags correctly. extract.ts already has a schema-head extractor (extract.ts:199-212) that would have stopped the wrong 41; see docs-08.
- **Proposal:** Rewrite Unreleased as the 1.0.0 release note, describing only the end state. Merge each superseded pair into one bullet. Drop code-level bullets, which their task records already hold. Fix the upgrade step's schema number, or drop the number as migration.md does. Use one list style throughout. Add a Keep a Changelog compare link when the release is tagged.
- **Operator decision:** CHANGELOG.md is the release note the operator publishes. Which items stay, and their wording, are the operator's call. Cutting bullets changes documented release content. Method: about 8 merged pairs (-50), about 10 code-level bullets dropped (-60), backstory trimmed in the 55 Changed bullets (-70).

#### docs-08 · Seven of nine generated-block extractors fence nothing while the same facts are hand-typed

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -120, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/extract.ts`, `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`, `scripts/docs/lib.d.mts`, `tests/unit/b15-generated-blocks.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b1-api-md-matches-the-route-tree.test.ts`, `docs/contributing/testing.md`, `docs/CONTRIBUTING.md`, `docs/contributing/repo-guide.md`
- **Evidence:** `grep -rn '<!-- generated:' docs` returns two fences: docs/reference/analytics-events.md:184 (event-types) and docs/reference/quests.md:49 (quests). scripts/docs/lib.mjs:55-65 GENERATED_BLOCK_IDS lists nine. Extractor ranges in extract.ts: achievementsBlock 74-85, lintStepsBlock 101-109, configSectionsBlock 111-121, seedManifestsBlock 123-149, apiRoutesBlock 151-189, schemaHeadBlock 191-212, envTableBlock 275-317; orphan JSDoc 214-218. tests/unit/b15-generated-blocks.test.ts:76-86 pins the nine, and its fixtures use the api-routes/env-table ids (:63-72, :97-158). org/tasks/T-0109.json: 'no docs page carries a fence yet, so B18 inserts the markers'.
- **Sceptic:** The facts check out: only 2 of 9 extractors have a fence, and the orphan JSDoc is at extract.ts:214-218. The framing as dead code is wrong. T-0109 records that 'no docs page carries a fence yet, so B18 inserts the markers', which makes this unfinished adoption, not code nobody wanted. The proposal also works against itself: it deletes schema-head and env-table while docs-03 found the CHANGELOG schema number wrong, and the lint count is hand-typed in three places. The better default is to adopt lint-steps, schema-head and env-table (env-reference.md) and delete only the extractors no page wants. Line sizes, measured: achievements 74-85 (12), config-sections 111-121 (11), seed-manifests 123-149 (27), api-routes with HTTP_METHODS 151-189 (39), schema-head 191-212 (22), env-table 275-317 (43), plus 2 switch lines each and the 5-line orphan. The finder's '-30 prose' overlaps docs-05 and docs-06. This departs from recorded plan decision 3, so the operator flag stays.
- **Proposal:** Use lint-steps now: fence it once in testing.md and link to it from CONTRIBUTING.md and repo-guide.md. Delete the other unused extractors unless a page adopts them in the same change, and shrink GENERATED_BLOCK_IDS and the test's list to match. api-routes cannot replace api.md's table without splitting out the hand-written Purpose column, so the b1 test stays. Delete the orphan JSDoc.
- **Operator decision:** B15 (T-0109), decision 3, fenced nine named facts. Deleting extractors departs from that recorded plan decision, so confirm which facts stay generated. Method: six deleted extractors ≈ 138 lines, plus 12 switch lines, -5 orphan comment, -30 prose from collapsing three lint lists into one 12-line fence, +10 test/list edits.

#### docs-21 · Docs tooling keeps batch-history comments that C5 cut elsewhere, and docs are not in the line census

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, low, -80, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`, `scripts/docs/build-site.mjs`, `scripts/docs/extract.ts`, `scripts/docs/check.mts`, `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`
- **Evidence:** `grep -cE '^\s*(//|\*|/\*\*|\*/)'` gives lib.mjs 194/786, build-site.mjs 107/349, extract.ts 103/462, check.mts 56/200, lib.d.mts 42/157. scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs:213-222 commentEssays iterates `tsFiles(src)` only. `grep docs scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs` finds no measure over docs/. Batch-history comments: lib.mjs:68 'The URL groups B3's regroup retired', :102 'What B15's placeholder guides say', :541 'B16 rebases'.
- **Sceptic:** The comment counts re-derive with the same regex, apart from one SITE_CSS `* {` line miscounted as a comment. The census gap is real, and it is wider than docs: commentEssays scans src only, so C5 never covered scripts/. The -150 has no file-by-file method behind it, and much of the commentary is contract: lib.mjs:416-423 on why search avoids fetch, the lazy import in check-derived-views.mjs:171-176, and the ordering and path-form rules in readPages. A realistic cut from narration alone is smaller and uncertain.
- **Proposal:** Cut batch-history narration from the docs tooling and keep comments that state a contract (e.g. lib.mjs:416-423 on why search is not a fetch). Consider adding docs/**/*.md lines as a census measure, so a docs consolidation is held the way src and tests are.

#### docs-10 · runtime-architecture.md is two merged pages that say four sections twice, with stale claims

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -50, confirmed
- **Files:** `docs/reference/runtime-architecture.md`
- **Evidence:** docs/reference/runtime-architecture.md:130 'Merged from `PLATFORM_VISION.md` in T-0109'; :132 '## PatterStage platform vision' at the same level as the page's own sections. The ASCII layers diagram at :18-31 repeats as mermaid at :138-151. The scheduler is described at :63-70 and :157-164, runs at :53-61 and :166-168, testing at :80-99 and :180-182. :77 'CH-owned'; :178 'powers the command-center dashboard', against CHANGELOG.md:310-311 'the Command Center block are gone'. :195 links the page to itself as '[RUNTIME_ARCHITECTURE.md](runtime-architecture.md)'; :197 labels env-reference.md as '[HERMES_CONFIG_INTEGRATION.md]'.
- **Sceptic:** I re-read the whole page, and every duplicate and stale line is where the finding says. The :77 and :178 fixes overlap docs-15; those count 0 net lines, so nothing is double-counted in size.
- **Proposal:** Fold the vision half into the first half. Keep one diagram and one section each for scheduler, runs and testing. Keep the unique Core features table (:170-178) and Security (:184-191). Fix :77 and :178, and drop the self-link.

#### docs-11 · Docs tooling re-implements the docs walk five times and page parsing three times

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -50, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/check.mts`, `scripts/docs/build-site.mjs`, `scripts/docs/extract.ts`, `scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs`, `scripts/tooling/check-derived-views.mjs`, `scripts/docs/lib.d.mts`, `tests/unit/b15-docs-check-refusals.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b15-front-matter-and-manifest.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b15-site-links-are-relative.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** .md walks: check.mts:41-48 markdownFiles, extract.ts:361-368 markdownFiles, check-doc-links.mjs:24-31 walk, check-derived-views.mjs:145-153 markdownUnder. build-site.mjs:83-92 walk returns all files and is used at :108 (pages) and :225 (assets). The parse loop is written three times: check.mts:139-147, build-site.mjs readPages, check-derived-views.mjs:175-184. rel() at check.mts:37-39, extract.ts:37-39, check-doc-links.mjs:22. `interface DocPage` at lib.d.mts:25, b15-docs-check-refusals.test.ts:40, b15-front-matter-and-manifest.test.ts:40, b15-site-links-are-relative.test.ts:37. docs-pages.yml:16 runs only `npm run docs:check`, not check-doc-links.
- **Sceptic:** The duplication is real, but it is smaller and harder to take than claimed. build-site.mjs's walk (83-92) returns every file, not only .md, and copyAssets reuses it (build-site.mjs:225), so four walks are .md-only. The walks sort differently: extract.ts uses compare(), the others localeCompare, and manifest bytes may depend on the order. Path forms also differ: check.mts builds absolute-to-rel paths, while build-site and check-derived-views build `docs/<rel>` against a --docs fixture dir. check-derived-views deliberately lazy-imports lib.mjs (:171-176) so that fixture repos without docs/ work, so a shared helper must be lazy too. checkDocs is a pure function over injected input, so folding the link check in means injecting a file-exists function and updating the refusal-code list and tests. The tests probably declare DocPage locally because they load lib.mjs through a runtime lib() require; an `import type` would still work. The ~-100 does not account for any of this.
- **Proposal:** Add a small fs module (e.g. scripts/docs/read-docs.mjs, ~20 lines) exporting readDocPages(dir) that returns {pages, errors}, and use it in check.mts, build-site.mjs, extract.ts and check-derived-views.mjs. Fold the link check into checkDocs as a 'broken-link' refusal (with docs-01's exact-case lookup and docs-02's root docs) and delete the separate lint step. Have the three tests import DocPage from lib.d.mts.
- **Operator decision:** Folding check-doc-links into docs:check deletes the command scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs and changes the lint chain that testing.md:164 documents as twelve gates. Method: 5 walks × ~8 lines + 3 loops × ~10 + 3 rel × 3 ≈ -79, helper +20; merging the link check -76 +25; test type imports -21 +3.

#### docs-12 · The docs build writes a file and keeps a function nothing needs, and its refusal-code list is incomplete

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -30, confirmed
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/build-site.mjs`, `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`, `scripts/docs/lib.d.mts`, `tests/unit/b15-site-links-are-relative.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b15-docs-check-refusals.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** build-site.mjs:282 `write(join(args.out, "search.json"), searchJson)`; :317 writes public/help/search.json, which src/lib/help/help-source.ts:97 reads. lib.mjs:545-554 renderFragment does `void page; void manifest; return html;`, called at build-site.mjs:333, declared at lib.d.mts:129 and in b15-site-links-are-relative.test.ts:66, tested at :230-253. lib.mjs:45-52 and lib.d.mts:59-65 list 6 codes. lib.mjs:719 'guide-is-a-stub' and :733 'guide-missing-sections'. b15-docs-check-refusals.test.ts:159 pins six. lib.mjs:109 STUB_MARKERS includes 'Written in B18'. docs-pages.yml:19 uploads `site`.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived each part. site/search.json is written at build-site.mjs:282. The site's own search reads search-index.js (lib.mjs:416-429), and the app reads public/help/search.json, written separately at build-site.mjs:317. renderFragment returns its html argument unchanged. checkDocs emits 8 codes while REFUSAL_CODES and RefusalCode list 6. The operator flag for removing the Pages URL is correct.
- **Proposal:** Stop writing site/search.json. Replace renderFragment with body(page) at build-site.mjs:333, and remove it from lib.d.mts and the 3 tests. Add the two missing codes to REFUSAL_CODES, RefusalCode and the test.
- **Operator decision:** Dropping site/search.json removes a published URL from the GitHub Pages site (docs-pages.yml uploads site/).

#### docs-17 · SUPPORT.md and start-here/getting-help.md are two start-here pages for one question

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, low, -22, adjusted
- **Files:** `docs/SUPPORT.md`, `docs/start-here/getting-help.md`, `docs/README.md`
- **Evidence:** docs/SUPPORT.md front matter: section start-here, nav 60, 36 lines. docs/start-here/getting-help.md: section start-here, nav 50; :67 '[Support](../SUPPORT.md) is the short policy version of this page'. SUPPORT.md:24 '[USER_WALKTHROUGH_GUIDE.md](README.md), [RUNTIME_ARCHITECTURE.md](...), [DEPLOY.md](...)'; :26 '[TESTING.md](contributing/testing.md)'. The two pages share 3 twelve-word shingles.

Taking the page out of the start-here order still means giving it some section in front matter, because every page carries one, and then regenerating the manifest. getting-help.md:67 links to it as 'the short policy version of this page', so the two pages point at each other on purpose. It stays an operator voice decision.
- **Proposal:** Keep docs/SUPPORT.md, because GitHub reads it as a community file, but cut it to a ~10-line pointer to getting-help.md, security and the code of conduct, and take it out of the start-here reading order. Regenerate docs/manifest.json rather than editing it.
- **Operator decision:** SUPPORT.md is a GitHub community-health file and its wording is the maintainer's voice. Keep the filename; confirm the cut.

#### docs-16 · README repeats the install page and the start-here index

So the removable part is the WSL block (:55-63) and a trimmed token paragraph, about 15 lines. The AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md statement is 6 lines in README (:126-131) and 2 in docs/README.md (:83-84). This remains the operator's call for the GitHub front page.

#### docs-22 · branding/assets holds only a README describing assets to come

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, low, -12, adjusted
- **Files:** `branding/assets/README.md`, `branding/README.md`, `TRADEMARK.md`
- **Evidence:** `git ls-files branding` lists LICENSE.md, README.md, assets/README.md and guidelines/COLORS.md, with no asset files. branding/assets/README.md:10 'live here as they are added'; branding/README.md:10 says the same. TRADEMARK.md:26 links `[branding/assets/](branding/assets/)`.
- **Sceptic:** The facts are right, but the fold has a cost the finding missed. git does not track empty directories, so deleting branding/assets/README.md removes branding/assets/ from the tree. That breaks the TRADEMARK.md:26 link and deletes the location the trademark text reserves. Rewording the legal text is not optional as part of the fold, and the payoff is 12 lines. Keeping it as-is is defensible.
- **Proposal:** Fold branding/assets/README.md into branding/README.md and update TRADEMARK.md:26 to name branding/, or leave as is until assets exist.
- **Operator decision:** branding/ and TRADEMARK.md define what is reserved. Changing the named location is a brand/legal wording decision.

#### docs-18 · Each screenshot recapture adds about 3 MB to history, and nothing notices when shots go stale

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** process, low, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `docs/images`, `tests/e2e/screenshots.spec.ts`, `scripts/docs/build-site.mjs`
- **Evidence:** `du -cb docs/images/*.png` = 2,947,070. `git log --format=%h -- docs/images | wc -l` = 24, the last being 8c61d674 (T-0143). `git rev-list --objects --all -- docs/images | git cat-file --batch-check` gives 347 PNG blobs and 47,456,951 bytes (45.3 MiB). `git log --oneline 8c61d674..HEAD | wc -l` = 11; limited to src/app src/components src/modules = 3; `git diff --stat 8c61d674 HEAD -- src/app src/components src/modules` = 221 files. .gitignore:66 `/site/`, :67 `/public/help/`.
- **Sceptic:** The byte counts and blob counts are right. Two supporting claims are misleading. site/ and public/help/ are gitignored (.gitignore:66-67), so copying images into them costs the repo nothing. And 11 commits sit between the capture and HEAD, but only 3 of them touch src/app, src/components or src/modules. 'About 3 MB per recapture' holds only when all 21 shots change. The proposal is a process change with no line impact.
- **Proposal:** Capture screenshots at release points rather than per UI batch, and losslessly compress PNGs before committing. Record the capture commit, e.g. in a small generated file beside docs/images, so docs:check can warn when a guide's screen changed after its shot.
- **Operator decision:** When screenshots are captured is a release-process decision. Reclaiming the 45.3 MB already in history would need a history rewrite, which is destructive and not proposed.

H1 differs from title on: design-tokens.md, api.md, catalog-and-profiles.md, schema.md, spend.md, cross-platform.md, data-storage.md, deploy.md, host-scheduling.md ('Host scheduling' vs '# System cron: Hindsight backup'), migration.md. lib.mjs:38-43 IGNORED_KEYS: 'front matter that another toolchain reads'. eos-compile.mjs:127 `body.push(`compiled_from: ${templatePath}`)`.
- **Sceptic:** Counts and H1 mismatches re-derived exactly: 10 pages. The -123 depends entirely on deleting front-matter keys. lib.mjs:38-43 says another toolchain reads them, scripts/tooling/eos-compile.mjs:103-127 writes compiled_from on EOS output, and the root legal pages carry the same keys. Nothing shows the keys are unused, so deletion is unsafe to count. The H1 half is real and nets 0 lines. Key removal belongs in operatorDecision only, with no line credit.

#### docs-02 · Root documents are outside the link gate, and C7 broke three of their links

The branding/guidelines/COLORS.md link to docs/design-tokens.md broke in B15 (T-0109), when that page moved. My exact-case scan found no other broken links in README.md, CHANGELOG.md, REBRANDING.md or AGENTS.md. Most of the change is path edits (0 net lines); widening the walk adds about 4 lines.

#### docs-01 · Six doc links resolve only on a case-insensitive filesystem

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 6, confirmed
- **Files:** `docs/reference/api.md`, `docs/running/cross-platform.md`, `docs/running/env-reference.md`, `docs/running/migration.md`, `scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs`
- **Evidence:** A scratchpad script (links.mjs) resolved every relative link in docs/, the root *.md files and branding/**/*.md against `git ls-files`. Six targets exist only under a different case: docs/reference/api.md:60 -> SPEND.md (tracked as spend.md); docs/running/cross-platform.md:94, env-reference.md:113 and migration.md:213 -> DEPLOY.md (deploy.md); env-reference.md:15 and :114 -> MIGRATION.md (migration.md). check-doc-links.mjs:53 `if (existsSync(abs)) continue;`. The lint script includes check-doc-links, and ci.yml:80 `run: npm run lint` runs under `runs-on: ubuntu-latest`.
- **Sceptic:** I re-derived all six links by comparing each target, case and all, against `git ls-files`. package.json `lint` chains `node scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs`, and ci.yml:80 runs `npm run lint` in an ubuntu-latest job (runs-on at ci.yml:56). check-doc-links.mjs:53 uses existsSync, which ignores case on NTFS. No operator decision is needed. I could not see whether CI on dev is red right now, because I did not query GitHub.
- **Proposal:** Point the six links at the lowercase filenames. Make check-doc-links check existence by exact case: readdirSync the parent and require an exact name match. A Windows or macOS run then fails the same way Linux does.

#### docs-20 · Built docs site has two h1 elements per page and unannounced search results

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** accessibility, low, 20, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`
- **Evidence:** scripts/docs/lib.mjs:456 `['<nav class="rail">', "<h1>PatterStage docs</h1>"]`; :523 `<h1>${escapeHtml(page.data.title)}</h1>`; `grep -c aria-live scripts/docs/lib.mjs` = 0. The SITE_CSS comment (lib.mjs:350-357) says design-lint does not reach it. WCAG luminance ratio for #6b7f95 on #0a141d = 4.506, applied to the .rail h2 11px rule at lib.mjs:380-383.
- **Sceptic:** Two h1 elements, no aria-live, and a 4.506:1 contrast ratio all re-derived. The markup fixes are about 3 lines. contrast-check re-derives the app's --color-ps-text-* tiers from globals.css, so pointing it at a CSS string inside a JS template literal is a real extension (a parser plus a threshold table), not a one-liner.
- **Proposal:** Render the rail title as a non-heading element (the nav's aria-label is enough), add aria-live="polite" to #results, and point contrast-check at the SITE_CSS tokens so the published site shares the app's contrast floor.

#### docs-09 · Docs gates cannot see prose claims, and the docs index overstates what docs:check refuses

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, medium, 90, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/docs/lib.mjs`, `scripts/docs/check.mts`, `scripts/tooling/check-doc-links.mjs`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/running/data-storage.md`, `docs/reference/catalog-and-profiles.md`
- **Evidence:** scratchpad stale.mjs: 286 backticked repo paths, 11 unresolved, of which the real ones are TRADEMARK.md:27, COLORS.md:16 (x2), CONTRIBUTING.md:62, data-storage.md:58-59 and repo-guide.md:167. /api mentions: 280, and the 5 unresolved are all tombstones or placeholders. `git ls-files src/app/api/agent/profiles` shows [id], [id]/toolsets, sync/import, sync/pull and sync/push, with no sync/drift. CHANGELOG.md:540-543 records the drift report as removed. lib.mjs refusal codes emitted: route-without-guide :681, screen-not-a-route :696, missing-image :706, guide-is-a-stub :719, guide-missing-sections :733, undefined-concept :744, stale-generated-block :754, retired-path :777.
- **Sceptic:** The gaps are real, and the two stale lines are real: data-storage.md:58-59, and catalog-and-profiles.md:83, since the tree has only sync/import, pull and push under agent/profiles. The overstatement is weaker than claimed. A page's `screen:` becomes the in-app link, so 'a link to a route that does not exist' roughly matches screen-not-a-route. What docs/README.md:92-94 really omits is retired-path, guide-is-a-stub and guide-missing-sections. My own scan shows a naive gate would be noisy. Of 280 /api mentions it flagged 5, and all were tombstones or placeholders: testing.md:116 /api/benchmarks, api.md:47 /api/cron, settings.md:233 /api/agent/files/<key>, api.md:245 /api/agent/*, CONTRIBUTING.md:78 /api/.... Of 286 backticked paths it flagged 11, with false positives at output-canary.md:76-77 (example foo.ts), help.md:166 public/help (gitignored build output) and host-scheduling.md:40 (a path under HERMES_HOME). The repo convention of one refusal test per code (b15-docs-check-refusals.test.ts) adds test lines, and the opt-out markers add more, so +55 is low.
- **Proposal:** Add a 'stale-path' refusal to checkDocs for backticked repo paths and a 'stale-api-route' refusal for /api/... mentions, both resolved against the tree. The route walk already exists in extract.ts:161-189. Skip globs, and let a tombstone opt out with a marker. Correct docs/README.md:92-94 to list what docs:check actually refuses. Fix the two stale lines found.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- Findings docs-08 and docs-03 work against each other. docs-08 proposes deleting the schema-head and env-table extractors (scripts/docs/extract.ts:191-212, :275-317), yet the CHANGELOG schema number (CHANGELOG.md:22 says 41, head is 42) and three hand-typed lint counts (CONTRIBUTING.md:31, repo-guide.md:25/:33, testing.md:164) are exactly the drift those fences prevent. T-0109 records that B18 was meant to insert the markers, and only 2 of 9 were inserted. The consolidation should settle adopt-or-delete per extractor, not delete by default.
- The docs rewrite will hit source-text oracles. `git ls-files tests/unit | xargs grep -lE '"docs"|docs/[a-zA-Z-]+(/[a-z-]+)?\.md|CHANGELOG\.md|README\.md' | wc -l` = 36 unit suites that read doc or README text. Examples: tests/unit/u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts, b18-a-guide-is-finished.test.ts, b17-quest-integrity.test.ts:339, b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts. No finding inventories which docs edits turn which suite red, so a docs programme will be costed too low.
- The GitHub Pages publish path skips the link gate. .github/workflows/docs-pages.yml:16-17 runs `npm run docs:check` then `docs:build`, never check-doc-links, so a push to main can publish case-broken or missing links (the docs-01 set) without the link check. This ties to docs-11.
- The legal and brand root documents (REBRANDING.md:1-6, TRADEMARK.md, branding/README.md, branding/assets/README.md:1-6, branding/guidelines/COLORS.md) open with EOS front matter (summary/type/tags/compiled_from) and no title. GitHub renders that block as a metadata table above the legal text. No gate or finding covers front matter on root documents.
- scripts/docs/lib.mjs:90-94, the RETIRED_RE comment, justifies its lookbehind with the path `(main)/sessions/page.tsx`, a route group that no longer exists. That same stale path survives in docs/running/data-storage.md:58-59 and docs/CONTRIBUTING.md:62, so the exemption now shelters stale prose instead of a real source path.
- docs/guides/missions.md:128-130 ('use **Schedule a mission** in that same section') is a third stale Schedules sentence beyond the two docs-07 names.
- A naive stale-path or stale-API gate (docs-09) would currently flag tombstones and placeholders as failures: docs/contributing/testing.md:116 (/api/benchmarks, deleted by decision), docs/reference/api.md:47 (the /api/cron removal note), docs/guides/settings.md:233 (/api/agent/files/<key>), docs/contributing/output-canary.md:76-77 (example src/lib/foo.ts), docs/guides/help.md:166 (public/help, gitignored). Any such gate needs an explicit tombstone and placeholder syntax designed first.

### Not examined

- Whether GitHub Actions is currently red on Linux because of the six case-mismatched links (docs-01). I did not query Actions or use gh.
- Whether the 21 screenshots still match the current UI. Checking needs `npm run screenshots` (Playwright, binds a port), which the review rules exclude.
- Output of `npm run docs:build`: not run, because it writes site/ and public/help/. The local site/ is gitignored and dated Sep 6, so I did not inspect it as current.
- Read in full: docs/README.md, README.md, CHANGELOG.md, repo-guide.md, runtime-architecture.md, testing.md, CONTRIBUTING.md, SUPPORT.md, getting-help.md, adr/README.md, REBRANDING.md and the branding READMEs. The other ~60 pages (the 21 guides, 20 concepts and most running/reference pages) were checked by script for paths, routes, env vars, identifiers, npm scripts and bold labels, plus targeted greps, not read line by line.
- Behavioural claims in the guides (what a control does, counts such as '32 quests' or '~36 achievements') beyond label existence.
- design-tokens.md token values against globals.css. Only names were checked: 25 named, 2 absent, both the page's intentional warning about --ch-* variables.
- Content of the org/ pages the docs point to (org/START.md, org/EOS_OPERATORS_GUIDE.md, org/decisions/*); they exist but are outside this scope.
- Legal adequacy of NOTICE, TRADEMARK.md and branding/LICENSE.md.
- public/help/ output shape and the in-app Help resolver, apart from the one read path in src/lib/help/help-source.ts:97.

## Dimension: cross-cutting

On dev at ce4ac1fd, src is 100,881 lines and tests 121,114 (line census). Most type escape hatches sit in tests, not src. Tests have 306 `as unknown as`, 88 `as never` and 285 require-import lint directives; src has 10 casts, 2 explicit `any` and 11 lint directives. Configuration is the weakest seam. About 45 env names are read through six different spellings with five different yes/no vocabularies, 13 of them legacy aliases, and env-reference.md leaves out at least 12 names the code reads. Two diagnostics disagree with what the app actually does: the boot line's composer check, and the gateway URL reported when only PS_LLM_API is set. The only feature flag, composer, is not switched off by any shipped config, yet it costs 126 lines of flag code, 12 copied route guards and 343 lines of flag-specific tests. Errors reach a response in at least seven ways, including 62 raw NextResponse.json error bodies (49 in rec-room handlers). Server logging outside API routes is 28 bare console calls under about 15 tag spellings, and 242 catch blocks discard the error. Duplicated utilities are real but small: 7 duration formatters, 10 byte-size spellings, 4 compact-number copies, 3 escapeHtml copies with two escape sets, 3 markdown renderers, and 5 identical .env.local loaders in scripts whose precedence disagrees with the .mjs loader. The biggest cheap win is one eslint override for tests (about -280 test lines). The biggest wins that need a decision are retiring the CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ aliases (about -250 lines) or removing the composer flag (about -550).

### Measurements

- **src lines**: 100,881. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, counts.srcLines (every physical line of .ts/.tsx/.css/.mjs under src, comments included: line-census.mjs:54)`
- **test lines**: 121,114. Method: `same report, counts.testLines`
- **`as unknown as`**: src 10, tests 306, scripts 0. Method: `git grep -n -F "as unknown as" -- <dir> | wc -l`
- **eslint-disable lines**: src 12 matches (11 directives + 1 prose mention), tests 288 in 259 files (285 are @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports, all on their own line). Method: `git grep -n -F eslint-disable; rule grouping via grep -o and uniq -c; own-line check with grep -c '^\s*(//|/\*)'`
- **@ts-expect-error / @ts-ignore / @ts-nocheck**: src 0/0/0; tests 7 matches of which 3 are real directives (parse-schedule.test.ts:41, session-tiles-agree-with-header.test.tsx:206, set-field.test.ts:134), 0 ts-ignore. Method: `git grep -n -F per token, then reading each hit`
- **explicit any**: src 2, both eslint-disabled (api-fetch.ts:96, templates-handlers/shared.ts:30); tests 0 (all ': any' grep hits are prose in comments). Method: `git grep -n -E ':\s*any\b|<any>|\bas any\b' plus reading hits`
- **`as never` in tests**: 88 (top: mission-categories-route.test.ts 20, deleting-a-credential-is-careful.test.ts 12). Method: `git grep -c -E '\bas never\b' -- tests`
- **distinct env names read by the app**: about 45, of which 13 are legacy aliases (11 CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ in src, CH_ALLOWED_DEV_ORIGINS in next.config.ts, AGENT_HOME). Method: `union of process.env.X literals in src, readEnv(...) arguments, gateway-gate.ts env.X, next.config.ts; PING_URL excluded (inside a generated script template, script-templates.ts:55)`
- **distinct env names read via process.env in scripts, mocks, harness and root configs**: 39. Method: `git grep -o process.env.X over scripts mock-* test-harness playwright.config.ts jest.config.js next.config.ts | sort -u`
- **feature flags**: 1 (composer); 0 shipped config files set PS_COMPOSER. Method: `src/lib/feature-flags.ts:17; git grep -w PS_COMPOSER over scripts, docker, ops, Dockerfile, install.ps1, .env.example = 0`
- **Composer flag guards in API routes**: 12 identical 3-line blocks in 8 route files. Method: `git grep -n 'isFeatureEnabled("composer")' -- src/app/api`
- **console.* outside the logger**: 28 calls in 12 files. Method: `git grep -n -E 'console\.(log|warn|error|info|debug|trace)' -- src (34) minus api-logger.ts:16, 3 inside a generated script template, 2 comment mentions`
- **logApiError calls**: 77 in 53 files. Method: `git grep -n -E '\blogApiError\(' -- src excluding definition and imports`
- **API route files using route()**: 76 of 99. Method: `git ls-files 'src/app/api/**/route.ts' and grep -l -E '\broute\('`
- **raw NextResponse.json error bodies outside api-response.ts**: 62 (rec-room handlers 49, src/lib 8, src/app/api 4, proxy.ts 1). Method: `git grep -c -E 'NextResponse\.json\(\s*\{\s*(error|ok:\s*false)' -- src, minus the 9 inside api-response.ts`
- **catch blocks without / with an error binding**: 242 / 173. Method: `git grep -n -E 'catch\s*\{' and 'catch\s*\(\s*\w+(\s*:\s*\w+)?\s*\)\s*\{' -- src`
- **lines carrying legacy alias names**: src 41 (13 files), scripts 85 (30), tests 132 (31), docs 25 (5), test-harness 5 (4), root configs 7. Method: `git grep -n -E '\b(CH|CONTROL_HUB)_[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*|control-hub\.db|/control-hub/|x-ch-|ch\.sessions\.|ch-deploy|\bAGENT_HOME\b|LEGACY_KEY|legacyKey'`
- **test files named by programme batch**: 220 of 712 (b 147, u 64, c 9); these names are referenced 68 times (org 30, tests 29, src 5, docs 3, scripts 1). Method: `git ls-files tests | grep -E '/[a-z][0-9]{1,2}[a-z]?-[^/]+\.(test|spec)\.(ts|tsx)$'`
- **.env.local loaders**: 8: five TS copies in scripts/tooling, _env-local.mjs, bootstrap/env-local.mjs, lib/ps-dotenv-local.sh. Method: `git grep for loadEnvLocal/readEnvFile/ps_load_patterstage_env_local; md5sum of the extracted TS function bodies`
- **tests mocking the load-time paths module**: 33 jest.mock('@/lib/host/paths') files, 18 using pathsMock(). Method: `git grep -l over tests`
- **unit suites that read src source text**: 105. Method: `git grep -l readFileSync -- tests/unit, filtered to files naming a src/ path`

### Findings that survived verification (24 of 24)

#### cross-cutting-06 · 285 require-import lint directives in tests could be one eslint override

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, -280, adjusted
- **Files:** `eslint.config.mjs`, `jest.config.js`, `tsconfig.tests.json`, `tests/unit`, `tests/helpers/mocks.tsx`
- **Evidence:** git grep -F no-require-imports -- tests | wc -l returns 285. git grep -lF no-require-imports -- tests | wc -l returns 258. git grep -hF no-require-imports -- tests | grep -c eslint-disable-next-line returns 116. git grep -hF no-require-imports -- tests | grep '^\s*/\*' | grep -vc '\*/' returns 4 (multi-line blocks). git grep -ln eslint.config -- tests returns nothing. jest.config.js:1 carries the same directive. eslint.config.mjs has a single global rules block and no files: override. Net: -285 directive lines -1 jest.config, +6 config.
- **Sceptic:** Real and mechanical, with small count corrections. 285 directive lines in 258 files (not 259): 169 file-level and 116 next-line; the claimed 171+117=288 does not add up to 285. No directive line names another rule. Four block-comment directives span more than one line, so they need a hand edit rather than a line delete. No test reads eslint.config.mjs, and c5-comments-that-narrate.test.ts:57 counts directives in SRC only. Enforcement is unchanged because every site already suppresses the rule, but the CLAUDE.md 'never weaken a check' sign-off is right to keep.
- **Proposal:** Add one block to eslint.config.mjs: files tests/**/*.{ts,tsx} and jest.config.js turn off @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports, with a one-line reason pointing at tsconfig.tests.json. Delete the 285 directive lines and jest.config.js:1. The rule stays on for src and scripts.
- **Operator decision:** This switches a lint rule off for tests/**. CLAUDE.md forbids weakening a check. Every site already suppresses the rule, so enforcement does not change, but the operator should confirm the scoped override counts as tidying, not weakening.

#### cross-cutting-04 · Legacy CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ alias surface has no retirement date

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, high, -200, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/host/paths.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-auth.ts`, `src/lib/api/read-only.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/run-deadline.ts`, `src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/app/api/agent/profiles/sync/pull/route.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/agent-runtime.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/home.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-paths.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/deploy-status.ts`, `src/app/results/sessions/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useStoredBool.ts`
- **Evidence:** P='\b(CH_[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+|CONTROL_HUB_[A-Z0-9_]+|AGENT_HOME|x-ch-(ts|signature))\b|control-hub\.db|control-hub/data|ch-deploy\.status|ch\.sessions\.'; for d in src scripts tests docs test-harness; do git grep -nE "$P" -- $d | wc -l; done returns 31/75/124/21/5. git grep -lE 'CH_[A-Z]|CONTROL_HUB_' -- tests/unit | xargs grep -l readFileSync | wc -l returns 8. setup-hindsight.sh:424 is `PS_WEB_PORT="${CONTROL_HUB_PORT:-3000}"`. ps-rename-migrate.sh is 43 lines. deploy-status.ts:25-26 and :38-39 hold the ch-deploy.status fallback. Net: about 263 lines times 0.85, plus about 15 for the warning, so about -200.
- **Sceptic:** The alias surface is real and retirement is correctly an operator decision, but the line count is inflated. Recounted with one regex covering env aliases, AGENT_HOME, x-ch headers, control-hub.db, control-hub/data, ch-deploy.status and ch.sessions keys: 256 lines in src, scripts, tests, docs and test-harness, plus 7 in root configs, against 295 claimed. Many doc and comment lines change rather than go, and the proposed pre-retirement boot warning costs lines. The finder also missed that the five TS scripts read the data-dir aliases directly (process.env.CH_DATA_DIR x4, CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR x3), outside readEnv.
- **Proposal:** The operator sets a retirement release. Before it, ship a boot warning whenever an alias is the value that won. At it, delete the alias arguments, the raw `|| CH_` fallbacks, the x-ch headers, the control-hub.db and ~/control-hub discovery, the legacy storage keys and AGENT_HOME, plus their tests and doc rows. Keep ps-rename-migrate.sh one more release, and add PS_WEB_PORT handling for setup-hindsight.sh before removing CONTROL_HUB_PORT.
- **Operator decision:** Removes 12 env var aliases, AGENT_HOME, 2 request headers (x-ch-ts, x-ch-signature), the control-hub.db and ~/control-hub/data fallbacks, the ch-deploy.status fallback and 2 browser storage keys. Each changes documented behaviour (env-reference.md:13, migration.md:40-52 and 217). Installs that never ran the rename migration would lose their settings. Each removal needs sign-off.

#### cross-cutting-17 · Type escape hatches grouped by cause; test fetch doubles bypass the existing typed helpers

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** type-safety, medium, -100, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-fetch.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/create.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/update.ts`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`, `src/lib/db/upgrade.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/composer/dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/gateway-gate.ts`, `src/lib/secret-mask.ts`, `src/app/agent/models/page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useModelFallbackChain.ts`
- **Evidence:** git grep -n 'as unknown as' -- src | wc -l returns 10. git grep -n 'as unknown as' -- tests | wc -l returns 306. git grep -nw 'as never' -- tests | wc -l returns 88. git grep -n 'as unknown as typeof fetch' -- tests | wc -l returns 48. git grep -nE '(global|globalThis)\.fetch *=' -- tests | wc -l returns 127, in 55 files. git grep -n 'json: async () =>' -- tests | wc -l returns 100, in 54 files. git grep -l fetch-map -- tests | wc -l returns 11. git grep -n 'prepare<' -- src | wc -l returns 0. git grep -l 'from "zod"' -- src | wc -l returns 22. sed -n 163,164p src/hooks/useModelFallbackChain.ts shows the inline arrow; models/page.tsx:201 has useCallback(..., []).
- **Sceptic:** src figures confirmed: 10 casts in the five groups; anys at api-fetch.ts:97 and templates-handlers/shared.ts:31; 11 real directives, since shared.ts:28 is a comment. The models-page 'setters are stable' claim is confirmed wrong but harmless: useModelFallbackChain.ts:163-164 returns a fresh arrow each render, and it only calls a stable setter. Test figures: 306 `as unknown as` and 88 `as never` confirmed; `as unknown as typeof fetch` is 48, not 51; fetch assignments are 127 lines in 55 files counting global. and globalThis.; `json: async () =>` is 100 sites in 54 files, not 97 in 52. The -100 for (b) remains an unmeasured guess that overlaps the tests dimension, so it is kept with low confidence.
- **Proposal:** (a) Default apiFetch<T = unknown> and let callers name T. Type TemplateActionBody as a zod discriminated union; zod already has 22 src importers. Make enrichCustomTemplateFromDisk generic to drop 3 casts. Correct the models page directive reasons, or memoise setEditingFallbackEntry. (b) Add stubFetch(map) to tests/helpers/fetch-map.ts that assigns global.fetch and returns a typed jest.Mock, and adopt it where hand-built Response literals exist.

#### cross-cutting-08 · Five identical .env.local loaders in scripts disagree with the mjs and shell loaders on precedence and quotes

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -90, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/ensure-hermes-model-sync.ts`, `scripts/tooling/import-hermes-state.ts`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/tooling/retention-prune.ts`, `scripts/tooling/seed-catalog.ts`, `scripts/tooling/_env-local.mjs`, `scripts/bootstrap/env-local.mjs`, `scripts/lib/ps-dotenv-local.sh`, `scripts/tooling/ps-deploy.mjs`
- **Evidence:** For each of scripts/tooling/{ensure-hermes-model-sync,import-hermes-state,migrate-db,seed-catalog}.ts, extracting from `function loadEnvLocal` to the first `^}` gives 20 lines with md5 207b867b9b11. retention-prune.ts:46 is 19 lines and differs only by inlining `text`. The TS copies end with `if (!process.env[key]) process.env[key] = val;`. scripts/tooling/_env-local.mjs:8 is `const WHITELIST = /^(PS_[A-Z0-9_]+|CH_[A-Z0-9_]+|INSTALL_HERMES_[A-Z0-9_]+|HERMES_HOME)$/;` and :31 is `process.env[key] = val;`. git grep -ohE 'process\.env\.[A-Z_]+' on the five TS scripts counts PS_DATA_DIR 9, CH_DATA_DIR 4, CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR 3, HERMES_HOME 3. Net: -99 copies, +5 imports, +4 quote handling.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed: the loader is byte-identical in four TS scripts (md5 207b867b9b11, 20 lines) plus a 19-line variant in retention-prune.ts. The TS copies strip quotes and never override; _env-local.mjs overrides and keeps quotes. The finder missed a second behaviour change. _env-local.mjs whitelists only /^(PS_|CH_|INSTALL_HERMES_|HERMES_HOME)$/, yet the TS scripts read process.env.CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR (3 reads). Moving them onto the .mjs loader would silently stop honouring a CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR line in .env.local, and any other non-whitelisted key. That belongs in the operatorDecision alongside precedence and quoting.
- **Proposal:** The operator picks one precedence rule (shell wins, or .env.local wins) and one quote rule. Implement it once in scripts/tooling/_env-local.mjs; tsx resolves .mjs imports. Have the five TS scripts import it and delete their copies.
- **Operator decision:** Choosing one precedence rule changes which data directory one of the two script families (db:migrate/db:seed/db:retention, or ps-deploy) acts on when the shell env and .env.local disagree.

#### cross-cutting-15 · src/lib/utils.ts has a dead export, a near-duplicate JSON parser and a stale header

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -68, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/utils.ts`, `src/lib/db/parse-json.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-repository.ts`, `tests/unit/utils.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/api-test-helpers.ts`, `tests/unit/api-routes-simple.test.ts`, `knip.json`, `package.json`
- **Evidence:** grep -n '^export' src/lib/utils.ts shows 10 exports, with an import of @/lib/models/task-types at :148. The header at :4 says 'Four tiny helpers'. git grep -nE '\btruncate\(' -- src tests returns only tests/unit/utils.test.ts:208-230. git grep -lw safeJsonParse -- src returns mission-repository.ts (5 calls, :64-68) and utils.ts. git grep -l 'db/parse-json' -- src | wc -l returns 9. grep -n '^export' src/lib/api/api-logger.ts returns :13, :28, :48. utils.test.ts has describe('safeJsonParse') at :12-33 and describe('truncate') at :206-231. Net: src about -20, tests about -50.
- **Sceptic:** The dead truncate, single-consumer safeJsonParse and stale header are confirmed. Two evidence corrections. src/lib/db/parse-json.ts has 9 importer files, not 5. The two 'stale stubs' (api-test-helpers.ts:98, api-routes-simple.test.ts:100) sit inside jest.mock('@/lib/api/api-logger') factories and stub an export api-logger does not have (it exports logApiError, serverErrorFromCatch, serverErrorFromError), so they are stale independently of utils. `parseJson(x) ?? []` differs slightly: a stored 'null' yields [] instead of null, which is safer.
- **Proposal:** Delete truncate and its tests. Change mission-repository to `parseJson(x) ?? []`, then delete safeJsonParse, its tests and the 2 stale stubs. Move emptyModelDefaults to lib/models, a lib move that must cover all five path spellings. Fix the header. Consider adding lint:knip to lint in a later batch.

#### cross-cutting-07 · The only feature flag is never off in shipped config and is guarded by 12 copied blocks

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -18, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/feature-flags.ts`, `src/lib/feature-flags-guard.ts`, `src/hooks/useFeatureFlags.ts`, `src/app/api/feature-flags/route.ts`, `src/app/work/composer/layout.tsx`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/cancel/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/clarify/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/events/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/runs/[id]/nodes/[nodeId]/approve/route.ts`, `src/app/api/composer/workflows/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** git grep -c 'isFeatureEnabled("composer")' -- src/app/api/composer returns 12 across 8 files. wc -l src/lib/feature-flags.ts src/lib/feature-flags-guard.ts src/hooks/useFeatureFlags.ts src/app/api/feature-flags/route.ts src/app/work/composer/layout.tsx gives 126 total. wc -l tests/unit/{b1-composer-events-honour-the-flag,feature-flag-route-is-dynamic,feature-flags-route,feature-flags,sidebar-config-feature-flag}.test.ts gives 210, plus 133 for b19-memory-quest-can-be-unavailable.test.tsx. git grep -lE "jest.mock\([\"']@/(lib/feature-flags|hooks/useFeatureFlags|lib/feature-flags-guard)" -- tests | wc -l returns 14. grep -n 'feature flag' org/decisions/ADR-0005-product-modules.md returns :46. Option A: 36 guard lines become 12 one-line guards, helper +6, net -18. Option B stays about -550 and needs an ADR amendment.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed: 12 identical guards in 8 route files, 126 lines of flag files, and PS_COMPOSER set nowhere in shipped config. Option A is sound; each guard sits inside a route() handler, and route() takes no options (api-route.ts:42), so a composerOff() helper is the right shape. Test figures corrected: 343 is five flag-only suites (210 lines) plus b19-memory-quest-can-be-unavailable (133) counted in full, contradicting the 'b19 at half' method; 14 test files jest.mock the flag modules, not 15. The Option B sign-off is incomplete. ADR-0005, which is protected, lists 'enabled | A feature flag, so a module can ship dark', and modules/types.ts:49,67 featureFlag implements it. Removing the mechanism therefore needs an ADR, not just operator sign-off. org/plans/2026-09-final-release.md:426 (D5) also shows the SSE-route guard was added on purpose.
- **Proposal:** Option A (keep the flag): add `composerOff(): NextResponse | null` beside the flag and write each guard as `const off = composerOff(); if (off) return off;`. Also make firstEnvValue use readEnv (cross-cutting-03). Option B (remove the flag): delete the flag files, guards, consumer fields, the quest unavailable copy and the flag tests. Update env-reference.md:86, reference/api.md:105 and :128, guides/composer.md:224, guides/quests.md:173, reference/quests.md:125,127 and troubleshooting.md:143. api.md is pinned to the route tree by a test (T-0095, D129).
- **Operator decision:** Option B removes the PS_COMPOSER env var, the GET /api/feature-flags route, the documented 503/404 behaviour and quests 5.1/5.2 'switched off' copy. Option A needs no sign-off.

#### cross-cutting-13 · Ten byte-size spellings and four compact-number copies with two rounding rules

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -16, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/utils.ts`, `src/app/agent/settings/system/page.tsx`, `src/components/scripts/ScriptRow.tsx`, `src/lib/sync/sources/MemorySync.ts`, `src/app/agent/skills/[...path]/page.tsx`, `src/app/results/sessions/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/components/agents/AgentProfileFiles.tsx`, `src/components/session/MessageBubble.tsx`, `src/components/session/SessionCard.tsx`, `src/app/results/insights/page.tsx`, `src/components/achievements/AchievementBadge.tsx`, `src/components/agents/AgentPerformanceStrip.tsx`
- **Evidence:** git grep -nE '1024\)?\.toFixed|/ ?1024' -- src lists system/page.tsx:36-37 (humanSize), ScriptRow.tsx:18-19 (fmtSize), MemorySync.ts:24-26, skills/[...path]/page.tsx:99 (' KB') and :186 ('K'), sessions/[id]/page.tsx:156 (' KB'), AgentProfileFiles.tsx:45 ('KB'), session/MessageBubble.tsx:139 ('KB', content.length), SessionCard.tsx:113 (' KB'). grep -c '@/lib/utils' across the 12 files returns 1 only for ScriptRow, session/MessageBubble and SessionCard. Net: bytes about -11 copies +5 imports = -6; compact -20 copies +6 helper +4 imports = -10; total about -16.
- **Sceptic:** The copies and both rounding rules are confirmed: insights and AchievementBadge use `n % 1_000_000 === 0`; AgentPerformanceStrip and StatStrip use `n >= 10_000_000`. So are the six inline /1024 sites. The net is overstated: 9 of the 12 touched files do not import @/lib/utils today, so each swap adds an import line. session/MessageBubble.tsx:139 divides content.length (characters), not bytes, so formatBytes is the wrong label there.
- **Proposal:** Route every byte display through formatBytes. Add one compactNumber(n) beside it, with the operator choosing one rounding rule. With four consumers the helper pays for itself; the two-consumer caveat does not apply.

ProgressLine.tsx:26 has a private `function timeUntil(iso, now)` returning 'in 5m'/'now'. ElapsedSince.tsx:15 has a private h:mm:ss `format`. - **Proposal:** Keep formatRunDuration (ms) and timeAgo. Turn formatElapsed into a 3-line wrapper over formatRunDuration, or delete it and update its 2 callers. Rename ProgressLine's private helper (e.g. untilLabel) or reuse utils.timeUntil with an 'in ' prefix. Leave ElapsedSince's clock format, since it is a deliberate ticker.

#### cross-cutting-22 · Dependency and knip hygiene: misplaced @types, unused ts-jest hidden by a knip ignore, phantom ignores, redundant Next option

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, -6, confirmed

The knip schema is knip@5 while knip is ^6.16.1. Next 16.2.9 config.js:984-985 always merges lucide-react into optimizePackageImports. No test pins optimizePackageImports.
- **Proposal:** Move @types/js-yaml to devDependencies. Remove ts-jest and its knip ignore. Declare @jest/globals as a devDependency or drop that import, and remove the jsdom ignore. Update the knip schema URL. Delete the redundant experimental block in next.config.ts.
- **Operator decision:** Removes the ts-jest devDependency (no importer found). Confirm no external tooling relies on it.

#### cross-cutting-24 · Timestamp display mixes locale-dependent and fixed-UTC formatting

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 0, adjusted

- **Evidence:** git grep -ohE 'toLocale(String|TimeString|DateString)\(' -- src | sort | uniq -c gives 17 toLocaleString, 4 toLocaleTimeString, 2 toLocaleDateString, in 16 files. Number formatting examples: insights/page.tsx:212, 271, 297; StatStrip.tsx:53, 106, 142; StoryCard.tsx:56; AgentGrowthPanel.tsx:59, 64; research/page.tsx:89. git grep -nE 'toLocale(TimeString|DateString)\(|Date\([^)]*\)\.toLocaleString\(' -- src lists 8 timestamp sites: restore/page.tsx:232, 348; settings/system/page.tsx:190; skills/[...path]/page.tsx:102; chat/MessageBubble.tsx:185; SubsystemsPanel.tsx:71; QuestRow.tsx:73; ui/Toast.tsx:167. AutomationList.tsx:71-75 formatWhen is fixed ISO UTC.

The toLocale* calls are 22 in 16 files, and most are NUMBER formatting, not timestamps: 14 of 17 toLocaleString calls format counts (words, tokens, events, XP). A sweep of '23 sites' into a formatTimestamp would break number display. The timestamp sites are 8. Two of them, Toast.tsx:167 and restore/page.tsx:232 'Done at', show the viewer's own just-completed action, where local time is the correct answer. The real inconsistency is at most 6 sites against AutomationList's deliberate UTC, which is low value.
