#### cross-cutting-09 · TypeScript sources under scripts/ are type-checked by no gate; tsconfig excludes a file that does not exist

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 2, adjusted
- **Files:** `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.tests.json`, `scripts/tooling/migrate-db.ts`, `scripts/tooling/import-hermes-state.ts`, `scripts/tooling/seed-catalog.ts`, `scripts/tooling/retention-prune.ts`, `scripts/tooling/generate-json-schema.ts`, `scripts/tooling/ensure-hermes-model-sync.ts`, `scripts/docs/extract.ts`, `scripts/docs/check.mts`
- **Evidence:** tsconfig.json exclude is ["node_modules","tests","scripts","tmp","trace_import.ts"]; git ls-files | grep -c trace_import returns 0. Scratch tsconfig {extends: <repo>/tsconfig.json, compilerOptions: {noEmit, incremental:false, moduleDetection:force, plugins:[]}, include: [scripts/**/*.ts, scripts/**/*.mts], exclude: [node_modules]} run with node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc -p gives exit 2 with 4 errors TS5097. Adding allowImportingTsExtensions:true gives exit 0 with 0 errors. --listFilesOnly lists 12 scripts and 117 src files. package.json:18 lint runs `npx tsx scripts/docs/check.mts` and `npm run typecheck:tests` (-p tsconfig.tests.json).
- **Sceptic:** The gap is confirmed, and I measured the unknown cost. A scratch tsconfig outside the repo extended tsconfig.json and included scripts/**/*.ts and *.mts, checking 12 scripts files and 117 src files through their imports. It gives 4 TS5097 errors (scripts/docs/check.mts:29-31, scripts/docs/extract.ts:231: import paths ending in .ts), and 0 errors once allowImportingTsExtensions: true is set, which is legal under noEmit. The scripts are type-clean today, so the gate costs two config lines and no fixes. The proposal must add allowImportingTsExtensions to tsconfig.tests.json, or the gate turns red.
- **Proposal:** Add scripts/**/*.ts and scripts/**/*.mts to tsconfig.tests.json include, and drop trace_import.ts from tsconfig.json. Fix whatever errors appear in the same batch.

#### cross-cutting-21 · Browser storage uses five key-prefix conventions and four hand-rolled accessors beside useStoredBool

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 4, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/hooks/useStoredBool.ts`, `src/hooks/useSelectedProfile.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-composer-utils.ts`, `src/app/recroom/story-weaver/create/page.tsx`, `src/modules/rec-room/components/ReaderSettings.tsx`, `src/app/results/sessions/page.tsx`, `src/lib/quests/quest-latch.ts`
- **Evidence:** git grep -nE 'localStorage|sessionStorage' -- src, excluding the two SQL comments, gives 15: useStoredBool.ts 5, story-weaver/create/page.tsx 4 (:189, 207, 211, 489, with no try/catch), useSelectedProfile.ts 2, mission-composer-utils.ts 2, ReaderSettings.tsx 2. grep -n 'export function' src/lib/missions/mission-composer-utils.ts returns :21, :35, :45. sed -n 18,19p src/lib/quests/quest-latch.ts shows operator-prefs keys read through operator-prefs-repository. Net for the guard-only fix: about +4.
- **Sceptic:** The storage-call count is 15 in 5 files, not 17. The proposal does not fit two of the four sites. mission-composer-utils.ts exports plain functions (rememberLastCategory :35, readLastCategory :45), not hooks, so a useStoredValue hook cannot replace them without restructuring callers. The story-weaver draft is written on explicit actions, not reactive state. quest-latch keys ('quests.completedAt') are server-side DB operator prefs, not browser storage, so they are not a sixth storage convention. The concrete defect is the four unguarded story-weaver calls (create/page.tsx:189, 207, 211, 489), which can throw where storage is blocked. The smaller fix is to guard those and leave the keys alone; renaming keys resets saved preferences and would then need sign-off.
- **Proposal:** Generalise useStoredBool into useStoredValue<T>(key, fallback, legacyKey?) with a single 'ps.' prefix. Move the four direct accessors onto it and migrate their current keys through legacyKey.
- **Operator decision:** Renaming storage keys resets users' saved preferences and drafts unless each old key is migrated through legacyKey. Confirm the migration or accept the reset.

#### cross-cutting-01 · Boot line prints composer=on while the flag is off for PS_COMPOSER=off or no

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 6, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/deploy/boot-diagnostics.ts`, `src/lib/feature-flags.ts`, `tests/unit/boot-says-how-it-is-configured.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** sed -n 45,48p src/lib/deploy/boot-diagnostics.ts shows `const composer = composerRaw === "0" || composerRaw === "false" ? "off" : "on";`. sed -n 15p src/lib/feature-flags.ts shows `const FALSY = new Set(["0", "false", "no", "off"]);`. grep -n PS_COMPOSER docs/running/env-reference.md returns :86 '0 (or false/no/off)'. git grep -n PS_COMPOSER -- tests/unit/boot-says-how-it-is-configured.test.ts returns nothing. Net: src 0 (two lines become one, plus one import), tests +6.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. boot-diagnostics.ts:46-47 treats only 0/false as off. feature-flags.ts:15 FALSY is 0/false/no/off, and env-reference.md:86 documents all four. So PS_COMPOSER=off or no disables the routes (503) and the page (404) while the boot line prints composer=on. The boot test never sets PS_COMPOSER. No prior programme touched this, and the fix follows the file's own T-0095 rationale.
- **Proposal:** Replace lines 46-47 with `const composer = onOff(isFeatureEnabled('composer'));`. Add PS_COMPOSER=off and PS_COMPOSER=no cases to the boot test.

#### cross-cutting-02 · Runtime status and boot line report a gateway the runtime does not use when only PS_LLM_API is set

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 6, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/status/runtime-status.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/boot-diagnostics.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/agent-runtime.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/gateway.ts`, `src/instrumentation.ts`
- **Evidence:** grep -n DEFAULT_GATEWAY src/lib/status/runtime-status.ts src/modules/hermes/lib/agent-runtime.ts returns runtime-status.ts:33 and :77, agent-runtime.ts:25 and :39. boot-diagnostics.ts:48 is `readEnv("HERMES_GATEWAY_URL") ?? "default"`. agent-runtime.ts:31-44 derives gatewayBase from PS_LLM_API/CONTROL_HUB_LLM_API only when the value includes /v1/chat/completions. git grep -n getAgentGateway -- src/lib shows core consumers already. Proposal: in both reporters, use getAgentGateway().baseUrl (boot line may keep 'default' when it equals the default), delete DEFAULT_GATEWAY_URL and its comment, and add a unit case with only PS_LLM_API set. Net: src about -2, tests about +8.
- **Sceptic:** The bug is real but narrower than stated, and the proposed plumbing is unnecessary. getAgentLlmEndpoints only derives a non-default base when PS_LLM_API contains /v1/chat/completions (agent-runtime.ts:32-35). Set to a bare base, the gateway stays at the default. Passing the value in through the route is also not needed: core already has the sanctioned ADR-0005 port, src/lib/runtime/gateway.ts getAgentGateway(), which lib/models/gateway-client.ts:5 and llm.ts:6 import. runtime-status.ts and boot-diagnostics.ts can call getAgentGateway().baseUrl directly. The existing test (b3-runtime-status.test.ts:106-109) asserts only a non-empty string, so nothing pins the wrong value.
- **Proposal:** Report the resolved base. Core may not import a module (ADR-0005 rule 1; runtime-status.ts:60-62 notes that the route hands module facts in). So the status route should pass getAgentLlmEndpoints().gatewayBase alongside hermesHome, and instrumentation.ts should pass the same value into describeOperationalFlags. Then delete DEFAULT_GATEWAY_URL from runtime-status.ts and add a unit case with only PS_LLM_API set.

#### cross-cutting-10 · Errors reach responses in seven ways; 62 raw NextResponse.json error bodies bypass the status-locked factories

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 10, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/api/api-response.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-logger.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-route.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/library.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/generate.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/edit.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/crud.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/create.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/spend.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-auth.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/delete.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/update.ts`
- **Evidence:** git grep -n 'NextResponse.json({ *error' -- src | wc -l returns 71, 9 of them in api-response.ts. git grep -c on the same pattern gives rec-room library 14, generate 10, edit 10, crud 10, create 3, spend 2. git grep -n -A1 'NextResponse.json($' -- src | grep -c 'error:' returns 19. git ls-files 'src/app/api/**/route.ts' | wc -l returns 99, and 76 contain route(. grep -n 'export function' src/lib/api/api-response.ts shows no 401 factory. grep -n unauthorized src/proxy.ts returns :163.
- **Sceptic:** The per-file split of the 62 single-line raw bodies is confirmed, but the census misses 19 multi-line `NextResponse.json(\n { error:` bodies: sync/route.ts 3, deploy-actions.ts 3, proxy.ts 3, orchestration/chat 2, update 2, plus one each in admin/sessions/backfill-status, agent/files/[key], api-auth, sessions-api-guard, update-handlers/shared and rec-room crud. The true raw count is about 81. Adding `unauthorized` to api-response would clash with proxy.ts:163's local `unauthorized(request)`. Rec-room handlers import NextResponse directly today, and b14-story-handlers-pass-spend.test.ts:39 mocks NextResponse.json as the 'house double'; the factories call the same next/server export, so the mock still applies. Most touched files need a new import line, so the net is nearer +10 than +4.
- **Proposal:** Swap each raw body 1:1 for badRequest/notFound/forbidden/conflict/serverError/methodNotAllowed/payloadTooLarge/serviceUnavailable. Add `unauthorized(error)` for the three 401s in api-auth.ts. Leave proxy.ts alone if its runtime cannot import next/server helpers. ADR-0005 allows modules to import core, so rec-room may import these.

#### cross-cutting-11 · Server logging outside API routes is 28 bare console calls under about 15 tag spellings, with silent failure paths

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 12, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/instrumentation.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/scheduler/BackgroundScheduler.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/config-sync.ts`, `src/lib/seed/catalog-seed.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/seed-agent-catalog.ts`, `src/lib/sessions/session-sync.ts`, `src/lib/sessions/session-orphan-sweep.ts`, `src/lib/sessions/session-repository.ts`, `src/lib/retention/retention-prune.ts`, `src/lib/db/index.ts`, `src/lib/db/apply-cron-schedule-canonicalisation.ts`, `src/app/error.tsx`
- **Evidence:** git grep -nE '\bconsole\.(log|info|warn|error|debug)\(' -- src ':!src/app/api' returns 32 lines. Minus api-logger.ts:16 and script-templates.ts:39,60,64 leaves 28. Minus error.tsx:22 (client) and retention-prune.ts:184 (`options.log ?? ((line) => console.log(line))`) leaves 26. git grep -ohE 'catch *\{' -- src | wc -l returns 242. git grep -ohE 'catch *\( *[A-Za-z_]+[^)]*\)' -- src | wc -l returns 173. git grep -n 'logApiError(' -- src | grep -v 'export function' | wc -l returns 79, in 53 files.
- **Sceptic:** All 28 calls in 12 files re-derived; they exclude api-logger.ts and the three console calls inside the script-templates.ts template string. Two are not server logging. app/error.tsx:22 is a client error boundary writing to the browser console. retention-prune.ts:184 is the injectable line printer whose default prints `npm run db:retention` CLI output. So the sweep covers 26 calls in 10 files. The .catch(() => {}) sites (dispatch.ts:76,140; run-reconcile.ts:158,282), the 242 binding-less vs 173 bound catch counts, and the watchdog note (session-sync.ts:101-103) are confirmed.
- **Proposal:** Add a small serverLog(tag) returning {info, warn, error} in src/lib/logs with a closed tag list (subsystems, not function names). Convert the 28 calls and log the two dropped advances in dispatch.ts. Auditing the 242 binding-less catches is a separate sweep.
- **Operator decision:** Log line tags change (for example catalog-seed: becomes [seed]). An operator's log watcher may match today's strings; session-sync.ts:101-103 mentions a watchdog that reads this output.

#### cross-cutting-05 · env-reference.md claims every variable but misses at least 12 that code reads

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** doc-drift, low, 22, adjusted
- **Files:** `docs/running/env-reference.md`, `.env.example`, `scripts/tooling/ps-deploy.mjs`, `scripts/hardware/ps-log-rotate.mjs`, `scripts/lib/ps-deploy-status.sh`, `scripts/bootstrap/setup-hindsight.sh`, `playwright.config.ts`, `docker-compose.yml`, `src/lib/api/api-auth.ts`
- **Evidence:** for k in CH_SCRIPTS_DIR CH_HARDWARE_LOG_DIR CH_ENABLE_DEPLOY_API CH_READ_ONLY CH_REQUEST_SIGNING_SECRET CH_UPDATE_GIT_BRANCH CH_ALLOWED_DEV_ORIGINS PS_DEPLOY_STATUS_FILE PS_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS PS_RUNTIME_LOG PS_E2E_AUTH_TOKEN CONTROL_HUB_PORT; do grep -c -w $k docs/running/env-reference.md; git grep -l -w $k -- docs; done returns 0 in env-reference and nothing under docs for each. CH_DATA_DIR appears in docs/running/data-storage.md and migration.md. grep -n '(env-reference.md)' docs/running/env-reference.md returns :115, :129, :158. git grep -n -w CH_URL returns docker-compose.yml:9 and test-harness baseline-fix-verify.mjs:5, bench-gateway-e2e.mjs:14,17, poll-pair.mjs:2, poll-run.mjs:2. tests/integration/runtime/composer-smoke.mjs:25 reads `process.env.PS_URL || process.env.CH_URL`.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed: all 12 names are read by code and absent from env-reference.md, and the three self-links (lines 115, 129, 158) exist. Four corrections. CH_DATA_DIR is also documented in docs/running/data-storage.md. PS_E2E_AUTH_TOKEN is a test-only variable and belongs in testing docs, not the operator page. tests/integration already reads `PS_URL || CH_URL`, so test-harness should gain the same fallback rather than rename CH_URL, which would break anyone invoking the harness with CH_URL. The .env.example 'Default: true' reflects what setup writes (api-auth.ts:16-19 comment); it is misleading only for installs that predate that. docs/manifest.json is derived.
- **Proposal:** Add the missing rows (legacy aliases grouped under the naming note), fix the three self-links, and correct the .env.example default note. Rename CH_URL to PS_URL in test-harness and the compose comment. Enforce the page with the registry test from cross-cutting-03. docs/manifest.json is derived: regenerate it, never hand-edit.

#### cross-cutting-03 · Env vars read through six spellings with five boolean vocabularies and no key registry

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, medium, 34, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/host/paths.ts`, `src/lib/feature-flags.ts`, `src/lib/api/read-only.ts`, `src/lib/api/api-auth.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/boot-diagnostics.ts`, `src/app/api/agent/profiles/sync/pull/route.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/run-deadline.ts`, `src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/agent-runtime.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/gateway-gate.ts`, `src/lib/sessions/sessions-api-guard.ts`, `src/lib/help/help-source.ts`
- **Evidence:** git grep -n 'readEnv(' -- src | grep -v 'function readEnv' | wc -l returns 17 (api-auth 2, auth-token 4, read-only 1, db/backup 1, boot-diagnostics 3, paths 4, runtime-status 2). git grep -nE 'process\.env\.(PS_|CH_|CONTROL_HUB_)[A-Z_]+ *\|\| *process\.env\.(CH_|CONTROL_HUB_)' -- src next.config.ts returns 5 sites (next.config.ts:9, pull/route.ts:52, run-deadline.ts:23, update-handlers/shared.ts:27, agent-runtime.ts:31). git grep -l "jest.mock(['\"]@/lib/host/paths" -- tests | wc -l returns 32. git grep -l 'pathsMock(' -- tests | wc -l returns 18. help-source.ts:38-39 reads PS_HELP_DIR at call time.
- **Sceptic:** The vocabularies are confirmed: read-only 1/true (read-only.ts:33); deploy 1/true/yes and 0/false/no (api-auth.ts:24-25); flags 0/false/no/off; boot 0/false; pull '1' only (pull/route.ts:52). The raw PS||CH sites, intFrom (gateway-gate.ts:57) and three Number.isFinite parses (sessions-api-guard.ts) are confirmed too. Two counts are slightly off: readEnv has 17 call lines in 7 src files (not 14), and 32 test files jest.mock @/lib/host/paths (not 33). next.config.ts already imports ./src/lib/config/config-sections, so a src/lib/host/env.ts is importable there. The net size and the operatorDecision stand.
- **Proposal:** Create one src/lib/host/env.ts with readEnv (moved from paths.ts), envFlag(keys, default) and envInt(keys, default, min). Add an ENV_KEYS table (name, aliases, default, doc anchor) that the readers use. A unit test diffs that table against docs/running/env-reference.md. Route the sites above through it and delete firstEnvValue, intFrom and the three session parses. Moving readEnv out of paths.ts is a lib move, so account for all five path spellings.
- **Operator decision:** Unifying the vocabularies changes documented behaviour. env-reference.md documents PS_READ_ONLY as `1` and PS_ENABLE_DEPLOY_API as 0/false/no. The operator picks the single truthy/falsy set, e.g. whether PS_READ_ONLY=yes should start refusing writes.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- Error helpers live in a client module. src/lib/api/api-fetch.ts:1 says 'the one API fetch in the client, and the error helpers beside it', but 50 src files import it. Server-side importers include src/lib/api/api-logger.ts:4, composer/dispatch.ts, orchestration/dispatch.ts, orchestration/chat-dispatch.ts, memory/hindsight-client.ts, runtime/gateway-error.ts, sessions/session-sync.ts, status/subsystems.ts and 7 API routes. Splitting toError/messageFromError into a server-safe errors module is a lib move (five path spellings). Re-run: git grep -l 'from "@/lib/api/api-fetch"\|from "./api-fetch"' -- src | wc -l returns 50. Net about +5.
- ISO-timestamp helper sits beside the DB driver. src/lib/db/index.ts:133 exports now() returning new Date().toISOString(), while 46 inline `new Date().toISOString()` calls remain in 34 src files. Only 4 of those files import @/lib/db (ProcessSync, stats-repository, catalog-seed, retention-repository). A time helper that drags in better-sqlite3 cannot be shared, so it belongs in a dependency-free module. Re-run: git grep -n 'new Date().toISOString()' -- src | wc -l returns 46. Net about 0 (1:1 swaps).
- The five TS scripts carry their own data-dir alias chains outside readEnv: process.env.CH_DATA_DIR x4 and CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR x3 across migrate-db, seed-catalog, import-hermes-state, retention-prune and ensure-hermes-model-sync. cross-cutting-04 does not count these sites, and the scripts/tooling/_env-local.mjs:8 whitelist omits CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR, so the loader unification in cross-cutting-08 would silently drop that alias. Re-run: git grep -ohE 'process\.env\.[A-Z_]+' -- scripts/tooling/*.ts | sort | uniq -c.
- Scripts are type-clean, so the gate is nearly free. Measured with a scratch tsconfig extending tsconfig.json over scripts/**/*.ts and *.mts (12 scripts files, 117 src files): 0 errors with allowImportingTsExtensions:true, and 4 TS5097 errors without it (scripts/docs/check.mts:29-31, extract.ts:231). This turns cross-cutting-09 from 'unknown fixes' into two config lines.
- Fetch-timeout spellings: 5 `AbortSignal.timeout(` sites against 7 manual `setTimeout(() => controller.abort()` sites in src. The manual form needs its own clearTimeout bookkeeping. Re-run: git grep -nE 'AbortSignal.timeout\(' -- src | wc -l; git grep -nE 'setTimeout\(\(\) *=> *(controller|ac|ctrl)\.abort' -- src | wc -l. Net small, about -10.
- src/proxy.ts has 3 more multi-line raw error bodies and its own local `unauthorized(request)` at :163, which any shared 401 factory (cross-cutting-10) must not shadow. It also carries a third escapeHtml copy (:120). Proxy-specific response helpers are a separate small tidy.
- ~/.hermes and HERMES_HOME defaults are repeated in shell and python as well as TS: scripts/bootstrap/hindsight-server.py:13, setup-hindsight.sh:206,211,304, hardware/reconnect-hindsight.sh:98, lib/ps-env.sh:105,221-222, hardware/ps-backup.sh:39-40, lib/ps-deploy-status.sh:4. The retirement and default work in cross-cutting-04 and -20 should inventory these too. Re-run: git grep -nE '\.hermes' -- scripts.

### Not examined

- Bundle size was not measured. It needs `next build`, which this review may not run. Only static import checks were done (no zod or js-yaml in 'use client' files; @xyflow/react deferred via next/dynamic).
- knip was not run (heavy on a shared machine), so the dead-export and unused-dependency claims come from grep.
- tsc over scripts/**/*.ts was not run, so the number of latent type errors behind cross-cutting-09 and the caller errors behind apiFetch<T = unknown> are unknown.
- No server or jest suite was run. The boot-line bug (cross-cutting-01) and the gateway mismatch (cross-cutting-02) come from reading the code.
- The 242 catch blocks without an error binding were counted, not individually classified as intentional or swallowing.
- Env reads in Python (tests/integration/*.py) and shell scripts were scanned with a ${VAR} regex only. Shell-internal variables such as PS_C_CYAN were not separated from operator config, and a regex cannot tell which shell names are exported.
- Client-side error presentation (toasts, banners, inline error text) was not inventoried for duplicate spellings.
- lib-to-modules imports (6 in 5 files) were not re-examined: ADR-0005 records them as measured exceptions, and the lib reviewer owns them.
- org/decisions ADRs were not read in full, apart from ADR-0005's dependency rule. I did not confirm whether any ADR sets a lifetime for the rename aliases.
- CSS token and class naming was out of scope; the UI programme U0-U20 covers it.
- Docs and org content beyond environment-variable references were not reviewed; other dimensions cover them.

## Dimension: org

org/ has 200 tracked files. The 145 task records come to 6,991 lines and 902,747 bytes; the other 55 files come to 12,264 lines. About 3,000 of those lines are living governance. The rest is dated history: plans 2,551, reviews 2,655, decisions 1,545, Session-0 and compile artefacts 1,363, and never-filled EOS forms 524. The line census does not count org/ at all. The most serious problems are governance. T-0144 edited org/policy.json and two accepted ADRs under an R2 ruling that mentions none of them, while T-0094 ruled the same kind of change R3. Batches C4 to C8 also ran 5 to 10 parallel agents against a policy cap of 2 lanes, with no claims file. The task records do not keep the shape TEMPLATES.md promises. 54 records ruled above R0 have no reasons, 53 standard-mode records sit at R2, and 232 of 274 top-level keys appear in only one record. No in-repo check reads more than five fields. The landing steps are visible in history (21 of 32 recent records have an oracle commit), but the mutation sweep, the codemods and the agent briefs lived in a session scratchpad. The view renderer comes from a sibling checkout 6 commits past its pin, and no git hook or CI job enforces any step. Living documents contradict each other and the tree: HANDOVER and the closed plan both say batches remain, 15 path mentions in living docs point at pre-move locations, and QUESTIONS.md keeps an answered question open. Moving or archiving files saves no lines and breaks at least four suites. The real line savings are about 40 stale lines, 524 lines of blank forms (needs an ADR) and the dead 248-line eos-compile script.

### Measurements

- **org tracked files**: 200 (145 task records, 55 other). Method: `git ls-files org | wc -l; git ls-files org | awk -F/ '{print $2}' | sort | uniq -c`
- **org non-task lines**: 12,264. Method: `git ls-files org | grep -v '^org/tasks/' | xargs wc -l`
- **org non-task lines by kind**: living governance 3,012; derived views 195; HANDOVER 171; blank EOS forms 524 (ACCEPTANCE_SPINE 94, PRODUCT_MAP 116, genesis 314); COMPILE_REPORT 617; eos-session0 746; logs 248; plans 2,551; reviews 2,655; decisions 1,545. Method: `sum of the per-file wc -l above grouped by folder and front-matter kind`
- **task records size**: 6,991 lines, 902,747 bytes, median 5,702 bytes, largest T-0057 17,186 bytes. Method: `node script over org/tasks/T-*.json (Buffer.byteLength, split on newline)`
- **task records over the TEMPLATES.md 40-line budget**: 96 of 145. Method: `count org/tasks/*.json with more than 40 lines`
- **distinct top-level record keys**: 274, of which 232 appear in exactly one record; 11 core keys appear in all 145. Method: `node: Object.keys per record, frequency table`
- **records ruled above R0 with an empty reasons array**: 54 (T-0031 to T-0092). Method: `node: tier_ruled != R0 && reasons.length === 0`
- **standard-mode records by tier**: R0 27, R1 57, R2 53. Method: `node: group mode==='standard' by tier_ruled`
- **high-assurance records with owed artefacts**: 8 records; oracle_provenance 2; rollback 2; verdicts 0. Method: `node: mode==='high-assurance', key presence`
- **non-done task records**: 10: active 2 (T-0003, T-0004), in_progress 1 (T-0113), proposed 5, discarded 2. Method: `node: status !== 'done'`
- **task records naming a scratchpad path**: 17 records (e.g. T-0142, T-0143, T-0144). Method: `node: regex /AppData.Local.Temp|scratchpad|\/tmp\// over every string value`
- **dead path mentions in org (excluding tasks)**: 187 of 791 distinct per-file mentions; about 15 in living docs (LOCKBOOK, QUESTIONS, TESTING, PLAYBOOKS, GRAPH_BUILD, VENTURE_BRIEF, policy.json). Method: `regex for (src|tests|scripts|docs|org|ops|...)/path in org md/json, existsSync per mention; spot-check with git grep -n -E 'docs/(LOCKBOOK|RULINGS|VENTURE_BRIEF|EOS_FEEDBACK|COMPILE_REPORT|TESTING)\.(md|json)' -- org ':!org/tasks'`
- **test files reading an org path**: 15. Method: `git grep -l -E "\"org\"|'org'|org/" -- tests | wc -l`
- **records T-0114..T-0145 with an oracle or red-first test commit subject**: 21 of 32 (none for T-0114 to T-0124). Method: `git log --format=%s | grep T-01xx | grep -E '^test: .*(oracle|red at)'`
- **git hooks or CI steps enforcing the landing discipline**: 0 (no .husky, no core.hooksPath, no active .git/hooks, no .github reference to org/ or check-derived-views). Method: `ls .husky; git config --get core.hooksPath; ls .git/hooks; git grep -n -E 'org/|check-derived|census' -- .github`
- **cadence rows ever run**: 0 of 5 (last_run null); upkeep next_due 2026-09-05 has passed. Method: `cat org/cadence.json`
- **EOS checkout drift from lock-book pin**: HEAD 1d206078 is 6 commits past pin 727bee44; tools/eos/taskops.py unchanged, cli.py +95 lines, cli.py:651 still render_views(REPO). Method: `git -C ../PatterTech_EOS rev-list --count 727bee44..HEAD; git diff --stat 727bee44..HEAD -- tools/eos kernel/schemas`
- **line census coverage of org/**: not counted. Method: `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs:50-51 walks src and tests only`

### Findings that survived verification (17 of 19)

#### org-11 · eos-compile.mjs is a 248-line script that refuses to run, hard-codes a personal path, and is still pinned by a test

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, medium, -259, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/eos-compile.mjs`, `tests/unit/b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts`, `org/COMPILE_REPORT.md`, `org/VENTURE_BRIEF.md`
- **Evidence:** - `wc -l scripts/tooling/eos-compile.mjs` gives 248. :141-177 refuse with process.exit(1). - :24 reads `process.env.EOS_ROOT || "C:/Users/Daniel/..."`, a fallback default. - `grep -n eos package.json` returns nothing. - `git grep -l eos-compile` hits org docs, T-0057, T-0109, b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts (:138-150, two its, 13 lines) and b15-docs-pipeline-is-wired.test.ts:5 (a comment, which the reviewer missed). - COMPILE_REPORT.md item 5 (about :563) says it 'needs a v2 pass or a retirement note'. - ADR-0010:103-106 records the refusal as the guard until the estate moves the matrix rows. Deleting the script also removes any compile, so the guard's purpose still holds. - line-census.mjs:50-51 does not walk scripts/, so the census-visible saving is only the 13 test lines.  Net: -248 script, -13 test, -1 comment, +3 note, about -259 repo lines (-13 census lines).
- **Sceptic:** The script is dead and self-refusing, as claimed. The line saving is mostly outside the census, it has one more consumer, and the path is an environment-variable default rather than a hard-code.
- **Proposal:** Retire it: delete the script and the two b15 assertions, write the retirement note COMPILE_REPORT item 5 asks for, and point VENTURE_BRIEF at that note. EOS_FEEDBACK.md:38 already files the friction. Net: -248 script, -13 test, +3 doc, so about -258.
- **Operator decision:** Removes a CLI script that ADR-0010:107-109 names as the guard against a compile writing to the old paths. Deleting it is also a seed-ancestry matter for the estate. The operator signs off.

#### org-05 · HANDOVER.md contradicts itself, repeats the plan's table, and is outside the boot path and over its budget

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** doc-drift, low, -24, adjusted
- **Files:** `org/HANDOVER.md`, `org/START.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- **Evidence:** Confirmed: - HANDOVER.md:114 says '**C7, C8** remain; C7's starting notes are below'. Rows :44-45 say both landed, :146 says 'The programme is closed', and no C7 notes exist. - :158 says 'The open items below', but they sit at :112-142. - :166-171 restate :154-157. - :141-142 are walk artefacts. - `git grep -l HANDOVER -- ':!org/tasks'` returns only org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md. - The reading order (:15-25) totals 1,123 lines by `wc -l`, against the START.md:20 Standard budget of 550. That budget is for a task boot, not a handover read, so the comparison is indicative only.  Refuted: HANDOVER.md:52 heads that column 'Plan start', and :55's 121,651 equals org/reviews/2026-09-consolidation-recon.md:35 and plan:68. The 121,762 at plan:237 and c8 AT_C0 is the first committed baseline, `git show 4c5d37c9:scripts/tooling/line-census.baseline.json`, which includes C0's own oracle. Both numbers are true at different times.  New inconsistency: HANDOVER.md:66 gives design-lint debt at 350 to 0, while plan:249 says 'design-lint 369 violations to **0**'.  Net: delete :114 (1 line), :141-142 (2) and :166-171 (6), and replace the 18-line table at :50-67 with a pointer, keeping the jest row the plan lacks (about -15). Total about -24.
- **Sceptic:** The in-file contradictions are real. The claimed census disagreement is not: HANDOVER's column is 'Plan start', and 121,651 was the true figure at plan time. The table has a different inconsistency, in the design-lint row.
- **Proposal:** Delete line 114, the Release duplicate (166-171) and the walk-artefact item (141-142). Fix 'below' to 'above'. Replace the census table with a one-line pointer to the plan's closing section, which the c8 test holds. Move the landing procedure out (see org-06). Then decide whether HANDOVER is a boot file, and name it in START.md within budget, or a transient note replaced at the next programme's start. Net about -25 lines (1 + 6 + 2 + 16).
- **Operator decision:** Only if the boot sequence in AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md/START.md is changed to name HANDOVER, since that changes documented behaviour. The in-file corrections need none.

#### org-08 · The closed consolidation plan still says C6 to C8 remain and gives two C0 testLines numbers

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** doc-drift, low, -6, adjusted
- **Files:** `org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md`
- **Evidence:** - Plan :5 says status done and :226 says 'Closed at C8 (T-0145) on 2026-09-11'. :28-34 still say 'C6, C7 and C8 remain'. - :68 heads its column 'Now' at plan-writing time, and 121,651 matches org/reviews/2026-09-consolidation-recon.md:35. - :234-237 head theirs 'C0' from baseline 4c5d37c9 (`git show 4c5d37c9:scripts/tooling/line-census.baseline.json` gives testLines 121762, which includes C0's oracle). - Neither c0-the-line-census.test.ts:111-124 nor c8:117-129 reads :28-34.  Delete :28-34 (7 lines). At most add a one-line footnote at :68; do not change the number. Net -6.
- **Sceptic:** The stale Progress section is real. The 'two C0 numbers' are two different measurement points, so correcting line 68 would falsify a historical figure.
- **Proposal:** Delete the Progress section (7 lines). Correct or footnote line 68 to the committed C0 baseline 121,762. Both edits are outside what the c0 and c8 suites read. org/plans/ is listed as reversible in policy.json path_patterns. Net -7.

#### org-15 · The C8 suite ties jest to a closed plan's prose, so the next programme must edit a closed record to stay green

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** test-debt, medium, -5, adjusted
- **Files:** `tests/unit/c8-the-programme-is-closed.test.ts`, `tests/unit/u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts`, `tests/unit/c0-the-line-census.test.ts`, `tests/unit/b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts`, `org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md`
- **Evidence:** - c8-the-programme-is-closed.test.ts:111-125 compares the plan's closing numbers with scripts/tooling/line-census.baseline.json. Once a later --update-baseline lowers srcLines, it fails until the closed plan is edited. - :94-97 fail once any MISSED measure reaches target. - :104-110 calls this 'the coupling that should exist', so the design is deliberate. - u16-the-docs-describe-the-system.test.ts:119-136 and c0-the-line-census.test.ts:111-124 pin literals in closed plans that no future batch needs to change, so they do not block. - `git grep -l -E '"org"|org/' -- tests | wc -l` gives 15 files mentioning an org path.  Net: replace the live baseline read with a frozen AT_C8 constant (about 7 lines) and a short comparison (about 5), removing 15 and rewording 4, about -5.
- **Sceptic:** Only the c8 suite couples a closed plan to a live artefact, and its comment says the coupling is deliberate. The u16, c0 and b15 pins read closed text that never changes and block nothing. The net saving is smaller than claimed.
- **Proposal:** Before the next programme lowers the line-census baseline, have the operator approve re-anchoring c8. Replace the live baseline read at :111-125 with the closing numbers frozen in the test, and replace :94-97 with a check that the MISSED list matches what the plan recorded at C8. The closed plan and closed test then stay closed while the census keeps falling. Net about -15 test lines.
- **Operator decision:** Changes what an existing check asserts, which is the escalation band 'weakening any check'. Needs operator approval even though the intent is to re-anchor, not weaken.

The wider scope: `git show --stat --format= cdf95b10 -- org` lists 55 files. They include: - 38 historical task records, T-0008 to T-0140; - org/eos-session0/CORRECTIVE_RAW.json, WALK_RAW.json and fills.json; - a dated org/EOS_FEEDBACK.md entry (src/lib/api-auth.ts becomes src/lib/api/api-auth.ts); - the closed plans and reviews. They include historical task records and the Session-0 ancestry artefacts, both of which ADR-0010 section 3 says are not rewritten.
- **Proposal:** Do not let an agent settle this. Put it to the operator as an escalation item with two options: (a) a short ADR that retrospectively sanctions the three mechanical path corrections and records the exception, or (b) restore the accepted ADR text and handle the policy.json path update as its own R3 record with recorded approval. ADR prose describes the tree at acceptance and stays as written, as ADR-0010 section 3 already rules for records. A needed policy.json path change becomes a separate protected-set task. Net lines 0 (a process rule, not a code change).
- **Operator decision:** Protected set and escalation band. The operator must rule on the three landed edits (sanction by ADR or restore) and approve the codemod exclusion rule before any further move batch.

#### org-16 · Task record size and key sprawl: 96 of 145 exceed the 40-line template, and 232 of 274 keys are used once

The documents: - TEMPLATES.md:21 says 'Forty lines is the budget', and :58-64 admits the drift. - EOS_FEEDBACK.md:67-68 records that task-record.schema.json sets additionalProperties false, so an extension set (invariants, commits, verification ...) needs an estate schema change or a recorded deviation.  No basis was found for the 40% byte-reduction estimate. Net 0 now.

Document it in TEMPLATES.md, have the org-03 check enforce it, and keep the narrative in one place (the record or the commit body, not both). Historical records are untouched. Net 0 lines now. Future records should be smaller, roughly 40% by bytes if held to the template budget, which is an estimate from the median against the template.

Corrected: - Oracle-first. `git log --format=%s dev | grep T-01xx | grep ^test` finds at least one test commit for all 32 records T-0114 to T-0145. 25 of 32 name 'oracle' or 'red at': T-0114 to T-0117 and T-0125 to T-0145. For example, 52a324f0 is 'test(design): U0's oracle ... T-0114'. T-0118, T-0121 and T-0124 have test commits without the word. The claim that T-0114 to T-0124 have none is false. - Enforcement. .github/workflows/ci.yml:80 and :149 run `npm run lint`, whose package.json script includes check-derived-views.mjs, so TASKS.md-versus-records drift and the docs manifest are CI-enforced. What no workflow runs: `grep -n census .github/workflows/*.yml` returns nothing, so census:lines, census and any mutation sweep run nowhere in CI.  The proposal stands.
- **Sceptic:** The gap is real, but two supporting facts are wrong. Every record from T-0114 to T-0145 has a test commit, and CI does enforce the derived-view step through npm run lint.
- **Proposal:** Bring the reproducible parts into the repo: (a) scripts/tooling/mutation-sweep.mjs, reading a committed mutants file per task (path to be ruled, e.g. org/mutants/T-####.json; not inside org/tasks, whose checker filters T-####.json). Each mutant carries anchor, replacement and the suite expected to fail. It runs against a clean checkout of HEAD. (b) npm run gate (scripts/tooling/gate.mjs) running HANDOVER's nine commands in order by exit code and stamping the tree before and after. (c) a record field naming the EOS commit the views were rendered with. Do not vendor taskops: that is an estate distribution decision (ADR-0003). Net: about +200 script lines, minus about 25 lines of HANDOVER prose these replace, so +175.
- **Operator decision:** Adds new npm commands (gate) and a new committed artefact type under org/, and changes the documented landing procedure. The operator signs off on each.

### Refuted, and why

- **org-10 · 524 lines of EOS forms never filled (acceptance spine, product map, genesis) after Genesis-lite was discarded.** Deleting the five forms breaks the EOS seed contract that the lock-book adopts. They are required seed files at ORG scale, and the blank state is documented as legitimate. The lines are not counted by the line census either.
- **org-13 · policy.json guard.mapping_ref points at org/guard-mapping.json, which does not exist.** A mapping_ref naming an unshipped file, with validated false, is exactly the shape the EOS sanctions. Nulling it would fail the seed check and the policy schema.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- The T-0144 codemod rewrote 55 files under org/, not the 3 protected ones. `git show --stat --format= cdf95b10 -- org` lists 38 historical task records (T-0008 to T-0140), the Session-0 ancestry artefacts (org/eos-session0/CORRECTIVE_RAW.json, WALK_RAW.json, fills.json), a dated entry in org/EOS_FEEDBACK.md, and the closed plans and reviews. ADR-0010 section 3 (lines 121-122) and b15-corpus-moves-under-org.test.ts:17-18 say task records are not rewritten, and ADR-0010:142-145 names the Session-0 files as the seed's proof of ancestry. T-0109 (41ddb25b) also edited a dated EOS_FEEDBACK line, changing docs/RULINGS.json to org/RULINGS.json. The next programme's five-spellings codemod needs an exclusion list covering all of these, not only the protected set.
- ADR-0010 section 2 (lines 103-106) promised that org/EOS_FEEDBACK.md would gain an entry asking the estate to move the matching kernel/SCALE_MATRIX.md rows. The entry never landed: `grep -n -E '^- \`2026-09' org/EOS_FEEDBACK.md` finds no 2026-09 entries, and grepping for ADR-0010, 'matching rows' or 'old homes' finds nothing. Meanwhile the seed is out of conformance with its own check. seed.py:680-685 requires `rulings_record: docs/RULINGS.json`, and seed.py:677 reads seed/docs/RULINGS.json, but the file lives at org/RULINGS.json. SCALE_MATRIX.md:43-47 still names the docs/ paths of the Genesis forms. The venture owes the estate a feedback entry. Net about +10 lines, no protected-set edit.
- No CI workflow runs the shrink-only line census, the design census or a mutation sweep: `grep -n census .github/workflows/*.yml` returns nothing. package.json:30-32 defines census and census:lines, but CI runs only `npm run lint` (ci.yml:80 and :149), and lint does not include them. The ratchet that refereed C0 to C8 holds only while the landing agent runs it by hand, so a PR from any other route can grow src or tests unchecked. Adding census:lines to the lint job costs about 3 CI lines. It is a CI-config change, which policy.json:16 floors at ci-stateful-infra R2.
- HANDOVER.md:66 gives design-lint debt as 350 at plan start, while org/plans/2026-09-consolidation.md:249 says 'design-lint 369 violations to **0**' and the plan's own C0 row at :79 gives 222/24/77/20. Three figures for one baseline appear across two living documents, and no test holds any of them.
- STATE.md's operator-flag gap is wider than T-0113. T-0003 (a dirty worktree awaiting a decision), T-0004 (branch protection, operator only, by its own intent) and T-0113 all wait on the operator, but their statuses are active or in_progress, so the renderer (flags blocked and in-review only, COMPILE_REPORT.md:164) prints 'Nothing waits on the operator' at STATE.md:19. That is 3 records, not 1.

### Not examined

- Did not read the full text of all 145 task records, the 7 reviews or the 4 plans. Records were measured by script, and plans and reviews by front matter, status sections and grep.
- Did not verify the bodies of ADR-0001 to ADR-0006 and ADR-0009 against the code beyond the path-existence scan and the T-0144 diff.
- Did not validate RULINGS.json, eos-session0/*.json or the task records against the EOS JSON schemas; that needs running python -m tools.eos from the sibling checkout, which this review did not do.
- Did not run check-derived-views.mjs, check-doc-links.mjs or the c0/c8/u16/b15 jest suites to confirm they are green now; conclusions come from reading their source.
- Did not check the LOCKBOOK.md body (360 lines; only the header and part of the Tokens section read) or its rulings against the code.
- Did not confirm whether the EOS seed check accepts an org/ path in LOCKBOOK's rulings_record before proposing the change in org-07.
- Did not inspect GitHub-side state (branch protection, remote branches beyond git branch -a) that bears on T-0003.
- Did not review the user's auto-memory files outside the repository, although they restate HANDOVER content.
- Did not assess EOS_FEEDBACK.md entries one by one for whether the estate resolved them after the pin, beyond the task views friction.

## Dimension: lib-domains

The in-scope tree is 288 TypeScript files and 36,345 lines (src/modules alone is 10,533). Seven files are 465 to 608 lines long: config-schema, the composer engine, quest-defs, models-repository, chat-utils, HermesRuntime and profile-config-builder. The C7 moves put every file in a domain, but several domain boundaries are still wrong. There are three domain-less *-handlers folders beside two other handler conventions. Laboratory was named a module in ADR-0005 but still lives in core, and Composer imports it directly. Hermes-shaped data also sits in core: the agent_root repository (12 of its 15 importers are in the hermes module) and the 336-line Settings field table. 94 of the 288 files have exactly one non-test importer. 15 exports (about 178 lines, counting utils.truncate) are reached only by tests, because knip treats test files as entry points and does not run in npm run lint. The biggest shrink left is duplication the line census cannot see, since it only counts a window repeated across files. Within-file repeats turn up in 15 files. The mission promote and update handlers each list the same 17 fields twice. Four data shapes are declared twice, and ChatMessage has already drifted between its two copies. Two real defects surfaced on the way. A Stop during llm.ts's retry backoff waits up to 60 seconds. The chat markdown renderer rewrites the inside of fenced code, so the Copy button copies markup. Legacy naming is mostly documented back-compat (14 CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ env names, the control-hub.db fallback). The exceptions are PatterStage's own <hermes_mission> prompt envelope and its deploy logs under ~/.hermes/logs, and both need sign-off to change. Summed across findings the net is roughly -490 src lines, far short of the 2,881 the programme missed by. The larger value in this scope is getting the boundaries right, not the line count.

### Measurements

- **In-scope TypeScript files**: 288. Method: `git ls-files src/lib src/modules | grep -E '\.(ts|tsx)$' excluding src/lib/{api,analytics,dashboard,db,retention,schema,seed,sessions,stats,sync,ui}/`
- **In-scope lines**: 36,345 (src/modules: 10,533). Method: `same file list | xargs wc -l; git ls-files src/modules | xargs wc -l`
- **Largest in-scope files**: config-schema.ts 608, composer/engine.ts 598, quest-defs.ts 580, models-repository.ts 503, chat-utils.ts 497, HermesRuntime.ts 496, profile-config-builder.ts 465. Method: `xargs wc -l | sort -rn`
- **Census srcRepeatedWindowLines attributed to in-scope files (top-20 list)**: 64 lines in 3 files: mission-repository.ts 28, agent-root-repository.ts 18, profiles-repository.ts 18. Method: `node scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs --report, srcDup.byFile filtered to scope`
- **In-scope files with a 6-line window repeated inside the same file**: 15. Method: `scratch script reusing line-census.mjs repeatedWindows normalisation (trimmed, non-comment, non-bracket lines), counting hashes seen twice within one file`
- **Exports with no non-test reference outside their own file**: 71 (19 also unused in their own file; 15 product-dead after removing scripts/docs users and two test seams). Method: `scratch script: every 'export function|const|let|class' name word-searched across git ls-files src scripts tests, then the 19 re-checked across the whole repo including .mts and org/`
- **In-scope files with exactly one non-test importer**: 94 of 288 (0 with none). Method: `scratch resolver over alias (@/) and relative import specifiers in src and scripts`
- **In-scope files importing next/server**: 31 (5 outside any handlers folder). Method: `grep -rlE 'from "next/server"' src/lib src/modules, scoped`
- **Directories named *handlers***: 6 in 3 shapes (3 top-level lib, 1 nested in missions, 2 in modules). Method: `find src -type d -name '*handlers*'`
- **Non-comment 'hermes' lines in in-scope core outside runtime/ and modules/**: 43. Method: `grep -rniE hermes src/lib, excluding comment-leading lines, runtime/ and other scopes`
- **Distinct legacy CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ env names read in non-test files**: 14 (CH_DATA_DIR 38 mentions, CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR 29). Method: `git ls-files | grep -v tests/ | xargs grep -ohE '\bCH_[A-Z_]+\b|\bCONTROL_HUB_[A-Z_]+\b' | sort | uniq -c`
- **@public JSDoc tags (knip suppressions) in scope**: 21. Method: `grep -rn '@public' src/lib src/modules, scoped`
- **src/lib/utils.ts exports and named importers**: 10 exports; timeAgo 16, pluralise 12, formatBytes 3, titleCase 2, timeUntil 2, formatElapsed 2, messageSummary 1, emptyModelDefaults 1, safeJsonParse 1 (relative import), truncate 0. Method: `parse of 'import {…} from "@/lib/utils"' across src plus grep for relative ../utils`
- **Unit suites referencing the files most likely to be split**: chat-utils 12, config-schema 11, scripts-manager 11, profile-config-builder 6. Method: `grep -rlE '<path>' tests | wc -l`

### Findings that survived verification (19 of 19)

#### lib-domains-05 · 15 exports are reached only by tests, which knip cannot see

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** dead-code, low, -155, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/frameworks/repository.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/skills-config.ts`, `src/lib/modules/registry.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/toolset-unify.ts`, `src/lib/models/llm-output.ts`, `src/lib/help/help-source.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/providers.ts`, `src/lib/fs/local-dir-entry.ts`, `src/lib/chat/chat-repository.ts`, `src/lib/host/platform.ts`, `src/lib/host/hardware-cron.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`
- **Evidence:** for n in updateFramework listFrameworks buildDisabledYamlLines MODULE_ACCENTS getModule mergeAdvancedOverrides extractAnswerSpan helpIsBuilt isKeylessProvider formatLocalDirEntryLine getConversationWithMessages isMac isLinux homeDir HARDWARE_CRON_PRESET_SCRIPT_FILES; do grep -rnw $n src scripts; done: each prints only its definition line. grep -rnE '\btruncate\(' src prints only src/lib/utils.ts:114. The lockbook ruling is at org/LOCKBOOK.md:112-114, and grep -n MODULE_ACCENTS tests/unit/lockbook-tokens.test.ts prints 34, 164 and 168. grep -rnw ChatConversationWithMessages src prints only chat-repository.ts:64 and :184. grep -rn 'Kept exported for tests' src/lib src/modules finds 4 sites.
- **Sceptic:** All 15 exports plus truncate have no non-test reference in src or scripts, and none is used inside its own file. MODULE_ACCENTS must come off the delete list or be flagged for the operator. org/LOCKBOOK.md:112-114 records WG-WEB-009 (B): "the map exists, in src/lib/modules/registry.ts", four entries, one per module. tests/unit/lockbook-tokens.test.ts:34,164,168 holds that ruling. Deleting it removes a recorded lock-in artifact, and the finder's operatorDecision does not mention it. HARDWARE_CRON_PRESET_SCRIPT_FILES is derived from HARDWARE_CRON_UI_PRESETS (hardware-cron.ts:24-26) and appears in tests only as mock keys, so it is safe. Two small gains were missed. Deleting getConversationWithMessages also leaves ChatConversationWithMessages (chat-repository.ts:64-66) unused. And 4 more @public tags literally say "Kept exported for tests/unit/..." (credentials-repository.ts:72,96,148; hermes-env-sync.ts:111), so they are the same class of export, excluded deliberately. The knip facts hold: lint at package.json:18 omits knip, lint:knip is at :23, and knip.json does not list tests as entries, so the jest plugin adds them. Net is 178 minus the 25-line MODULE_ACCENTS block plus 3 for the orphaned interface, about -155 src lines, with further test lines removed.
- **Proposal:** Delete the 15 exports and truncate, together with the test cases that exist only to exercise them: frameworks-repository.test.ts, skills-config.test.ts, hermes-toolset-unify.test.ts, llm-output.test.ts, module-registry.test.ts, b6-keyless-providers.test.tsx, b16-help-source-refuses-a-traversal.test.ts, chat-repository.test.ts, local-dir-entry-normalize.test.ts and the truncate block in utils.test.ts. For platform.ts, also drop the keys from the 3 suites that mock @/lib/host/platform. Then add a report-only `knip --production --include exports` run so the next test-only export shows up.
- **Operator decision:** isKeylessProvider and helpIsBuilt are tagged @public, declared as API for future server-side callers. updateFramework and listFrameworks are the only update and list functions for the frameworks table (reads go through getActiveFrameworkConfig). Confirm these four before deleting.

#### lib-domains-06 · Four shapes are declared twice, outside the three shapes the C2 census watches

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** type-safety, low, -60, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/chat/chat-repository.ts`, `src/types/chat.ts`, `src/lib/models/fallbacks-repository.ts`, `src/types/console.ts`, `src/lib/models/models-repository.ts`, `src/lib/models/task-types.ts`, `src/lib/templates-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/components/missions/templates/types.ts`, `src/lib/templates/catalog-template-repository.ts`, `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`
- **Evidence:** Chat pair: sed -n 28,62p src/lib/chat/chat-repository.ts and sed -n 11,50p src/types/chat.ts. Fallback pair: sed -n 8,19p src/lib/models/fallbacks-repository.ts and sed -n 216,227p src/types/console.ts. Model defaults: sed -n 20,33p src/lib/models/models-repository.ts and sed -n 20,33p src/lib/models/task-types.ts. Templates: sed -n 61,92p src/lib/templates-handlers/shared.ts and sed -n 7,30p src/components/missions/templates/types.ts. Census shapes: sed -n 172,178p scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs.
- **Sceptic:** All four duplications re-derived. ChatConversation is identical at chat-repository.ts:39-48 and types/chat.ts:41-50. ChatMessage has drifted: chat-repository.ts:50-62 has required reasoning and ToolCallRecord[], while types/chat.ts:26-39 has optional reasoning and ToolCall[]. FallbackEntryRecord (fallbacks-repository.ts:8-19) is identical to FallbackChainEntry (types/console.ts:216-227). ModelDefaults (models-repository.ts:20-33) hand-lists the TASK_TYPES slots, and useModels.ts:17-28 repeats them. CustomTemplate and MissionTemplate share their core fields. The census shapes list holds only missionDraftFields, modelRow and syncSourceFailure. The finder missed 3 more duplicates in the same pair of files. ToolCallRecord (chat-repository.ts:32-37) is identical to ToolCall (types/chat.ts:19-24), and ChatRole and ChatMessageStatus are declared in both files (chat-repository.ts:28-30; types/chat.ts:11-14, not exported there). One constraint is unstated: the template base type must live in a client-safe type-only module, because templates-handlers/shared.ts imports fs and next/server and MissionTemplate is consumed by client modals. The net rises to about -60.
- **Proposal:** - chat-repository imports ChatConversation from @/types/chat, and ChatMessage is reconciled into one type (the row mapper produces the nullable form). - fallbacks-repository uses FallbackChainEntry as its record type. - `export type ModelDefaults = Record<TaskType, string | null>`. - One template base type lives in src/lib/templates, with CustomTemplate = base & {dispatchMode: DispatchMode; createdAt; updatedAt; categoryId?}. - Add all four shapes to the census shapes list.

#### lib-domains-10 · Repetition that pays without any new cross-file interface

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -55, confirmed
- **Files:** `src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/promote.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/update.ts`, `src/lib/composer/composer-repository.ts`, `src/lib/missions/build-mission-prompt.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/deploy-status.ts`
- **Evidence:** sed -n 28,72p src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/promote.ts. sed -n 28,78p src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/update.ts. sed -n 68,88p src/lib/missions/mission-body.ts. The intra2.mjs output lists composer-repository.ts 28 (79/207), build-mission-prompt.ts 18 (93/108) and deploy-status.ts 18 (73/120). Test check: grep -rlE 'mission-handlers/(promote|update)' tests | xargs grep -l readFileSync prints nothing.
- **Sceptic:** Re-derived. - promote.ts:33 destructures 17 fields and lines 49-69 re-list them into promoteMission. update.ts:31 does the same, re-listing into buildMissionFieldPatch at 52-75. - composer-repository.ts 79-93 and 207-221 are byte-identical node and edge insertion (28 within-file window lines). - build-mission-prompt.ts attrStr is identical at 95-101 and 110-116. - deploy-status.ts spells the same 8-field idle literal at 73-82 and 120-129.  No test reads promote.ts or update.ts as source: neither suite that references them uses readFileSync. Spreading f works because TypeScript does not excess-check spread members, and f.categoryId and schedule are overridden explicitly. The count is about -15 (promote), -16 (update), -12 (insertGraph), -6 (attrString) and -8 (IDLE_STATUS), about -57.
- **Proposal:** - Spread `...f` into promoteMission and buildMissionFieldPatch, overriding schedule and categoryId, instead of destructuring and re-listing. promote's instruction check reads f.instruction. - insertGraph(workflowId, def, ts) inside composer-repository. - attrString(attrs) in build-mission-prompt. - const IDLE_STATUS in deploy-status.  No interface crosses a file boundary, so the rule that two-consumer duplication does not pay does not apply.

#### lib-domains-15 · The mission-dispatch compatibility shim has outlived its reason, and run submission is written three times

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, medium, -40, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/missions/mission-dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/chat-dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/composer/dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-queue-tick.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-promote-handler.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l src/lib/missions/mission-dispatch.ts gives 54. grep -rlE 'missions/mission-dispatch"' src gives 3. grep -rl 'jest.mock("@/lib/missions/mission-dispatch"' tests | wc -l gives 9. The submit sites: sed -n 45,110p src/lib/orchestration/dispatch.ts, sed -n 72,122p src/lib/orchestration/chat-dispatch.ts and sed -n 165,192p src/lib/composer/dispatch.ts.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed. mission-dispatch.ts is 54 lines with the stale header, its body applies the overrides and then calls dispatchMissionRun, it has 3 importers, and 9 suites jest.mock it. The three submit sequences exist at orchestration/dispatch.ts:49-107, chat-dispatch.ts:76-120 and composer/dispatch.ts:171-188, each with different side effects and return shapes. Two corrections. The 9 mocks cannot simply be re-pointed. They stub dispatchMissionNow's {ok, sessionId} return, while dispatchMissionRun returns {ok, runId, backendRunId, sessionId, error}, and @/lib/orchestration exports much more, so each suite needs a rewritten factory. That is real test cost. Folding costs about 12 lines in dispatchMissionRun against the 54-line file, so the net is about -40 without the optional submitTrackedRun. As the finder says, that helper barely pays.
- **Proposal:** Fold the override step into dispatchMissionRun as an overrides option and delete mission-dispatch.ts. Re-point the 3 importers and the 9 mocks to @/lib/orchestration. Optionally extract submitTrackedRun({runId, input, profileName, sessionId?, previousResponseId?, scope}) returning a handle or an error for the three sites. It has three consumers, but each saves only about 5 lines, so do it only if it reads better.

#### lib-domains-09 · Repetition inside the largest files, invisible to the line census

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -30, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/composer/engine.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/HermesRuntime.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-config-builder.ts`, `src/lib/scripts/scripts-manager.ts`
- **Evidence:** Within-file window re-run: node <scratchpad>/intra2.mjs <repo>, which copies the line-census.mjs:59-96 normalisation but counts a window seen twice in the same file. It reports 288 in-scope files and 15 files with 250 covered lines. Those 15 are library-repository, composer-repository, profiles-repository, deploy-status, build-mission-prompt, rec-room generate, chat-dispatch, profile-drift, sync-manager, models-repository, HermesRuntime, next-run, schedules-repository, profile-pull and seed-agent-catalog. The by-reading repeats: sed -n 114-120,192-198,552-558p src/lib/composer/engine.ts, and sed -n 92-106,130-138,330-347p src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-config-builder.ts.
- **Sceptic:** The evidence is misattributed. A re-run of the census normalisation within each file finds the 15 files, but the three files this finding leads with are NOT among them: composer/engine.ts, profile-config-builder.ts and scripts-manager.ts. Of the four named, only HermesRuntime.ts (419/438) is. The repeats in engine.ts and profile-config-builder.ts are real by reading, but fall below the 6-normalised-line window. Recomputed savings: - engine: mergeStageOutput saves about 8 (3 × 6 lines to 3 × 1 plus a 7-line helper). endRunFailed saves about 4. - HermesRuntime listData: about 6. - profile-config-builder: parseJsonToolsets via coerceToolsets plus sameSorted, about 12. This changes whether empty strings survive (coerceToolsets drops them), so it is a behaviour change unless preserved. - scripts-manager isBareScriptName: 0, since each guard stays one line. - parseMetadata via db/parse-json.parseJson: about 5, but parseJson does not reject arrays or scalars, so an object check must remain.  About -30.
- **Proposal:** Local helpers only, with no new modules: - engine: mergeStageOutput(composerRunId, key, value) and endRunFailed(id, error, {nudge}). - HermesRuntime: listData(json, altKey?). - profile-config-builder: parseJsonToolsets becomes JSON.parse + coerceToolsets, and one sameSorted(a, b). - scripts-manager: isBareScriptName(name), and parseJson from db/parse-json.  coerceToolsets drops empty strings and parseJsonToolsets keeps them, so keep the drift tests green or preserve that difference.

#### lib-domains-18 · Repeated blocks inside the hermes and rec-room modules

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, low, -30, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-discovery.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-pull.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profiles-repository.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-drift.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/sync-manager.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/lib/library-repository.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/generate.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/components/CharacterEditorDialog.tsx`, `src/modules/rec-room/components/ThemeEditorDialog.tsx`
- **Evidence:** node <scratchpad>/intra2.mjs <repo>. The sites: sed -n 70,120p src/modules/rec-room/lib/library-repository.ts; sed -n 118,163p src/modules/hermes/lib/profiles-repository.ts; sed -n 210,268p src/modules/hermes/lib/sync-manager.ts; sed -n 225,250p src/modules/rec-room/handlers/generate.ts; sed -n 120,140p src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-discovery.ts against sed -n 186,206p src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-pull.ts.
- **Sceptic:** The within-file re-run confirms these sites: - library-repository.ts: 36 lines (78/106) - profiles-repository.ts: 20 (125/148) - generate.ts: 16 (230/269) - profile-drift.ts: 14 (122/164) - sync-manager.ts: 14 (217/257, the same 10-line catch result)  profile-discovery.ts:126 against profile-pull.ts:192 is a cross-file repeat of an 8-line upsertSkill block. Two consumers, so it does not pay, as the finder says. Recomputed per helper: - library-repository: the INSERT and UPDATE argument orders differ (id first and ts twice, against now() and id last). A shared column-args function covers only the 9 middle values, about -3 per entity. - sync-manager failure(): about -15. - effectiveToolsets: about -6. - requireStoryChapter: about -6. - profile-drift: about -3.  About -30, close to the finder's -25.
- **Proposal:** Add local helpers: effectiveToolsets(row, seed) in profiles-repository, diskConfigMatches(bundle, expected) in profile-drift, a failure(err) builder in sync-manager, a column-args function in library-repository and a requireStoryChapter(body) guard in generate.ts. Share upsertSkillFromFile(skillKey, content) between discovery and pull only if the two must stay in step, since it is about 9 lines per site against a 7-line helper. Leave the two dialogs alone.

#### lib-domains-11 · Small helpers re-implemented across domains (HTML escaping, JSON parsing, env reading, durations)

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** duplication, medium, -25, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`, `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/markdown.ts`, `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/report.ts`, `src/lib/search/duckduckgo.ts`, `src/lib/search/visit.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`, `src/lib/db/parse-json.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-repository.ts`, `src/lib/feature-flags.ts`, `src/lib/host/paths.ts`, `src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/run-deadline.ts`
- **Evidence:** grep -rnE 'replace\(/&/g, "&amp;"\)' src finds chat-utils.ts:83, deep-research/markdown.ts:29, report.ts:37 and proxy.ts:122. sed -n 10,24p src/lib/search/duckduckgo.ts and sed -n 15,27p src/lib/search/visit.ts. grep -rn safeJsonParse src. grep -rlE 'db/parse-json"' src | wc -l gives 8. sed -n 26,32p src/lib/feature-flags.ts. sed -n 84,97p src/lib/utils.ts against sed -n 90,99p src/lib/missions/mission-run-state.ts. formatElapsed callers: src/app/work/research/page.tsx:87 and src/components/session/SessionCard.tsx:136.
- **Sceptic:** The sites exist. - escapeHtml: chat-utils.ts:81-88 escapes 5 characters, deep-research/markdown.ts:27-33 escapes 4, and report.ts:36-38 escAttr escapes 4 (the last two are identical, in the same domain). - decodeEntities: duckduckgo.ts:13-22, and the same replaces inline in visit.ts:19-25. - safeJsonParse has one caller, mission-repository.ts:10 and 64-68. - feature-flags.ts firstEnvValue re-implements readEnv with lowercasing, and update-handlers/shared.ts:27 and run-deadline.ts:23 read their aliases inline. - formatElapsed differs from formatRunDuration only in the days bucket.  Corrections. db/parse-json has 8 importers, not 7. The semantics are not interchangeable. safeJsonParse returns a caller-chosen fallback, while parseJson returns null. parseStringArrayOrEmpty filters out non-strings, so local_dirs (unknown[] passed to normalizeLocalDirsInput) cannot use it. A new text/html.ts shared by 5 sites costs about 20 lines against about 34 removed. The honest net is about -25, not -35. The operator decisions (badge text past 24 h, apostrophe escaping in research HTML) are correctly flagged.
- **Proposal:** - One small text module (e.g. a new src/lib/text/html.ts) exporting escapeHtml and decodeEntities for the 5 sites. - Replace safeJsonParse with parseJson/parseStringArrayOrEmpty in mission-repository and delete it. Route the local JSON.parse wrappers through db/parse-json where the fallback semantics match. - feature-flags and the two inline `||` reads use readEnv. - Optionally, formatElapsed delegates to formatRunDuration. - Leave the SSE loops and crontab splits alone.
- **Operator decision:** Having formatElapsed delegate changes the active-session badge text past 24 hours ("25h 3m" becomes "1d 1h"). Also, escaping ' in research HTML changes output strings that tests may assert. Skip either if not accepted.
