#### lib-domains-04 · Route-handler code follows four conventions, and several handlers are misfiled

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, medium, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/templates-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/hardware-cron-handlers/crontab-store.ts`, `src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-handlers/promote.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-promote-handler.ts`, `src/lib/status/runtime-status.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/handlers/shared.ts`, `src/modules/rec-room/components/StoryBiblePanel.tsx`, `src/lib/fs/path-security.ts`, `src/lib/missions/mission-response.ts`, `src/lib/memory/hindsight-route-helpers.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/fallback-import.ts`
- **Evidence:** find src -type d -name '*handlers*'. for d in src/lib/{templates,hardware-cron,update}-handlers; do ls $d | wc -l; cat $d/* | wc -l; done. grep -rlE 'from "next/server"' src/lib src/modules | grep -v handlers. Line 30 of src/lib/status/runtime-status.ts: import { runGit } from "@/lib/update-handlers/shared". Test fan-out: for p in templates-handlers hardware-cron-handlers update-handlers mission-promote-handler rec-room/handlers/shared; do grep -rl $p tests | wc -l; done gives 2, 2, 4, 11, 2. Source-reading suites: grep -rln readFileSync tests/unit | xargs grep -ln 'templates-handlers\|hardware-cron-handlers\|update-handlers' gives 5. Docs: grep -rl hardware-cron-handlers docs gives docs/contributing/repo-guide.md and docs/running/host-scheduling.md.
- **Sceptic:** Every structural fact reproduces. There are 6 *handlers* folders: templates-handlers (6 files, 529 lines), hardware-cron-handlers (7, 735) and update-handlers (4, 401). In scope, 31 files import next/server (39 across src/lib and src/modules, minus 6 in api/ and 2 in sessions/), and exactly 5 of them sit outside a handlers folder. runtime-status.ts:30 imports runGit from update-handlers/shared.ts, which imports NextResponse at line 15. mission-promote-handler.ts is 214 lines with no next/server import. rec-room/handlers/shared.ts is 272 lines, has no next/server import, and StoryBiblePanel.tsx ("use client") imports safeArc from it. shared.ts imports only types and ../lib/characters, so this is a naming problem, not a client-bundle bug. The consolidation plan and lib-moves.json record no reason for keeping the *-handlers folders. The cost was understated: the moves touch 21 test files (templates-handlers 2, hardware-cron-handlers 2, update-handlers 4, mission-promote-handler 11, rec-room handlers/shared 2). Five of those read source with readFileSync, so their path strings must change too. Net src lines are 0, and the value is navigational only.
- **Proposal:** Adopt one rule, <domain>/handlers/: - templates-handlers → templates/handlers - hardware-cron-handlers → host/cron-handlers - update-handlers → deploy/handlers - runGit moves into src/lib/git/ - mission-promote-handler.ts is renamed missions/promote-mission.ts - rec-room handlers/shared.ts moves to rec-room/lib/story-prompt.ts, beside lib/prompts.ts  Record every move in lib-moves.json and update all five path spellings. Fix the two docs pages that name hardware-cron-handlers, and leave the org/tasks records as written.

#### lib-domains-14 · Pre-rename env aliases and data paths are still read, two of them inconsistently

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** naming, medium, 0, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/host/paths.ts`, `src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts`, `src/lib/orchestration/run-deadline.ts`, `src/lib/hardware-cron-handlers/crontab-store.ts`, `docs/running/env-reference.md`, `docs/running/data-storage.md`, `docs/running/migration.md`
- **Evidence:** grep -rnoE 'process\.env\.(CH|CONTROL_HUB)_[A-Z_]+|"(CH|CONTROL_HUB)_[A-Z_]+"' src scripts --include=*.ts --include=*.mjs --include=*.mts --include=*.tsx | sed -E 's/.*((CH|CONTROL_HUB)_[A-Z_]+).*/\1/' | sort | uniq -c gives 11 names. grep -n 'CH_\|control-hub' src/lib/host/paths.ts. sed -n 25,28p src/lib/update-handlers/shared.ts. sed -n 20,24p src/lib/orchestration/run-deadline.ts. ls scripts/hardware scripts/application | grep '^ch-' gives 7. grep -rlw 'CH_UPDATE_GIT_BRANCH\|CH_SCRIPTS_DIR\|CH_HARDWARE_LOG_DIR' docs prints nothing.
- **Sceptic:** The substance holds, but the counts do not reproduce. Counting only env-name reads (process.env.X or a quoted name) in src and scripts .ts/.mjs gives 11 distinct CH_/CONTROL_HUB_ names: CH_DATA_DIR 13, CONTROL_HUB_DATA_DIR 12, the rest 1 each. Shell scripts add CONTROL_HUB_PORT. The finder's 38 and 29 are not reproducible, and a bare token grep inflates them with unrelated constants such as CH_MODES and CH_LEN. Confirmed: paths.ts:76,119,137,145 read through readEnv, the control-hub fallbacks are at 33, 45 and 100, the inline || reads are at update-handlers/shared.ts:27 and run-deadline.ts:23, crontab-store.ts:62 maps both ps- and ch- prefixes, and 7 ch-*.sh scripts ship. CH_UPDATE_GIT_BRANCH, CH_SCRIPTS_DIR and CH_HARDWARE_LOG_DIR appear in no doc. docs/running/data-storage.md:112-113 calls the fallbacks "deliberate and load-bearing". The no-sign-off part (routing two reads through readEnv) is 0 net lines. The -25 applies only after a signed-off deprecation release. The operator decision is correctly flagged.
- **Proposal:** Now: route the two inline reads through readEnv so every alias is read one way. Later, with sign-off: ship a release that warns at boot when a legacy name or path is used and add a deprecation note to docs/running/migration.md. Then remove the aliases, the control-hub.db and ~/control-hub/data probe, the ch- prefix mapping and the ch-*.sh scripts.
- **Operator decision:** Removing the CH_*/CONTROL_HUB_* env vars and the control-hub.db and ~/control-hub/data fallbacks removes documented back-compat (docs/running/env-reference.md:15, data-storage.md:112-113). An un-migrated install would stop finding its database. Needs sign-off per alias and per path.

#### lib-domains-07 · A Stop during llm.ts's retry backoff waits out the sleep, up to 60 seconds

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** performance, low, 2, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/models/llm.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/HermesRuntime.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/deploy-spawn.ts`
- **Evidence:** grep -rnE 'new Promise\(\(r\) => setTimeout' src/lib src/modules finds llm.ts:362, 377, 399 and 404, and deploy-spawn.ts:67. sed -n 326,414p src/lib/models/llm.ts shows the sleeps at 362 and 377 inside try and at 399 and 404 inside catch. sed -n 40,58p src/lib/runtime/HermesRuntime.ts. grep -rlw callLLM src lists the callers.
- **Sceptic:** The defect is real. callGateway links the signal only to each attempt's controller (llm.ts:338, linkAbort at 223-230). The 429 backoff at 362 is 30_000*attempt, which reaches 60 s on attempt 2. A Stop that lands during the sleep is noticed only when the next fetch aborts and the catch at 396 throws stoppedError. HermesRuntime.ts:45-58 already has sleepAbortable with the quoted comment, and callLLM's non-test callers are deep-research/engine.ts and rec-room handlers create, crud, edit and generate. Two corrections. The net is not -2: a shared sleep module with header and export costs about as many lines as the 16-line private copy it replaces, and the 4 call sites stay one line each, so the net is about +2. Also, the sleeps at 399 and 404 sit inside the catch block, where a sleepAbortable rejection would escape as signal.reason rather than stoppedError(). Those two sites need a wrapper to keep the existing error contract. The deploy-spawn.ts:67 sleep is an injectable probe dep with no signal, so pointing it at the shared helper changes nothing.
- **Proposal:** Export sleepAbortable from one module (for example src/lib/runtime/sleep.ts) and use it at the four llm.ts sites with input.signal, turning an abort into stoppedError(). Point deploy-spawn's default sleep at it too.

#### lib-domains-13 · The vendor name is used for PatterStage's own concepts in core

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** naming, medium, 2, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/missions/build-mission-prompt.ts`, `src/lib/deploy/deploy-spawn.ts`, `src/lib/status/runtime-status.ts`, `src/lib/status/runtime-status-format.ts`, `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`, `src/lib/models/llm.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/hermes-config-read.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/hermes-config-write.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/hermes-env-sync.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/hermes-fallback-config.ts`, `docs/running/deploy.md`, `docs/guides/missions.md`
- **Evidence:** grep -rniE hermes src/lib --include=*.ts --include=*.tsx | grep -vE '^src/lib/(runtime|api|db|sessions|sync|seed|analytics|dashboard|retention|schema|stats|ui)/' | grep -vE ':[0-9]+:\s*(//|\*|/\*)' | wc -l gives 43. grep -rl hermes_mission tests/unit | wc -l gives 11. grep -n hermes-outside-adapter src/lib/deploy/deploy-spawn.ts shows 2 disable pragmas with reasons. sed -n 110,112p src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts. sed -n 166,168p src/lib/models/llm.ts.
- **Sceptic:** Mostly reproduces. - 43 non-comment lines contain 'hermes' in in-scope core outside runtime. - build-mission-prompt.ts has the tag at lines 33, 199 and 267. - deploy-spawn writes ~/.hermes/logs, documented at docs/running/deploy.md:65-67 and admitted at limitations.md:155. - chat-utils.ts:111 uses the literal "hermes-agent" (formatModelName) while CHAT_DEFAULT_MODEL exists in types/chat.ts. - llm.ts:168 defaults to "hermes". - hermesHome appears at runtime-status.ts:64,72 and runtime-status-format.ts:17,38.  Corrections: 11 unit suites reference hermes_mission, not 12. The deploy-spawn log strings already carry design-lint-disable pragmas with written reasons, so they are a recorded exception, not an oversight. Dropping the hermes- prefix from 4 files inside src/modules/hermes has little value: the prefix is accurate inside the adapter, and renaming touches test mock paths. The chat-utils constant and the status field rename are the only no-sign-off parts.
- **Proposal:** Now, with no behaviour change: import CHAT_DEFAULT_MODEL in chat-utils, drop the hermes- prefix from the 4 module files, and rename the status field to agentHome while keeping the pasted `hermes-home=` label if tests rely on it. With sign-off: rename the envelope to <mission> while line 267 still recognises <hermes_mission for stored rows, and move PatterStage's own logs into the PatterStage data dir.
- **Operator decision:** Renaming <hermes_mission> changes the prompt format sent to the agent and read back from stored missions. Moving deploy and restart logs off ~/.hermes/logs changes file locations documented in docs/running/deploy.md. Each needs sign-off.

#### lib-domains-03 · The Hermes config.yaml section table (336 lines) lives in core config-schema.ts

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, high, 5, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/config/config-schema.ts`, `src/lib/config/config-sections.ts`, `src/lib/config/env-file.ts`, `src/lib/config/yaml-config.ts`, `src/components/config/SettingsSection.tsx`, `src/hooks/useSettingsEditor.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l src/lib/config/config-schema.ts gives 608. grep -n 'tirith_enabled\|camofox\|HERMES.md\|"hermes"' src/lib/config/config-schema.ts. grep -rlE 'config/config-schema"' src gives 7 files, config-sections.ts not among them. grep -rlE config-schema tests/unit | wc -l gives 13. grep -rlE 'config/env-file"' src gives src/modules/hermes/lib/{config-import,hermes-env-sync}.ts. The recorded exceptions are at org/decisions/ADR-0005-product-modules.md:316-331 ("the `/config/hermes_md` route, `agent-file-store`'s `\"hermes\"` file key").
- **Sceptic:** The core facts hold. CONFIG_SECTIONS runs from 68 to about 403 in a 608-line file. tirith_enabled is at 159, camofox at 242, HERMES.md at 384/389, and fileKeyForFilePath returns "hermes" at 517. The lucide imports are at 10-32. env-file.ts is imported only by 2 hermes-module files. Three details are wrong. config-sections.ts does NOT import config-schema: it mentions it only in a comment at line 13 and is deliberately pure data. The importers are 7 (2 app pages, the api/config route, 3 components/config files, hooks/useSettingsEditor.ts), and 13 unit suites reference it, not 11. The finder also overstates the conflict. ADR-0005:316-331 states the boundary claim precisely and lists what it does NOT cover, naming the /config/hermes_md route and the "hermes" file key. The hermes_md section and the "hermes" file key are therefore a recorded exception. Only the config.yaml field vocabulary (tirith_enabled, camofox and the rest) is unruled. The hermes-outside-adapter lint pattern (design-lint.mjs:263) does not cover these words either. Moving env-file.ts into the module is a separate, clean part: 2 importers, both in the module, no URL change. Needs an ADR-level owner ruling.
- **Proposal:** Get a ruling on whether the Settings field table is Hermes adapter vocabulary. If it is: move CONFIG_SECTIONS into src/modules/hermes as pure data with IconName strings, and expose it to core Settings through a data composition point read by src/lib/modules. validateSectionValues and resolveSectionRedirect stay in core. If it is not: record that exemption beside hermes-outside-adapter in design-lint so the boundary claim is stated precisely. In both cases, move env-file.ts into the module now, since all its importers are there.
- **Operator decision:** Needs an owner ruling on ADR-0005 boundary scope: is the Settings field table Hermes protocol knowledge? No URL or config key changes.

#### lib-domains-12 · The chat markdown renderer rewrites fenced code, so Copy copies markup

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** consistency, low, 8, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`, `src/hooks/useChatPage.ts`, `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/markdown.ts`
- **Evidence:** Repro: node <scratchpad>/md.cjs <repo> evaluates renderMarkdown copied from src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts:81-107 and reads button.getAttribute('data-code') in jsdom. "```py\nprint(2*3*4)\n```" gives "print(2<em>3</em>4)<br />". "```sh\necho `date`\n```" gives "echo <code class=" (the attribute is truncated). "```js\nconst a = 1;\nconst b = 2;\n```" gives "const a = 1;<br />const b = 2;<br />". The clipboard reads the attribute at src/hooks/useChatPage.ts:113. The only tests are tests/unit/chat-page-toast.test.tsx:124 and u14-a-failed-run-is-an-error.test.tsx:25, both mocking renderMarkdown as (s) => s.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed by running chat-utils' own escapeHtml and renderMarkdown, extracted from the file, in jsdom, then reading the button's data-code. The defect is broader than reported. Line 105 turns every newline into <br /> after the fence is substituted into data-code. Any multi-line fenced block therefore copies literal "<br />" text instead of newlines, even with no markdown characters inside, so Copy is wrong for effectively all multi-line code. The two tests that touch renderMarkdown mock it as identity, so no suite covers the output. The proposal needs changing. The research renderer is not a drop-in: it emits citation links for [n] (href #dr-src-n), turns _x_ into <em>, and renders headings and lists (renderReportHtml, markdown.ts:110). Adopting it wholesale would mangle chat. The minimal fix protects fence bodies with a sentinel inside the chat renderer (markdown.ts:37-59 pattern) and takes data-code from the raw body. That adds lines rather than removing 20. The operator decision stands only if a renderer swap is chosen; a sentinel fix changes nothing except the broken markup.
- **Proposal:** Render chat through one shared renderer that protects code spans, either the research renderer or its code-span protection extracted. Keep the copy-button contract by setting data-code from the raw fence body. Add a unit case with * and backticks inside a fence.
- **Operator decision:** Chat message rendering output changes. Fenced code stops gaining <em>/<code>, and if the research renderer is adopted wholesale, headings and lists start rendering in chat. Confirm which.

#### lib-domains-01 · Laboratory was named a module in ADR-0005 but still lives in core, and Composer imports it directly

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** structure, high, 10, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/engine.ts`, `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/search.ts`, `src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/run-job.ts`, `src/lib/search/index.ts`, `src/lib/composer/dispatch.ts`, `src/lib/composer/engine.ts`, `src/lib/modules/registry.ts`, `src/lib/modules/server.ts`, `org/decisions/ADR-0005-product-modules.md`
- **Evidence:** sed -n 56p org/decisions/ADR-0005-product-modules.md. wc -l src/lib/laboratory/deep-research/*.ts src/lib/search/*.ts gives 1,391 and 408. grep -rlE "@/lib/search" src returns 3 files, all under deep-research. grep -n laboratory src/lib/composer/dispatch.ts src/lib/composer/engine.ts returns 4 import lines. The rule is core-imports-no-module at scripts/tooling/design-lint.mjs:292, and its files() exemptions are at 306-312. grep -rn 'jest.mock("@/lib/laboratory/deep-research/search"' tests returns 3 suites (b14-research-cancel, b4-emits-research-composer, research-gather-is-persisted). find src/app/api/laboratory -name route.ts | wc -l gives 6. The research tables come from src/lib/db/migrations/019_deep_research.sql and 034_research_usage.sql.
- **Sceptic:** The facts reproduce. ADR-0005:56 names laboratory as a module. registry.ts:114 declares laboratoryModule. laboratory is 8 files and 1,391 lines, and search is 6 files and 408 lines whose only importers are deep-research/{engine,search,types}.ts. The Composer imports are at dispatch.ts:15-17 and engine.ts:19, and there are 6 route files under src/app/api/laboratory. Neither ADR-0005 nor org/plans records a later ruling that laboratory stays in core. Three things were missing. First, the search.ts re-export is 8 lines by wc -l, and 3 unit suites jest.mock its path, so deleting it means re-pointing those mocks. Second, option (a) also raises ADR-0005 rule 2: a module owns its own prefixed tables, so the research tables and their migrations (019_deep_research.sql, 034_research_usage.sql) would need an ownership ruling too. Third, the lint rule is at design-lint.mjs:292-314, not 285 (285 is module-registry-stays-pure). The net of about +10 is plausible: a new ServerModule capability plus its interface.
- **Proposal:** Pick one of two. (a) Carry out the ADR: move deep-research and search into src/modules/laboratory/, give Composer's research node a ServerModule capability registered in src/lib/modules/server.ts (as hermes and rec-room have) instead of a direct import, and delete the search.ts re-export. (b) Amend ADR-0005 so laboratory stays core and drop it from rule 4. Either way, use one code name for the subject (research) and keep URLs unchanged unless ruled otherwise. Record the move in scripts/tooling/lib-moves.json and update all five spellings of each path.
- **Operator decision:** Option (b) edits ADR-0005, which is in the protected set and needs an accepted ADR. Option (a) carries out an accepted ADR but changes the Composer research-node dispatch path. Renaming /api/laboratory/research/* or /work/research would change URLs and needs individual sign-off.

#### lib-domains-16 · The largest files: which to split, and which to leave whole

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** complexity, medium, 12, adjusted
- **Files:** `src/lib/quests/quest-defs.ts`, `src/lib/quests/evaluate.ts`, `src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts`, `src/lib/scripts/scripts-manager.ts`, `src/lib/runtime/HermesRuntime.ts`, `src/lib/composer/engine.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-config-builder.ts`, `src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-drift.ts`
- **Evidence:** wc -l src/lib/quests/quest-defs.ts src/lib/quests/evaluate.ts src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts src/lib/scripts/scripts-manager.ts src/lib/runtime/HermesRuntime.ts src/modules/hermes/lib/profile-config-builder.ts. sed -n 520,585p src/lib/quests/quest-defs.ts | grep function. sed -n 16,18p src/lib/quests/quest-defs.ts. grep -n '^import' src/lib/quests/evaluate.ts src/lib/stats/derive.ts. grep -n '^// ──' src/lib/chat/chat-utils.ts. grep -rl chat-utils tests | wc -l gives 12.
- **Sceptic:** The sizes are right: quest-defs 580, chat-utils 497, scripts-manager 446, HermesRuntime 496, profile-config-builder 465. chat-utils has 5 sections, downloadFile is imported by the results/artifacts and results/logs pages, and 12 unit suites reference chat-utils. quest-defs has 5 evaluator functions from 529 on, beside the 155-line quests/evaluate.ts. One piece of context was missed. The quest-defs header (lines 16-18) records that derive.ts imports the counters (questsInChapter, questsMet, questsMetInChapter) as values. Moving them into evaluate.ts is still cycle-free, because evaluate.ts imports derive only as a type (evaluate.ts:24), but that header must be rewritten. questAvailable is also imported by a client component (components/dashboard/NextQuestCard.tsx:32), so its new home must stay client-safe; evaluate.ts currently is. Also, the finder's paraphrase of the header ("typing is the point") comes from the ConceptId @public note at line 29, not the file header. These are moves, costing roughly +12 lines of headers and imports.
- **Proposal:** - Split chat-utils into chat/export.ts, chat/chat-api.ts and chat/run-events.ts, and move downloadFile to a browser helper the results pages share. - Move the quest evaluators into quests/evaluate.ts and keep the data in TypeScript. - Move profile-config-builder's comparison block into profile-drift.ts. - Leave HermesRuntime and engine whole; their repetition is covered in lib-domains-09.  These are moves: they do not shrink the line count, and they cost test import edits (12 for chat-utils).

#### lib-domains-19 · The line census and knip have blind spots over this scope

- **Category, risk, net lines, verdict:** tooling, low, 40, adjusted
- **Files:** `scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs`, `knip.json`, `package.json`
- **Evidence:** sed -n 59,96p scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs; the dup filter reads fs.size >= 2. sed -n 172,178p scripts/tooling/line-census.mjs. sed -n 18p package.json and sed -n 23p package.json. grep -rn -A1 @public src/lib src/modules | grep @public shows every tag followed by reason text. A working intra-file measure is <scratchpad>/intra2.mjs: 15 files, 250 lines in scope.
- **Sceptic:** Confirmed: - repeatedWindows counts only hashes seen in 2 or more files (fs.size >= 2 in line-census.mjs). - The shapes list holds 3 regexes. - lint omits knip. - There are 21 @public tags in scope, with the per-file counts as stated.  One part of the proposal is already met. All 21 @public tags already carry a written reason. Four even name their test (credentials-repository.ts:72,96,148; hermes-env-sync.ts:111). A reason-required rule would therefore catch nothing today, though it may guard the future. The two report-only measures are sound and cheap. An intra-file window measure is about 25 lines (my scratch version is 50 with reporting). The knip --production exports measure is about 15 lines. Adding 4 shapes is 4 lines. These are tooling lines under scripts/, not src.
- **Proposal:** Add two report-only measures to line-census: srcIntraFileRepeatedWindowLines, and testOnlyExports taken from `knip --production --include exports --reporter json`. Add the four shapes. Make each measure shrink-only once the batch that fixes it lands. Require a written reason on every @public, as design-lint pragmas already do.

### What the sceptic found that the reviewer missed

- The reviewer's own scratch pass found 15 files with within-file repeats but named only 9. Six are unreported, each about 5-8 lines net and all local helpers: - orchestration/chat-dispatch.ts:55/134: the same lookup and user-message block in dispatchChatTurn and appendFastTurn. - models/models-repository.ts:248/477: INSERT argument lists. - schedule/next-run.ts:89/120: the 5-field cron split and expandField block in cronCanEverFire and nextCronAfter. - schedule/schedules-repository.ts:105/253: the updates type re-lists CreateScheduleInput fields, which Partial<Pick<...>> would replace. - modules/hermes/lib/profile-pull.ts:78/124: the read-config and build-patch block in pullProfile and pullRootFromHermes. - modules/hermes/lib/seed-agent-catalog.ts:180/197: a repeated upsertProfile literal.  Total about -40. Evidence: node <scratchpad>/intra2.mjs <repo>.
- More chat-type duplication beside lib-domains-06: - ToolCallRecord (src/lib/chat/chat-repository.ts:32-37) is field-for-field identical to ToolCall (src/types/chat.ts:19-24). - ChatRole and ChatMessageStatus are declared in both files (chat-repository.ts:28-30; types/chat.ts:11-14, unexported there).  About -10 lines more.
- The chat Copy defect (lib-domains-12) is wider than one case. renderMarkdown's newline pass (chat-utils.ts:105) also runs over the data-code attribute, so EVERY multi-line fenced block copies literal '<br />' instead of newlines (jsdom repro: 'const a = 1;<br />const b = 2;<br />'). No test covers the output: both suites that touch renderMarkdown mock it as identity (chat-page-toast.test.tsx:124, u14-a-failed-run-is-an-error.test.tsx:25).
- Deleting MODULE_ACCENTS (lib-domains-05) would contradict a recorded lock-in ruling. org/LOCKBOOK.md:112-114 (WG-WEB-009 B) names the map in src/lib/modules/registry.ts, and tests/unit/lockbook-tokens.test.ts:164-168 holds it. Any dead-export sweep must exempt ruled artifacts or bring them to the operator.
- 4 @public exports say outright that they exist for tests: credentials-repository.ts:72,96,148 and hermes-env-sync.ts:111 ('Kept exported for tests/unit/...'). They belong in the test-only-export inventory of lib-domains-05, deleted or deliberately kept as seams, and should be named rather than left out silently.
- Moving laboratory into a module (lib-domains-01 option a) also brings in ADR-0005 rule 2, table ownership and prefixing, for the Deep Research tables from src/lib/db/migrations/019_deep_research.sql and 034_research_usage.sql. The research_repository would have to move, and table prefixing needs a ruling. 3 unit suites also mock the deep-research/search.ts path.

### Not examined

- No jest, knip, build, Playwright or design-lint runs, per the review rules. The test-only export list comes from a word-boundary text search, so a string-built dynamic import or re-export chain could hide a user; all 19 candidates were spot-checked across the whole repo, including .mts files and org/.
- Read only through census windows, importer counts and headers, not body by body: most of missions/ (30 files, 3,370 lines, including mission-repository.ts and mission-category-repository.ts), models/models-repository.ts, schedule/schedules-repository.ts, orchestration/run-reconcile.ts and scheduler/tick.ts, and the rec-room handlers create/edit/generate and components.
- memory/ (Hindsight files and memory-providers), spend/, search/ internals, skills/, help/help-manifest.ts logic and schedule/ internals: importers and headers only.
- runtime/state-db.ts and runtime/memory-db.ts were treated as the sanctioned Hermes port and not audited for naming or duplication.
- No security review of fs/path-security.ts, search/url-guard.ts or the scripts-manager exec path beyond reading scripts-manager.ts.
- Which unit suites assert on source text by regex was measured for only 8 files (chat-utils, config-schema, scripts-manager, profile-config-builder, engine, quest-defs, HermesRuntime, utils), so the refactor cost for the other files is unknown.
- Other scopes (src/lib/api, db, seed, sessions, stats, sync, ui, analytics, dashboard, retention, schema; src/hooks; scripts/) were examined only where a finding crosses into them, e.g. hooks/useModels.ts, db/parse-json.ts, the ch-*.sh scripts.
